

const int TK_EOF = 0;
const int TK_INT_LIT = 1;
const int TK_FLOAT_LIT = 2;
const int TK_STRING_LIT = 3;
const int TK_CHAR_LIT = 4;
const int TK_BOOL_LIT = 5;
const int TK_NULL_LIT = 6;
const int TK_IDENT = 7;
const int TK_ANNOTATION = 8;

const int TK_IF = 10;
const int TK_ELSE = 11;
const int TK_WHILE = 12;
const int TK_FOR = 13;
const int TK_FOREACH = 14;
const int TK_RETURN = 15;
const int TK_MATCH = 16;
const int TK_VOID = 17;
const int TK_INT = 18;
const int TK_INT32 = 19;
const int TK_INT64 = 20;
const int TK_UINT = 21;
const int TK_FLOAT = 22;
const int TK_FLOAT64 = 23;
const int TK_DOUBLE = 24;
const int TK_BOOL = 25;
const int TK_CHAR = 26;
const int TK_STRING = 27;
const int TK_VAR = 28;
const int TK_STRUCT = 29;
const int TK_CLASS = 30;
const int TK_ENUM = 31;
const int TK_DEF = 32;
const int TK_FOUND = 33;
const int TK_COLLECT = 34;
const int TK_MOD = 35;
const int TK_IMPORT = 36;
const int TK_FROM = 37;
const int TK_AS = 38;
const int TK_NEW = 39;
const int TK_CONST = 40;
const int TK_TRUE = 41;
const int TK_FALSE = 42;
const int TK_NULL = 43;
const int TK_PUBLIC = 44;
const int TK_PRIVATE = 45;
const int TK_STATIC = 46;
const int TK_PROP = 47;
const int TK_CONSTRUCT = 48;
const int TK_UNSET = 49;
const int TK_MUT = 50;
const int TK_REF = 51;
const int TK_TRY = 52;
const int TK_CATCH = 53;
const int TK_IN = 54;
const int TK_TO = 55;
const int TK_SWITCH = 56;
const int TK_CASE = 57;
const int TK_ASSERT = 58;
const int TK_AWAIT = 59;
const int TK_SLEEP = 60;
const int TK_FUN = 61;
const int TK_ELIF = 62;
const int TK_BREAK = 63;
const int TK_CONTINUE = 64;
const int TK_THROW = 65;
const int TK_FINALLY = 66;
const int TK_EXTENDS = 67;
const int TK_LOCAL = 68;
const int TK_ALIAS = 69;
const int TK_FIXED = 70;
const int TK_LIVE = 71;
const int TK_ONCE = 72;
const int TK_SHARED = 73;

const int TK_LPAREN = 100;
const int TK_RPAREN = 101;
const int TK_LBRACE = 102;
const int TK_RBRACE = 103;
const int TK_LBRACKET = 104;
const int TK_RBRACKET = 105;
const int TK_SEMI = 106;
const int TK_COMMA = 107;
const int TK_DOT = 108;
const int TK_COLON = 109;
const int TK_ASSIGN = 110;
const int TK_PLUS = 111;
const int TK_MINUS = 112;
const int TK_STAR = 113;
const int TK_SLASH = 114;
const int TK_PERCENT = 115;
const int TK_AMP = 116;
const int TK_PIPE = 117;
const int TK_CARET = 118;
const int TK_TILDE = 119;
const int TK_LESS = 120;
const int TK_GREATER = 121;
const int TK_BANG = 122;
const int TK_QUESTION = 123;
const int TK_DOLLAR = 124;
const int TK_HASH = 125;
const int TK_UNDERSCORE = 126;

const int TK_PLUS_EQ = 130;
const int TK_MINUS_EQ = 131;
const int TK_STAR_EQ = 132;
const int TK_SLASH_EQ = 133;
const int TK_EQ = 134;
const int TK_NEQ = 135;
const int TK_LEQ = 136;
const int TK_GEQ = 137;
const int TK_AND = 138;
const int TK_OR = 139;
const int TK_SHL = 140;
const int TK_SHR = 141;
const int TK_INC = 142;
const int TK_DEC = 143;
const int TK_RANGE = 144;
const int TK_ARROW = 145;
const int TK_FAT_ARROW = 146;
const int TK_SPREAD = 147;
const int TK_QUESTIONQUESTION = 148;
const int TK_AT = 150;

class Token {
    public prop int kind;
    public prop string text;
    public prop int line;
    public prop int col;
    public prop int64 intVal;
    public prop float64 floatVal;
    public prop string strVal;
    public prop char charVal;

    construct (int kind, string text, int line, int col) {
        this.kind = kind;
        this.text = text;
        this.line = line;
        this.col = col;
        this.intVal = 0;
        this.floatVal = 0.0;
        this.strVal = "";
        this.charVal = ' ';
    }

    public string toString() {
        string k = kindToString(this.kind);
        return k + " '" + this.text + "' @ " + this.line.toString() + ":" + this.col.toString();
    }
}

string kindToString(int kind) {
    if (kind == TK_EOF) { return "EOF"; }
    if (kind == TK_INT_LIT) { return "INT_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_FLOAT_LIT) { return "FLOAT_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_STRING_LIT) { return "STRING_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_CHAR_LIT) { return "CHAR_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_BOOL_LIT) { return "BOOL_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_NULL_LIT) { return "NULL_LIT"; }
    if (kind == TK_IDENT) { return "IDENT"; }
    if (kind == TK_IF) { return "if"; }
    if (kind == TK_ELSE) { return "else"; }
    if (kind == TK_WHILE) { return "while"; }
    if (kind == TK_FOR) { return "for"; }
    if (kind == TK_FOREACH) { return "foreach"; }
    if (kind == TK_RETURN) { return "return"; }
    if (kind == TK_MATCH) { return "match"; }
    if (kind == TK_VOID) { return "void"; }
    if (kind == TK_INT) { return "int"; }
    if (kind == TK_INT32) { return "int32"; }
    if (kind == TK_INT64) { return "int64"; }
    if (kind == TK_UINT) { return "uint"; }
    if (kind == TK_FLOAT) { return "float"; }
    if (kind == TK_FLOAT64) { return "float64"; }
    if (kind == TK_DOUBLE) { return "double"; }
    if (kind == TK_BOOL) { return "bool"; }
    if (kind == TK_CHAR) { return "char"; }
    if (kind == TK_STRING) { return "string"; }
    if (kind == TK_VAR) { return "var"; }
    if (kind == TK_STRUCT) { return "struct"; }
    if (kind == TK_CLASS) { return "class"; }
    if (kind == TK_ENUM) { return "enum"; }
    if (kind == TK_DEF) { return "def"; }
    if (kind == TK_NEW) { return "new"; }
    if (kind == TK_CONST) { return "const"; }
    if (kind == TK_MUT) { return "mut"; }
    if (kind == TK_LPAREN) { return "("; }
    if (kind == TK_RPAREN) { return ")"; }
    if (kind == TK_LBRACE) { return "{"; }
    if (kind == TK_RBRACE) { return "}"; }
    if (kind == TK_LBRACKET) { return "["; }
    if (kind == TK_RBRACKET) { return "]"; }
    if (kind == TK_SEMI) { return ";"; }
    if (kind == TK_COMMA) { return ","; }
    if (kind == TK_DOT) { return "."; }
    if (kind == TK_COLON) { return ":"; }
    if (kind == TK_ASSIGN) { return "="; }
    if (kind == TK_PLUS) { return "+"; }
    if (kind == TK_MINUS) { return "-"; }
    if (kind == TK_STAR) { return "*"; }
    if (kind == TK_SLASH) { return "/"; }
    if (kind == TK_PERCENT) { return "%"; }
    if (kind == TK_EQ) { return "=="; }
    if (kind == TK_NEQ) { return "!="; }
    if (kind == TK_LESS) { return "<"; }
    if (kind == TK_GREATER) { return ">"; }
    if (kind == TK_LEQ) { return "<="; }
    if (kind == TK_GEQ) { return ">="; }
    if (kind == TK_AND) { return "&&"; }
    if (kind == TK_OR) { return "||"; }
    if (kind == TK_BANG) { return "!"; }
    if (kind == TK_QUESTION) { return "?"; }
    if (kind == TK_ARROW) { return "->"; }
    if (kind == TK_FAT_ARROW) { return "=>"; }
    if (kind == TK_RANGE) { return ".."; }
    if (kind == TK_INC) { return "++"; }
    if (kind == TK_DEC) { return "--"; }
    if (kind == TK_PLUS_EQ) { return "+="; }
    if (kind == TK_MINUS_EQ) { return "-="; }
    if (kind == TK_STAR_EQ) { return "*="; }
    if (kind == TK_SLASH_EQ) { return "/="; }
    if (kind == TK_SHL) { return "<<"; }
    if (kind == TK_SHR) { return ">>"; }
    if (kind == TK_AMP) { return "&"; }
    if (kind == TK_PIPE) { return "|"; }
    if (kind == TK_SPREAD) { return "..."; }
    if (kind == TK_QUESTIONQUESTION) { return "??"; }
    if (kind == TK_BREAK) { return "break"; }
    if (kind == TK_CONTINUE) { return "continue"; }
    if (kind == TK_EXTENDS) { return "extends"; }
    return "UNKNOWN(" + kind.toString() + ")";
}







class Lexer {
    private prop string src;
    private prop int pos;
    private prop int line;
    private prop int col;

    construct (string source) {
        this.src = source;
        this.pos = 0;
        this.line = 1;
        this.col = 1;
    }

    private char? peek() {
        if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            return this.src[this.pos];
        }
        return null;
    }

    private char? peekNext() {
        if (this.pos + 1 < this.src.length) {
            return this.src[this.pos + 1];
        }
        return null;
    }

    private char? advance() {
        if (this.pos >= this.src.length) { return null; }
        char c = this.src[this.pos];
        this.pos += 1;
        if (c == '\n') {
            this.line += 1;
            this.col = 1;
        } else {
            this.col += 1;
        }
        return c;
    }

    private void skipWhitespace() {
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char c = this.src[this.pos];
            if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
                this.advance();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    private void skipLineComment() {
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char c = this.src[this.pos];
            if (c == '\n') { break; }
            this.advance();
        }
    }

    private bool skipBlockComment() {
        int depth = 1;
        while (this.pos < this.src.length && depth > 0) {
            char c = this.src[this.pos];
            if (c == '/' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '*') {
                depth += 1;
                this.advance();
                this.advance();
            } else if (c == '*' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '/') {
                depth -= 1;
                this.advance();
                this.advance();
            } else {
                this.advance();
            }
        }
        return depth == 0;
    }

    private Token makeToken(int kind, string text) {
        return new Token(kind, text, this.line, this.col);
    }

    private Token readString() {
        int ln = this.line;
        int c = this.col;
        string s = "";
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char ch = this.src[this.pos];
            this.advance();
            if (ch == '"') { break; }
            if (ch == '\\') {
                if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                    char esc = this.src[this.pos];
                    this.advance();
                    if (esc == 'n') { s += "\n"; }
                    else if (esc == 't') { s += "\t"; }
                    else if (esc == 'r') { s += "\r"; }
                    else if (esc == '\\') { s += "\\"; }
                    else if (esc == '"') { s += "\""; }
                    else if (esc == '$') { s += "$"; }
                    else if (esc == '0') { s += char(0); }
                    else if (esc == 'x') {
                        // Hex escape \xHH: decode 1-2 hex digits into a raw
                        // byte (matches the compiler's lexer). With no hex
                        // digit following, keep the literal `\x`.
                        int hv = 0;
                        int nd = 0;
                        while (nd < 2 && this.pos < this.src.length) {
                            char hc = this.src[this.pos];
                            int dv = -1;
                            if (hc >= '0' && hc <= '9') { dv = hc - '0'; }
                            elif (hc >= 'a' && hc <= 'f') { dv = hc - 'a' + 10; }
                            elif (hc >= 'A' && hc <= 'F') { dv = hc - 'A' + 10; }
                            if (dv < 0) { break; }
                            this.advance();
                            hv = hv * 16 + dv;
                            nd = nd + 1;
                        }
                        if (nd > 0) {
                            char xc = char(hv);
                            s += xc.toString();
                        } else {
                            s += "\\x";
                        }
                    }
                    else { s += esc.toString(); }
                }
            } else {
                s += ch.toString();
            }
        }
        Token tok = new Token(TK_STRING_LIT, s, ln, c);
        tok.strVal = s;
        return tok;
    }

    private Token readChar() {
        int ln = this.line;
        int c = this.col;
        char ch = ' ';
        if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            ch = this.src[this.pos];
            this.advance();
            if (ch == '\\') {
                if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                    char esc = this.src[this.pos];
                    this.advance();
                    if (esc == 'n') { ch = '\n'; }
                    else if (esc == 't') { ch = '\t'; }
                    else if (esc == 'r') { ch = '\r'; }
                    else if (esc == '\\') { ch = '\\'; }
                    else if (esc == '\'') { ch = '\''; }
                    else if (esc == '0') { ch = char(0); }
                    else if (esc == 'x') {
                        // Hex escape \xHH: decode 1-2 hex digits into a raw
                        // byte (matches the compiler's lexer). With no hex
                        // digit following, the char is a literal 'x'.
                        int hv = 0;
                        int nd = 0;
                        while (nd < 2 && this.pos < this.src.length) {
                            char hc = this.src[this.pos];
                            int dv = -1;
                            if (hc >= '0' && hc <= '9') { dv = hc - '0'; }
                            elif (hc >= 'a' && hc <= 'f') { dv = hc - 'a' + 10; }
                            elif (hc >= 'A' && hc <= 'F') { dv = hc - 'A' + 10; }
                            if (dv < 0) { break; }
                            this.advance();
                            hv = hv * 16 + dv;
                            nd = nd + 1;
                        }
                        if (nd > 0) { ch = char(hv); } else { ch = esc; }
                    }
                    else { ch = esc; }
                }
            }
        }
        if (this.pos < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos] == '\'') {
            this.advance();
        }
        Token tok = new Token(TK_CHAR_LIT, ch.toString(), ln, c);
        tok.charVal = ch;
        return tok;
    }

    private Token readNumber(char first) {
        int ln = this.line;
        int c = this.col;
        string s = first.toString();
        bool isFloat = false;
        if (first == '0' && this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char next = this.src[this.pos];
            if (next == 'x' || next == 'X') {
                s += next.toString();
                this.advance();
                while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                    char ch = this.src[this.pos];
                    if (isHexDigit(ch)) {
                        s += ch.toString();
                        this.advance();
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                return this.makeIntToken(s, 16, ln, c);
            }
            if (next == 'b' || next == 'B') {
                s += next.toString();
                this.advance();
                while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                    char ch = this.src[this.pos];
                    if (ch == '0' || ch == '1') {
                        s += ch.toString();
                        this.advance();
                    } else {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                return this.makeIntToken(s, 2, ln, c);
            }
        }
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char ch = this.src[this.pos];
            if (ch.isDigit() || ch == '_') {
                s += ch.toString();
                this.advance();
            } else if (ch == '.' && !isFloat) {
                if (this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '.') {
                    break;
                }
                isFloat = true;
                s += ch.toString();
                this.advance();
            } else if (ch == 'e' || ch == 'E') {
                isFloat = true;
                s += ch.toString();
                this.advance();
                if (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                    char sign = this.src[this.pos];
                    if (sign == '+' || sign == '-') {
                        s += sign.toString();
                        this.advance();
                    }
                }
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        string clean = removeUnderscores(s);
        if (isFloat) {
            Token tok = new Token(TK_FLOAT_LIT, clean, ln, c);
            tok.floatVal = clean.toFloat64();
            return tok;
        }
        Token tok = new Token(TK_INT_LIT, clean, ln, c);
        tok.intVal = clean.toInt64();
        return tok;
    }

    private Token makeIntToken(string s, int base, int ln, int c) {
        string clean = removeUnderscores(s);
        Token tok = new Token(TK_INT_LIT, clean, ln, c);
        if (base == 16) {
            string hex = clean.substr(2);
            // Parse the digit string manually: toLongFromBase/toInt64FromBase
            // are not dispatched by every backend, so compute the value with
            // arithmetic that all stages support (matches the compiler's lexer).
            int64 val = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < hex.length; i++) {
                char ch = hex[i];
                int d = -1;
                if (ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') { d = ch - '0'; }
                else if (ch >= 'a' && ch <= 'f') { d = ch - 'a' + 10; }
                else if (ch >= 'A' && ch <= 'F') { d = ch - 'A' + 10; }
                if (d >= 0) { val = val * 16 + d; }
            }
            tok.intVal = val;
        } else if (base == 2) {
            string bin = clean.substr(2);
            int64 val = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) {
                char ch = bin[i];
                if (ch == '1') { val = val * 2 + 1; }
                else if (ch == '0') { val = val * 2; }
            }
            tok.intVal = val;
        } else {
            tok.intVal = clean.toInt64();
        }
        return tok;
    }

    private Token readIdentifierOrKeyword(char first) {
        int ln = this.line;
        int c = this.col;
        string s = first.toString();
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char ch = this.src[this.pos];
            if (ch.isAlphaNum() || ch == '_') {
                s += ch.toString();
                this.advance();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        int kind = keywordKind(s);
        Token tok = new Token(kind, s, ln, c);
        if (kind == TK_IDENT) {
            tok.strVal = s;
        }
        return tok;
    }

    public Token[] tokenize() {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TL0_lex_start.txt", [49]);
        Token[] tokens = [];
        while (true) {
            this.skipWhitespace();
            if (this.pos >= this.src.length) {
                tokens.add(new Token(TK_EOF, "", this.line, this.col));
                break;
            }
            Token? tok = this.nextToken();
            if (tok != null) {
                tokens.add(tok);
            }
        }
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TL1_lex_end.txt", [49]);
        return tokens;
    }

    private Token? nextToken() {
        char c = this.src[this.pos];
        int ln = this.line;
        int cl = this.col;

        if (c == '/' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length) {
            char next = this.src[this.pos + 1];
            if (next == '/') {
                this.skipLineComment();
                return null;
            }
            if (next == '*') {
                this.advance();
                this.advance();
                this.skipBlockComment();
                return null;
            }
        }

        if (c == '"') {
            this.advance();
            return this.readString();
        }
        if (c == '\'') {
            this.advance();
            return this.readChar();
        }
        if (c.isDigit()) {
            this.advance();
            return this.readNumber(c);
        }
        if (c.isAlpha() || c == '_') {
            this.advance();
            return this.readIdentifierOrKeyword(c);
        }

        this.advance();

        if (c == '(') { return new Token(TK_LPAREN, "(", ln, cl); }
        if (c == ')') { return new Token(TK_RPAREN, ")", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '{') { return new Token(TK_LBRACE, "{", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '}') { return new Token(TK_RBRACE, "}", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '[') { return new Token(TK_LBRACKET, "[", ln, cl); }
        if (c == ']') { return new Token(TK_RBRACKET, "]", ln, cl); }
        if (c == ';') { return new Token(TK_SEMI, ";", ln, cl); }
        if (c == ',') { return new Token(TK_COMMA, ",", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '~') { return new Token(TK_TILDE, "~", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '^') { return new Token(TK_CARET, "^", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '?') {
            if (this.peek() == '?') {
                this.advance();
                return new Token(TK_QUESTIONQUESTION, "??", ln, cl);
            }
            return new Token(TK_QUESTION, "?", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '$') { return new Token(TK_DOLLAR, "$", ln, cl); }

        if (c == '.') {
            if (this.pos < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos] == '.' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '.') {
                this.advance();
                this.advance();
                return new Token(TK_SPREAD, "...", ln, cl);
            }
            if (this.pos < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos] == '.') {
                this.advance();
                return new Token(TK_RANGE, "..", ln, cl);
            }
            return new Token(TK_DOT, ".", ln, cl);
        }

        if (c == ':') { return new Token(TK_COLON, ":", ln, cl); }

        if (c == '+') {
            if (this.peek() == '+') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_INC, "++", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_PLUS_EQ, "+=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_PLUS, "+", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '-') {
            if (this.peek() == '-') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_DEC, "--", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_MINUS_EQ, "-=", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '>') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_ARROW, "->", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_MINUS, "-", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '*') {
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_STAR_EQ, "*=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_STAR, "*", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '/') {
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_SLASH_EQ, "/=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_SLASH, "/", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '%') { return new Token(TK_PERCENT, "%", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '&') {
            if (this.peek() == '&') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_AND, "&&", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_AMP, "&", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '|') {
            if (this.peek() == '|') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_OR, "||", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_PIPE, "|", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '<') {
            if (this.peek() == '<') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_SHL, "<<", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_LEQ, "<=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_LESS, "<", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '>') {
            if (this.peek() == '>') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_SHR, ">>", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_GEQ, ">=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_GREATER, ">", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '=') {
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_EQ, "==", ln, cl); }
            if (this.peek() == '>') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_FAT_ARROW, "=>", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_ASSIGN, "=", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '!') {
            if (this.peek() == '=') { this.advance(); return new Token(TK_NEQ, "!=", ln, cl); }
            return new Token(TK_BANG, "!", ln, cl);
        }
        if (c == '#') { return new Token(TK_HASH, "#", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '@') { return new Token(TK_AT, "@", ln, cl); }
        if (c == '_') { return new Token(TK_UNDERSCORE, "_", ln, cl); }

        printline("Warning: unexpected character '" + c.toString() + "' at " + ln.toString() + ":" + cl.toString());
        return null;
    }
}

int keywordKind(string s) {
    if (s == "if") { return TK_IF; }
    if (s == "else") { return TK_ELSE; }
    if (s == "while") { return TK_WHILE; }
    if (s == "for") { return TK_FOR; }
    if (s == "foreach") { return TK_FOREACH; }
    if (s == "return") { return TK_RETURN; }
    if (s == "match") { return TK_MATCH; }
    if (s == "void") { return TK_VOID; }
    if (s == "int") { return TK_INT; }
    if (s == "int32") { return TK_INT32; }
    if (s == "int64") { return TK_INT64; }
    if (s == "uint") { return TK_UINT; }
    if (s == "float") { return TK_FLOAT; }
    if (s == "float64") { return TK_FLOAT64; }
    if (s == "double") { return TK_DOUBLE; }
    if (s == "bool") { return TK_BOOL; }
    if (s == "char") { return TK_CHAR; }
    if (s == "string") { return TK_STRING; }
    if (s == "var") { return TK_VAR; }
    if (s == "struct") { return TK_STRUCT; }
    if (s == "class") { return TK_CLASS; }
    if (s == "enum") { return TK_ENUM; }
    if (s == "def") { return TK_DEF; }
    if (s == "found") { return TK_FOUND; }
    if (s == "collect") { return TK_COLLECT; }
    if (s == "mod") { return TK_MOD; }
    if (s == "import") { return TK_IMPORT; }
    if (s == "from") { return TK_FROM; }
    if (s == "as") { return TK_AS; }
    if (s == "new") { return TK_NEW; }
    if (s == "const") { return TK_CONST; }
    if (s == "true") { return TK_BOOL_LIT; }
    if (s == "false") { return TK_BOOL_LIT; }
    if (s == "null") { return TK_NULL_LIT; }
    if (s == "public") { return TK_PUBLIC; }
    if (s == "private") { return TK_PRIVATE; }
    if (s == "static") { return TK_STATIC; }
    if (s == "prop") { return TK_PROP; }
    if (s == "construct") { return TK_CONSTRUCT; }
    if (s == "unset") { return TK_UNSET; }
    if (s == "mut") { return TK_MUT; }
    if (s == "ref") { return TK_REF; }
    if (s == "try") { return TK_TRY; }
    if (s == "catch") { return TK_CATCH; }
    if (s == "throw") { return TK_THROW; }
    if (s == "finally") { return TK_FINALLY; }
    if (s == "extends") { return TK_EXTENDS; }
    if (s == "in") { return TK_IN; }
    if (s == "to") { return TK_TO; }
    if (s == "switch") { return TK_SWITCH; }
    if (s == "case") { return TK_CASE; }
    if (s == "assert") { return TK_ASSERT; }
    if (s == "await") { return TK_AWAIT; }
    if (s == "fun") { return TK_FUN; }
    if (s == "elif") { return TK_ELIF; }
    if (s == "break") { return TK_BREAK; }
    if (s == "continue") { return TK_CONTINUE; }
    if (s == "local") { return TK_LOCAL; }
    if (s == "alias") { return TK_ALIAS; }
    if (s == "fixed") { return TK_FIXED; }
    if (s == "live") { return TK_LIVE; }
    if (s == "once") { return TK_ONCE; }
    if (s == "shared") { return TK_SHARED; }
    return TK_IDENT;
}

bool isHexDigit(char c) {
    return c.isDigit() || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F');
}

string removeUnderscores(string s) {
    string result = "";
    for (int i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
        if (s[i] != '_') {
            result += s[i].toString();
        }
    }
    return result;
}









const int ND_INT_LIT = 0;
const int ND_FLOAT_LIT = 1;
const int ND_STRING_LIT = 2;
const int ND_CHAR_LIT = 3;
const int ND_BOOL_LIT = 4;
const int ND_NULL_LIT = 5;

const int ND_BINARY = 7;
const int ND_UNARY = 8;
const int ND_CALL = 9;

const int ND_NEW = 21;
const int ND_INTERP = 22;
const int ND_PAREN = 23;
const int ND_DICT_LIT = 24;

const int ND_STRUCT_DEF = 101;
const int ND_CLASS_DEF = 102;
const int ND_ENUM_DEF = 103;

const int ND_TYPE_ALIAS = 107;
const int ND_FOUND = 108;

const int OP_ADD = 0;

const int PAT_WILDCARD = 0;
const int PAT_INT = 1;
const int PAT_FLOAT = 2;
const int PAT_STRING = 3;
const int PAT_CHAR = 4;
const int PAT_BOOL = 5;
const int PAT_NULL = 6;
const int PAT_IDENT = 7;
const int PAT_TYPED = 8;
const int PAT_TUPLE = 9;
const int PAT_MEMBER = 10;

class Node {
    public prop int kind;
    public prop int64 intVal;
    public prop float64 floatVal;
    public prop string strVal;
    public prop char charVal;
    public prop bool boolVal;
    public prop int op;
    public prop string name;
    public prop string typeHint;
    public prop bool isMut;
    public prop bool isPublic;
    public prop bool isStatic;
    public prop bool isFixed;
    public prop bool isLive;
    public prop bool isOnce;
    public prop bool isAlias;
    public prop bool isShared;
    public prop bool hasFinally;
    public prop string parentClass;
    public prop Node[] children;
    public prop dict<string, Node> props;
    public prop int line;
    public prop int col;

    construct (int kind, int line, int col) {
        this.kind = kind;
        this.intVal = 0;
        this.floatVal = 0.0;
        this.strVal = "";
        this.charVal = ' ';
        this.boolVal = false;
        this.op = 0;
        this.name = "";
        this.typeHint = "";
        this.isMut = false;
        this.isPublic = true;
        this.isStatic = false;
        this.isFixed = false;
        this.isLive = false;
        this.isOnce = false;
        this.isAlias = false;
        this.isShared = false;
        this.hasFinally = false;
        this.parentClass = "";
        this.children = [];
        this.props = {};
        this.line = line;
        this.col = col;
    }

    public void addChild(Node n) {
        this.children.add(n);
    }

    public Node child(int i) {
        if (i >= 0 && i < this.children.length) {
            return this.children[i];
        }
        return new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, 0, 0);
    }

    public int childCount() {
        return this.children.length;
    }

    public void setProp(string key, Node val) {
        this.props[key] = val;
    }

    public Node? getProp(string key) {
        if (this.props.contains(key)) {
            return this.props[key];
        }
        return null;
    }

    public string toString() {
        return "Node(" + kindName(this.kind) + " '" + this.name + "')";
    }
}

string kindName(int k) {
    if (k == ND_INT_LIT) { return "IntLit"; }
    if (k == ND_FLOAT_LIT) { return "FloatLit"; }
    if (k == ND_STRING_LIT) { return "StringLit"; }
    if (k == ND_CHAR_LIT) { return "CharLit"; }
    if (k == ND_BOOL_LIT) { return "BoolLit"; }
    if (k == ND_NULL_LIT) { return "NullLit"; }
    if (k == ND_IDENT) { return "Ident"; }
    if (k == ND_BINARY) { return "Binary"; }
    if (k == ND_UNARY) { return "Unary"; }
    if (k == ND_CALL) { return "Call"; }
    if (k == ND_METHOD_CALL) { return "MethodCall"; }
    if (k == ND_INDEX) { return "Index"; }
    if (k == ND_SLICE) { return "Slice"; }
    if (k == ND_MEMBER) { return "Member"; }
    if (k == ND_MATCH) { return "Match"; }
    if (k == ND_IF_EXPR) { return "IfExpr"; }
    if (k == ND_BLOCK) { return "Block"; }
    if (k == ND_STRUCT_LIT) { return "StructLit"; }
    if (k == ND_TUPLE_LIT) { return "TupleLit"; }
    if (k == ND_ARRAY_LIT) { return "ArrayLit"; }
    if (k == ND_TERNARY) { return "Ternary"; }
    if (k == ND_NEW) { return "New"; }
    if (k == ND_INTERP) { return "Interp"; }
    if (k == ND_DICT_LIT) { return "DictLit"; }
    if (k == ND_VAR_DECL) { return "VarDecl"; }
    if (k == ND_RETURN) { return "Return"; }
    if (k == ND_IF_STMT) { return "IfStmt"; }
    if (k == ND_WHILE) { return "While"; }
    if (k == ND_FOR_C) { return "ForC"; }
    if (k == ND_FOR_RANGE) { return "ForRange"; }
    if (k == ND_FOREACH) { return "Foreach"; }
    if (k == ND_MATCH_STMT) { return "MatchStmt"; }
    if (k == ND_TRY_CATCH) { return "TryCatch"; }
    if (k == ND_THROW) { return "Throw"; }
    if (k == ND_EXPR_STMT) { return "ExprStmt"; }
    if (k == ND_ALIAS_DECL) { return "AliasDecl"; }
    if (k == ND_LIVE_DECL) { return "LiveDecl"; }
    if (k == ND_ONCE_DECL) { return "OnceDecl"; }
    if (k == ND_ADDRESS_OF) { return "AddressOf"; }
    if (k == ND_FUNC) { return "Func"; }
    if (k == ND_STRUCT_DEF) { return "StructDef"; }
    if (k == ND_CLASS_DEF) { return "ClassDef"; }
    if (k == ND_ENUM_DEF) { return "EnumDef"; }
    if (k == ND_CONST) { return "Const"; }
    if (k == ND_IMPORT) { return "Import"; }
    if (k == ND_MODULE) { return "Module"; }
    return "Node" + k.toString();
}








class Parser {
    private prop Token[] tokens;
    private prop int pos;
    // Inside a `[...]` index, a top-level `..` starts a slice rather than
    // a binary range expression.
    private bool rangeIsSliceBoundary;

    construct (Token[] toks) {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TP_c.txt", [49]);
        this.tokens = toks;
        this.pos = 0;
        this.rangeIsSliceBoundary = false;
    }

    private Token peek() {
        if (this.pos < this.tokens.length) {
            return this.tokens[this.pos];
        }
        return new Token(TK_EOF, "", 0, 0);
    }

    private int peekKind() {
        return this.peek().kind;
    }

    private Token advance() {
        Token t = this.peek();
        this.pos += 1;
        return t;
    }

    private bool check(int kind) {
        return this.peekKind() == kind;
    }

    private bool checkType() {
        int k = this.peekKind();
        return k == TK_INT || k == TK_INT32 || k == TK_INT64 || k == TK_UINT
            || k == TK_FLOAT || k == TK_FLOAT64 || k == TK_DOUBLE
            || k == TK_BOOL || k == TK_CHAR || k == TK_STRING
            || k == TK_VOID;
    }

    private Token expect(int kind) {
        if (this.peekKind() == kind) {
            return this.advance();
        }
        Token t = this.peek();
        printline("Parse error at " + t.line.toString() + ":" + t.col.toString() + ": expected " + kindToString(kind) + " but got " + kindToString(t.kind) + " '" + t.text + "'");
        return t;
    }

    private string expectIdent() {
        Token t = this.peek();
        if (t.kind == TK_IDENT) {
            this.advance();
            return t.text;
        }
        printline("Parse error: expected identifier at " + t.line.toString() + ":" + t.col.toString());
        return "_err";
    }

    private int line() { return this.peek().line; }
    private int col() { return this.peek().col; }

public Node[] parseProgram() {
    file.writeBytes("build/diag/TP_p.txt", [49]);
    Node[] items = [];
    int safety = 0;
    // Bound by the token count rather than a fixed 100: large preludes (the
    // embedded exception hierarchy has 100+ classes) must all be parsed. The
    // advance-on-stuck guard below guarantees forward progress, so the token
    // count is a safe upper bound.
    int safetyLimit = this.tokens.length + 16;
    while (!this.check(TK_EOF) && safety < safetyLimit) {
        safety += 1;
        int oldPos = this.pos;
        Node? item = this.parseItem();
        if (item != null) {
            items.add(item);
        }
        if (this.pos == oldPos) {
            this.advance();
        }
    }
    return items;
}

private Node? parseItem() {
    file.writeBytes("build/diag/TP_i.txt", [49]);
    if (this.check(TK_HASH)) {
            return this.parseAnnotation();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_PUBLIC) || this.check(TK_PRIVATE) || this.check(TK_STATIC)) {
            // Skip top-level access/static modifiers (e.g. `public const`,
            // `public void show(...)` in std module sources) before dispatching
            // to the declaration parser below.
            this.advance();
            return this.parseItem();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_MOD)) { return this.parseModule(); }
        if (this.check(TK_IMPORT)) { return this.parseImport(); }
        if (this.check(TK_CONST)) { return this.parseConst(); }
        if (this.check(TK_DEF)) { return this.parseDef(); }
        if (this.check(TK_FUN)) { return this.parseFun(); }
        if (this.check(TK_STRUCT)) { return this.parseStruct(); }
        if (this.check(TK_CLASS)) { return this.parseClass(); }
        if (this.check(TK_COLLECT)) { return this.parseCollect(); }
        if (this.check(TK_FOUND)) { return this.parseFound(); }
        if (this.checkType()) {
            return this.parseFunctionOrVarDecl();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            // Look ahead to see if this is a named type decl or an expression statement
            int saved = this.pos;
            string first = this.expectIdent();
            if (this.check(TK_IDENT) || this.check(TK_MUT)) {
                this.pos = saved;
                Node? result = this.parseNamedTypeDecl();
                return result ?? null;
            }
            this.pos = saved;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_VAR)) {
            return this.parseVarDeclItem();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LOCAL) || this.check(TK_ALIAS) || this.check(TK_FIXED) || this.check(TK_LIVE) || this.check(TK_ONCE) || this.check(TK_SHARED)) {
            return this.parseStmt();
        }
        // Fall back to expression statement (for top-level printline, etc.)
        Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_EXPR_STMT, this.line(), this.col());
        n.addChild(expr);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseAnnotation() {
        int ln = this.line();
        int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string content = "";
        while (!this.check(TK_SEMI) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            content += this.advance().text + " ";
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SEMI)) { this.advance(); }
        Node n = new Node(ND_ANNOTATION, ln, c);
        n.strVal = content.trim();
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseModule() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT) && this.peek().text == "export") {
            this.advance();
            string name = this.expectIdent();
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            Node n = new Node(ND_MODULE, ln, c);
            n.name = name;
            n.strVal = "export";
            return n;
        }
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node[] items = [];
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            Node? item = this.parseItem();
            if (item != null) { items.add(item); }
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_MODULE, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.children = items;
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseImport() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string[] names = [];
        bool all = false;
        if (this.check(TK_STAR)) {
            this.advance();
            all = true;
        } elif (this.check(TK_LBRACE)) {
            // DOCS/213 -- import { a, b } from win32;
            this.advance();
            names.add(this.expectIdent());
            while (this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                this.advance();
                names.add(this.expectIdent());
            }
            this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        } else {
            names.add(this.expectIdent());
            while (this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                this.advance();
                names.add(this.expectIdent());
            }
        }
        string source = "";
        if (this.check(TK_FROM) || this.peek().text == "from") {
            this.advance();
            source = this.expectIdent();
            while (this.check(TK_DOT)) {
                this.advance();
                source += "." + this.expectIdent();
            }
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_IMPORT, ln, c);
        n.strVal = source;
        if (all) {
            n.name = "*";
        } else {
            string result = names[0];
            for (int i = 1; i < names.length; i++) {
                result += "," + names[i];
            }
            n.name = result;
        }
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseConst() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        Node n = new Node(ND_CONST, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = typ;
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseFun() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node n = new Node(ND_FUNC, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = "void";
        n.addChild(this.parseParamList());
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseDef() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            if (this.check(TK_ENUM)) {
                this.advance();
                this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
                string[] variants = [];
                while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                    variants.add(this.expectIdent());
                    if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
                }
                this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
                this.expect(TK_SEMI);
                Node n = new Node(ND_ENUM_DEF, ln, c);
                n.name = name;
                string joined = "";
                string prefix = "";
                for (int i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
                    joined += prefix + variants[i];
                    prefix = "|";
                }
                n.strVal = joined;
                return n;
            }
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_DEF_UNIT, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseStruct() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_STRUCT_DEF, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
            string fieldName = this.expectIdent();
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            Node field = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
            field.name = fieldName;
            field.typeHint = typ;
            n.addChild(field);
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseClass() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        string parent = "";
        if (this.check(TK_COLON) || this.check(TK_EXTENDS)) {
            this.advance();
            parent = this.expectIdent();
        }
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_CLASS_DEF, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.parentClass = parent;
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            if (this.check(TK_PUBLIC) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_COLON) {
                this.advance(); this.advance(); continue;
            }
            if (this.check(TK_PRIVATE) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_COLON) {
                this.advance(); this.advance(); continue;
            }
            bool isPub = true;
            bool isStat = false;
            if (this.check(TK_PUBLIC)) { this.advance(); isPub = true; }
            elif (this.check(TK_PRIVATE)) { this.advance(); isPub = false; }
            if (this.check(TK_STATIC)) { this.advance(); isStat = true; }
            if (this.check(TK_CONSTRUCT)) {
                Node ctor = this.parseConstructor(name);
                ctor.isPublic = isPub;
                ctor.isStatic = isStat;
                n.addChild(ctor);
                continue;
            }
            if (this.check(TK_PROP)) {
                this.advance();
                string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
                string fieldName = this.expectIdent();
                this.expect(TK_SEMI);
                Node field = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
                field.name = fieldName;
                field.typeHint = typ;
                field.isPublic = isPub;
                n.addChild(field);
                continue;
            }
            if (this.check(TK_STAR)) {
                this.advance();
                string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
                string fieldName = this.expectIdent();
                this.expect(TK_SEMI);
                Node field = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
                field.name = fieldName;
                field.typeHint = typ;
                field.isPublic = false;
                n.addChild(field);
                continue;
            }
            if (this.checkType() || this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
                string retType = this.parseTypeStr();
                string mname = this.expectIdent();
                if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) {
                    Node fn = this.parseFunctionDef(isPub, isStat, retType, mname);
                    if (!isStat) {
                        this.prependThisParam(fn, name);
                    }
                    n.addChild(fn);
                } else {
                    this.expect(TK_SEMI);
                    Node field = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
                    field.name = mname;
                    field.typeHint = retType;
                    field.isPublic = isPub;
                    n.addChild(field);
                }
                continue;
            }
            break;
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseConstructor(string className) {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node n = new Node(ND_FUNC, ln, c);
        n.name = "construct";
        n.parentType = className;
        n.addChild(this.parseParamList());
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        this.prependThisParam(n, className);
        return n;
    }

    private void prependThisParam(Node fn, string className) {
        Node params = fn.child(0);
        Node thisParam = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, 0, 0);
        thisParam.name = "this";
        thisParam.typeHint = className;
        Node[] newChildren = [thisParam];
        for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
            newChildren.add(params.child(i));
        }
        params.children = newChildren;
    }

    private Node parseCollect() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_ASSIGN);
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        string[] types = [];
        while (!this.check(TK_RPAREN) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            types.add(this.parseTypeStr());
            if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
        }
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_COLLECT, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        string joined = "";
        string prefix = "";
        for (int i = 0; i < types.length; i++) {
            joined += prefix + types[i];
            prefix = ",";
        }
        n.strVal = joined;
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseFound() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_FOUND, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            string retType = this.parseTypeStr();
            string mname = this.expectIdent();
            this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
            Node params = this.parseParamList();
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            Node sig = new Node(ND_FUNC, ln, c);
            sig.name = mname;
            sig.typeHint = retType;
            sig.addChild(params);
            n.addChild(sig);
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return n;
    }

private Node parseFunctionOrVarDecl() {
    int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
    bool isPub = false;
    bool isStat = false;
    if (this.check(TK_PUBLIC)) { this.advance(); isPub = true; }
    if (this.check(TK_STATIC)) { this.advance(); isStat = true; }
    string retType = this.parseTypeStr();
    string name = this.expectIdent();
    if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) {
        Node fn = this.parseFunctionDef(isPub, isStat, retType, name);
        return fn;
        }
        Node init = new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            init = this.parseExpr(0);
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = retType;
        n.isPublic = isPub;
        n.isStatic = isStat;
        n.addChild(init);
        return n;
    }

private Node parseFunctionDef(bool isPub, bool isStat, string retType, string name) {
    int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
    this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node params = this.parseParamList();
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        Node n = new Node(ND_FUNC, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = retType;
        n.isPublic = isPub;
        n.isStatic = isStat;
        n.addChild(params);
        if (this.check(TK_SHL)) {
            this.advance();
            Node bodyExpr = this.parseExpr(0);
            Node body = new Node(ND_BLOCK, ln, c);
            Node ret = new Node(ND_RETURN, ln, c);
            ret.addChild(bodyExpr);
            body.addChild(ret);
            n.addChild(body);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        } else if (this.check(TK_LBRACE)) {
            n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        }
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseParamList() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        Node params = new Node(ND_BLOCK, ln, c);
        while (!this.check(TK_RPAREN) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
            string pname = this.expectIdent();
            Node param = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
            param.name = pname;
            param.typeHint = typ;
            if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
                this.advance();
                param.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
            }
            params.addChild(param);
            if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
        }
        return params;
    }

    private Node parseNamedTypeDecl() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        string typeName = this.expectIdent();
        bool isMut = false;
        if (this.check(TK_MUT)) { this.advance(); isMut = true; }
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        // A named type followed by '(' is a function whose return type is a
        // class/type name (e.g. `Spinner dots() { ... }`), not a variable
        // declaration.
        if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) {
            return this.parseFunctionDef(false, false, typeName, name);
        }
        Node init = new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            init = this.parseExpr(0);
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = typeName;
        n.isMut = isMut;
        n.addChild(init);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseVarDeclItem() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        Node init = new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            init = this.parseExpr(0);
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.addChild(init);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseBlock() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node block = new Node(ND_BLOCK, ln, c);
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            int oldPos = this.pos;
            Node? stmt = this.parseStmt();
            if (stmt != null) { block.addChild(stmt); }
            if (this.pos == oldPos) {
                this.advance();
            }
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return block;
    }

    private Node? parseStmt() {
        if (this.check(TK_FOR)) { return this.parseFor(); }
        if (this.check(TK_FOREACH)) { return this.parseForeach(); }
        if (this.check(TK_WHILE)) { return this.parseWhile(); }
        if (this.check(TK_IF)) { return this.parseIf(); }
        if (this.check(TK_MATCH)) { return this.parseMatchStmt(); }
        if (this.check(TK_TRY)) { return this.parseTryCatch(); }
        if (this.check(TK_THROW)) { return this.parseThrow(); }
        if (this.check(TK_RETURN)) { return this.parseReturn(); }
        if (this.check(TK_SHR)) { return this.parseReturnShortcut(); }
        if (this.check(TK_UNSET)) { return this.parseUnset(); }
        if (this.check(TK_LOCAL)) {
            this.advance();
            return this.parseVarDecl();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_FIXED)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = this.parseVarDecl();
            n.isFixed = true;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LIVE)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = this.parseVarDecl();
            n.isLive = true;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_ONCE)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = this.parseVarDecl();
            n.isOnce = true;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SHARED)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = this.parseVarDecl();
            n.isShared = true;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_ALIAS)) {
            int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
            this.advance();
            string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
            string name = this.expectIdent();
            Node n = new Node(ND_ALIAS_DECL, ln, c);
            n.name = name;
            n.typeHint = typ;
            n.isAlias = true;
            if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
                this.advance();
                n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
            }
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SWITCH)) { return this.parseSwitch(); }
        if (this.check(TK_BREAK)) { this.advance(); this.expect(TK_SEMI); return new Node(ND_BREAK, this.line(), this.col()); }
        if (this.check(TK_CONTINUE)) { this.advance(); this.expect(TK_SEMI); return new Node(ND_CONTINUE, this.line(), this.col()); }
        if (this.check(TK_VAR)) { return this.parseVarDecl(); }
        if (this.checkType()) { return this.parseTypedDecl(); }
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            int saved = this.pos;
            string typeName = this.expectIdent();
            bool isTypeDecl = this.check(TK_IDENT) || this.check(TK_MUT);
            if (!isTypeDecl && this.check(TK_LBRACKET) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_RBRACKET) {
                this.advance(); this.advance();
                isTypeDecl = this.check(TK_IDENT) || this.check(TK_MUT);
            }
            if (!isTypeDecl && this.check(TK_QUESTION)) {
                this.advance();
                isTypeDecl = this.check(TK_IDENT) || this.check(TK_MUT);
            }
            if (!isTypeDecl && this.check(TK_LESS)) {
                int depth = 1;
                this.advance();
                while (depth > 0 && this.pos < this.tokens.length && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                    if (this.check(TK_LESS)) { depth += 1; }
                    elif (this.check(TK_GREATER)) { depth -= 1; }
                    this.advance();
                }
                isTypeDecl = this.check(TK_IDENT) || this.check(TK_MUT);
            }
            this.pos = saved;
            if (isTypeDecl) {
                return this.parseNamedTypeDeclStmt();
            }
        }
        Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_EXPR_STMT, this.line(), this.col());
        n.addChild(expr);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseFor() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node n = new Node(ND_FOR_C, ln, c);
        Node init = new Node(ND_EXPR_STMT, ln, c);
        if (this.check(TK_SEMI)) {
            this.advance();
        } elif (this.checkType() || this.check(TK_VAR)) {
            init = this.parseVarDecl();
        } else {
            Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
            init.addChild(expr);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        }
        Node cond = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node update = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        Node body = this.parseBlock();
        n.addChild(init);
        n.addChild(cond);
        n.addChild(update);
        n.addChild(body);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseForeach() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        string typ = "";
        if (this.checkType()) { typ = this.parseTypeStr(); }
        elif (this.check(TK_VAR)) { this.advance(); typ = "var"; }
        elif (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            int saved = this.pos;
            string first = this.expectIdent();
            bool isTyped = !this.check(TK_IN);
            this.pos = saved;
            if (isTyped) { typ = this.parseTypeStr(); }
        }
        string var_ = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_IN);
        Node collection = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        Node body = this.parseBlock();
        Node n = new Node(ND_FOREACH, ln, c);
        n.name = var_;
        n.typeHint = typ;
        n.addChild(collection);
        n.addChild(body);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseWhile() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node cond = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        Node body = this.parseBlock();
        Node n = new Node(ND_WHILE, ln, c);
        n.addChild(cond);
        n.addChild(body);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseIf() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node cond = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        Node then = this.parseBlock();
        Node n = new Node(ND_IF_STMT, ln, c);
        n.addChild(cond);
        n.addChild(then);
        if (this.check(TK_ELSE)) {
            this.advance();
            if (this.check(TK_IF)) {
                Node? elseIf = this.parseIf();
                if (elseIf != null) { n.addChild(elseIf); }
            } else {
                n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
            }
        }
        if (this.check(TK_ELIF)) {
            Node? elseIf = this.parseIf();
            if (elseIf != null) { n.addChild(elseIf); }
        }
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseMatchStmt() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) { this.advance(); }
        Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
        if (this.check(TK_RPAREN)) { this.advance(); }
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_MATCH_STMT, ln, c);
        n.addChild(expr);
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            Node pattern = this.parsePattern();
            this.expect(TK_FAT_ARROW);
            Node body = this.parseExpr(0);
            if (this.check(TK_SEMI)) { this.advance(); }
            if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
            Node arm = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
            arm.addChild(pattern);
            arm.addChild(body);
            n.addChild(arm);
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseTryCatch() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        Node n = new Node(ND_TRY_CATCH, ln, c);
        n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        if (this.check(TK_CATCH)) {
            this.advance();
            this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
            string catchType = this.parseTypeStr();
            string catchVar = this.expectIdent();
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            n.name = catchVar;
            n.typeHint = catchType;
            n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        } else {
            Node empty = new Node(ND_BLOCK, ln, c);
            n.addChild(empty);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_FINALLY)) {
            this.advance();
            n.hasFinally = true;
            n.addChild(this.parseBlock());
        }
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseThrow() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        Node n = new Node(ND_THROW, ln, c);
        n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseReturn() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        Node n = new Node(ND_RETURN, ln, c);
        if (!this.check(TK_SEMI)) {
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseReturnShortcut() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        Node n = new Node(ND_RETURN, ln, c);
        n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseUnset() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        Node n = new Node(ND_UNSET, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseSwitch() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
        Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
        this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
        this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
        Node n = new Node(ND_SWITCH, ln, c);
        n.addChild(expr);
        while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
            if (this.check(TK_CASE)) {
                this.advance();
                Node caseExpr = this.parseExpr(0);
                Node caseBody = this.parseBlock();
                Node arm = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
                arm.addChild(caseExpr);
                arm.addChild(caseBody);
                n.addChild(arm);
            } else if (this.check(TK_ELSE)) {
                this.advance();
                Node elseBody = this.parseBlock();
                Node arm = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
                arm.addChild(new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c));
                arm.addChild(elseBody);
                n.addChild(arm);
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
        this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseVarDecl() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        this.advance();
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SHL)) {
            this.advance();
            Node rhs = this.parseExpr(0);
            Node move = new Node(ND_BINARY, n.line, n.col);
            move.op = OP_MOVE;
            Node src = new Node(ND_IDENT, n.line, n.col);
            src.name = n.name;
            move.addChild(src);
            move.addChild(rhs);
            n.addChild(move);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            return n;
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseTypedDecl() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
        bool isMut = false;
        if (this.check(TK_MUT)) { this.advance(); isMut = true; }
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = typ;
        n.isMut = isMut;
        bool isFixed = false;
        int fixedSize = 0;
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET) && !(this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_RBRACKET)) {
            this.advance();
            Node size = this.parseExpr(0);
            this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
            isFixed = true;
            if (size.kind == ND_INT_LIT) { fixedSize = size.intVal; }
            n.typeHint = typ + "[]";
        }
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        } elif (isFixed) {
            // Materialize zero elements so the interpreter's normal array
            // representation has the requested length and can be indexed or
            // passed to native out-parameter helpers.
            Node arr = new Node(ND_ARRAY_LIT, ln, c);
            for (int i = 0; i < fixedSize; i = i + 1) {
                Node zero = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
                zero.intVal = 0;
                arr.addChild(zero);
            }
            n.addChild(arr);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SHL)) {
            this.advance();
            Node rhs = this.parseExpr(0);
            Node move = new Node(ND_BINARY, n.line, n.col);
            move.op = OP_MOVE;
            Node src = new Node(ND_IDENT, n.line, n.col);
            src.name = n.name;
            move.addChild(src);
            move.addChild(rhs);
            n.addChild(move);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            return n;
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parseNamedTypeDeclStmt() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        string typeName = this.parseTypeStr();
        bool isMut = false;
        if (this.check(TK_MUT)) { this.advance(); isMut = true; }
        string name = this.expectIdent();
        Node n = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
        n.name = name;
        n.typeHint = typeName;
        n.isMut = isMut;
        bool isFixed = false;
        int fixedSize = 0;
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET) && !(this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_RBRACKET)) {
            this.advance();
            Node size = this.parseExpr(0);
            this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
            isFixed = true;
            if (size.kind == ND_INT_LIT) { fixedSize = size.intVal; }
            n.typeHint = typeName + "[]";
        }
        if (this.check(TK_ASSIGN)) {
            this.advance();
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
        } elif (isFixed) {
            Node arr = new Node(ND_ARRAY_LIT, ln, c);
            for (int i = 0; i < fixedSize; i = i + 1) {
                Node zero = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
                zero.intVal = 0;
                arr.addChild(zero);
            }
            n.addChild(arr);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_SHL)) {
            this.advance();
            Node rhs = this.parseExpr(0);
            Node move = new Node(ND_BINARY, n.line, n.col);
            move.op = OP_MOVE;
            Node src = new Node(ND_IDENT, n.line, n.col);
            src.name = n.name;
            move.addChild(src);
            move.addChild(rhs);
            n.addChild(move);
            this.expect(TK_SEMI);
            return n;
        }
        this.expect(TK_SEMI);
        return n;
    }

    private Node parsePattern() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        if (this.check(TK_UNDERSCORE)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
            n.name = "_";
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_NULL_LIT)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
            n.name = "null";
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_BOOL_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_BOOL_LIT, ln, c);
            n.boolVal = this.advance().text == "true";
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_INT_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
            n.intVal = this.advance().intVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_FLOAT_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_FLOAT_LIT, ln, c);
            n.floatVal = this.advance().floatVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_STRING_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_STRING_LIT, ln, c);
            n.strVal = this.advance().strVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_CHAR_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_CHAR_LIT, ln, c);
            n.charVal = this.advance().charVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LESS)) {
            this.advance();
            string typ = this.parseTypeStr();
            this.expect(TK_GREATER);
            string bindName = this.expectIdent();
            Node n = new Node(ND_IDENT, ln, c);
            n.name = bindName;
            n.typeHint = typ;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
            while (!this.check(TK_RPAREN) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                n.addChild(this.parsePattern());
                if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
            }
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            string name = this.expectIdent();
            if (this.check(TK_DOT)) {
                this.advance();
                string variant = this.expectIdent();
                Node n = new Node(ND_IDENT, ln, c);
                n.name = name + "." + variant;
                return n;
            }
            Node n = new Node(ND_IDENT, ln, c);
            n.name = name;
            return n;
        }
        this.advance();
        return new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
    }

    private Node parseExpr(int minPrec) {
        Node lhs = this.parsePrefix();
        while (this.isInfix() && this.getInfixPrec() >= minPrec) {
            lhs = this.parseInfix(lhs);
        }
        return lhs;
    }

    private bool isInfix() {
        int k = this.peekKind();
        if (k == TK_RANGE && this.rangeIsSliceBoundary) {
            // `..` inside a `[...]` index starts a slice, so it is not an
            // infix operator there.
            return false;
        }
        return k == TK_PLUS || k == TK_MINUS || k == TK_STAR || k == TK_SLASH || k == TK_PERCENT
            || k == TK_EQ || k == TK_NEQ || k == TK_LESS || k == TK_LEQ || k == TK_GREATER || k == TK_GEQ
            || k == TK_AND || k == TK_OR
            || k == TK_SHL || k == TK_SHR
            || k == TK_ASSIGN || k == TK_PLUS_EQ || k == TK_MINUS_EQ || k == TK_STAR_EQ || k == TK_SLASH_EQ
            || k == TK_RANGE
            || k == TK_LPAREN || k == TK_LBRACKET || k == TK_DOT
            || k == TK_AMP || k == TK_PIPE || k == TK_CARET
            || k == TK_INC || k == TK_DEC || k == TK_QUESTION || k == TK_QUESTIONQUESTION
            || k == TK_FAT_ARROW;
    }

    private int getInfixPrec() {
        int k = this.peekKind();
        if (k == TK_ASSIGN || k == TK_PLUS_EQ || k == TK_MINUS_EQ || k == TK_STAR_EQ || k == TK_SLASH_EQ) { return 1; }
        if (k == TK_QUESTION || k == TK_QUESTIONQUESTION) { return 1; }
        if (k == TK_OR) { return 2; }
        if (k == TK_AND) { return 3; }
        if (k == TK_EQ || k == TK_NEQ) { return 4; }
        if (k == TK_LESS || k == TK_LEQ || k == TK_GREATER || k == TK_GEQ) { return 5; }
        if (k == TK_PIPE) { return 6; }
        if (k == TK_AMP) { return 7; }
        if (k == TK_SHL || k == TK_SHR) { return 8; }
        if (k == TK_PLUS || k == TK_MINUS) { return 9; }
        if (k == TK_STAR || k == TK_SLASH || k == TK_PERCENT) { return 10; }
        if (k == TK_RANGE) { return 11; }
        if (k == TK_LPAREN || k == TK_LBRACKET || k == TK_DOT) { return 12; }
        if (k == TK_INC || k == TK_DEC) { return 12; }
        if (k == TK_FAT_ARROW) { return 13; }
        return 0;
    }

    private Node parsePrefix() {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        if (this.check(TK_INT_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
            n.intVal = this.advance().intVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_FLOAT_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_FLOAT_LIT, ln, c);
            n.floatVal = this.advance().floatVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_STRING_LIT)) {
            Token t = this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_STRING_LIT, ln, c);
            n.strVal = t.strVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_CHAR_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_CHAR_LIT, ln, c);
            n.charVal = this.advance().charVal;
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_BOOL_LIT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_BOOL_LIT, ln, c);
            n.boolVal = this.advance().text == "true";
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_NULL_LIT)) {
            this.advance();
            return new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_DOLLAR)) {
            this.advance();
            Token t = this.expect(TK_STRING_LIT);
            return this.parseInterpolation(t.strVal, ln, c);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_MINUS)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_UNARY, ln, c);
            n.op = UOP_NEG;
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(10));
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_BANG)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_UNARY, ln, c);
            n.op = UOP_NOT;
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(10));
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_STAR)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_UNARY, ln, c);
            n.op = UOP_DEREF;
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(10));
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_AMP)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_UNARY, ln, c);
            n.op = UOP_REF;
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(10));
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_AT)) {
            this.advance();
            Node addr = new Node(ND_ADDRESS_OF, ln, c);
            addr.addChild(this.parseExpr(10));
            return addr;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_NEW)) {
            this.advance();
            string typeName = this.expectIdent();
            if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET)) {
                // Primitive array construction, currently including the
                // documented `new byte[count]` form. Keep ND_NEW so the
                // evaluator can share the existing construction path.
                this.advance();
                Node size = this.parseExpr(0);
                this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
                Node n = new Node(ND_NEW, ln, c);
                n.name = typeName;
                n.addChild(size);
                return n;
            }
            this.expect(TK_LPAREN);
            Node[] args = [];
            while (!this.check(TK_RPAREN) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                args.add(this.parseExpr(0));
                if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
            }
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            Node n = new Node(ND_NEW, ln, c);
            n.name = typeName;
            foreach (Node a in args) { n.addChild(a); }
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_MATCH)) {
            this.advance();
            if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) { this.advance(); }
            Node expr = this.parseExpr(0);
            if (this.check(TK_RPAREN)) { this.advance(); }
            this.expect(TK_LBRACE);
            Node n = new Node(ND_MATCH, ln, c);
            n.addChild(expr);
            while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                Node pattern = this.parsePattern();
                this.expect(TK_FAT_ARROW);
                Node body = this.parseExpr(0);
                if (this.check(TK_SEMI)) { this.advance(); }
                if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
                Node arm = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
                arm.addChild(pattern);
                arm.addChild(body);
                n.addChild(arm);
            }
            this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LPAREN)) {
            this.advance();
            Node first = this.parseExpr(0);
            if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                Node n = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
                n.addChild(first);
                while (this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                    this.advance();
                    n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
                }
                this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
                return n;
            }
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            Node n = new Node(ND_PAREN, ln, c);
            n.addChild(first);
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET)) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_ARRAY_LIT, ln, c);
            while (!this.check(TK_RBRACKET) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                if (this.check(TK_SPREAD)) {
                    this.advance();
                    Node spread = this.parseExpr(0);
                    spread.name = "spread";
                    n.addChild(spread);
                } else {
                    n.addChild(this.parseExpr(0));
                }
                if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
            }
            this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
            return n;
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACE)) {
            this.advance();
            if (this.check(TK_STRING_LIT) || this.check(TK_INT_LIT)) {
                int look = this.pos + 1;
                if (look < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[look].kind == TK_COLON) {
                    Node n = new Node(ND_DICT_LIT, ln, c);
                    while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                        Node key = this.parseExpr(0);
                        this.expect(TK_COLON);
                        Node val = this.parseExpr(0);
                        Node pair = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
                        pair.addChild(key);
                        pair.addChild(val);
                        n.addChild(pair);
                        if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
                    }
                    this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
                    return n;
                }
            }
            if (this.check(TK_RBRACE)) {
                this.advance();
                return new Node(ND_DICT_LIT, ln, c);
            }
            if (this.check(TK_IDENT) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_COLON) {
                Node n = new Node(ND_STRUCT_LIT, ln, c);
