                while (!this.check(TK_RBRACE) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                    string fieldName = this.expectIdent();
                    this.expect(TK_COLON);
                    Node val = this.parseExpr(0);
                    Node field = new Node(ND_VAR_DECL, ln, c);
                    field.name = fieldName;
                    field.addChild(val);
                    n.addChild(field);
                    if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
                }
                this.expect(TK_RBRACE);
                return n;
            }
            this.pos -= 1;
            return this.parseBlock();
        }
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_IDENT, ln, c);
            n.name = this.advance().text;
            return n;
        }
        // Primitive type keywords are callable in expression position:
        // `string(x)`, `int(x)`, `float(x)` etc. are casts, resolved here as
        // ordinary calls so they dispatch to the matching builtin (e.g.
        // `string` -> string_cast). Mirroring the compiler, keep the keyword's
        // source text as the callee name.
        if (this.check(TK_INT) || this.check(TK_INT32) || this.check(TK_INT64) || this.check(TK_UINT)
            || this.check(TK_FLOAT) || this.check(TK_FLOAT64) || this.check(TK_DOUBLE)
            || this.check(TK_BOOL) || this.check(TK_CHAR) || this.check(TK_STRING) || this.check(TK_VOID)) {
            Node n = new Node(ND_IDENT, ln, c);
            n.name = this.advance().text;
            return n;
        }
        this.advance();
        return new Node(ND_NULL_LIT, ln, c);
    }

    private Node parseInfix(Node lhs) {
        int ln = this.line(); int c = this.col();
        int k = this.peekKind();

        if (k == TK_QUESTION) {
            this.advance();
            Node thenExpr = this.parseExpr(1);
            this.expect(TK_COLON);
            Node elseExpr = this.parseExpr(1);
            Node n = new Node(ND_TERNARY, ln, c);
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(thenExpr);
            n.addChild(elseExpr);
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_QUESTIONQUESTION) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = OP_COALESCE;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(1));
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_ASSIGN) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = OP_ASSIGN;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(1));
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_PLUS_EQ) { this.advance(); return this.makeAssignOp(lhs, OP_ADD_ASSIGN, ln, c); }
        if (k == TK_MINUS_EQ) { this.advance(); return this.makeAssignOp(lhs, OP_SUB_ASSIGN, ln, c); }
        if (k == TK_STAR_EQ) { this.advance(); return this.makeAssignOp(lhs, OP_MUL_ASSIGN, ln, c); }
        if (k == TK_SLASH_EQ) { this.advance(); return this.makeAssignOp(lhs, OP_DIV_ASSIGN, ln, c); }
        if (k == TK_INC) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = OP_ADD_ASSIGN;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            Node one = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
            one.intVal = 1;
            n.addChild(one);
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_DEC) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = OP_SUB_ASSIGN;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            Node one = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
            one.intVal = 1;
            n.addChild(one);
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_RANGE) {
            this.advance();
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = OP_RANGE;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(11));
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_LPAREN) {
            this.advance();
            Node[] args = [];
            while (!this.check(TK_RPAREN) && !this.check(TK_EOF)) {
                args.add(this.parseExpr(0));
                if (this.check(TK_COMMA)) { this.advance(); }
            }
            this.expect(TK_RPAREN);
            if (lhs.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                Node n = new Node(ND_CALL, ln, c);
                n.name = lhs.name;
                foreach (Node a in args) { n.addChild(a); }
                return n;
            }
            if (lhs.kind == ND_MEMBER) {
                Node n = new Node(ND_METHOD_CALL, ln, c);
                n.addChild(lhs.child(0));
                n.name = lhs.name;
                foreach (Node a in args) { n.addChild(a); }
                return n;
            }
            Node n = new Node(ND_CALL, ln, c);
            n.name = lhs.name;
            foreach (Node a in args) { n.addChild(a); }
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_LBRACKET) {
            this.advance();
            // Inside the brackets, `..` is a slice boundary rather than a
            // binary range operator, so the index expression stops there.
            bool oldBoundary = this.rangeIsSliceBoundary;
            this.rangeIsSliceBoundary = true;
            Node idx = this.parseExpr(0);
            bool isSlice = this.check(TK_RANGE);
            Node n;
            if (isSlice) {
                this.advance();
                Node end = this.parseExpr(0);
                this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
                n = new Node(ND_SLICE, ln, c);
                n.addChild(lhs);
                n.addChild(idx);
                n.addChild(end);
            } else {
                this.expect(TK_RBRACKET);
                n = new Node(ND_INDEX, ln, c);
                n.addChild(lhs);
                n.addChild(idx);
            }
            this.rangeIsSliceBoundary = oldBoundary;
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_DOT) {
            this.advance();
            string member = this.expectIdent();
            Node n = new Node(ND_MEMBER, ln, c);
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.name = member;
            return n;
        }
        if (k == TK_FAT_ARROW) {
            this.advance();
            Node body = this.parseExpr(0);
            Node n = new Node(ND_TUPLE_LIT, ln, c);
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(body);
            n.name = "arrow";
            return n;
        }

        int op = this.tokenToOp(k);
        if (op >= 0) {
            this.advance();
            int prec = this.opToPrec(op);
            Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
            n.op = op;
            n.addChild(lhs);
            n.addChild(this.parseExpr(prec));
            return n;
        }
        this.advance();
        return lhs;
    }

    private Node makeAssignOp(Node lhs, int op, int ln, int c) {
        Node n = new Node(ND_BINARY, ln, c);
        n.op = op;
        n.addChild(lhs);
        if (this.check(TK_INT_LIT) && this.peek().intVal == 1) {
            if (op == OP_ADD_ASSIGN || op == OP_SUB_ASSIGN) {
                this.advance();
                Node one = new Node(ND_INT_LIT, ln, c);
                one.intVal = 1;
                n.addChild(one);
                return n;
            }
        }
        n.addChild(this.parseExpr(1));
        return n;
    }

    private int tokenToOp(int k) {
        if (k == TK_PLUS) { return OP_ADD; }
        if (k == TK_MINUS) { return OP_SUB; }
        if (k == TK_STAR) { return OP_MUL; }
        if (k == TK_SLASH) { return OP_DIV; }
        if (k == TK_PERCENT) { return OP_MOD; }
        if (k == TK_EQ) { return OP_EQ; }
        if (k == TK_NEQ) { return OP_NEQ; }
        if (k == TK_LESS) { return OP_LT; }
        if (k == TK_LEQ) { return OP_LEQ; }
        if (k == TK_GREATER) { return OP_GT; }
        if (k == TK_GEQ) { return OP_GEQ; }
        if (k == TK_AND) { return OP_AND; }
        if (k == TK_OR) { return OP_OR; }
        if (k == TK_SHL) { return OP_SHL; }
        if (k == TK_SHR) { return OP_SHR; }
        if (k == TK_AMP) { return OP_BIT_AND; }
        if (k == TK_PIPE) { return OP_BIT_OR; }
        if (k == TK_CARET) { return OP_BIT_XOR; }
        return -1;
    }

    private int opToPrec(int op) {
        if (op == OP_AND) { return 3; }
        if (op == OP_OR) { return 2; }
        if (op == OP_EQ || op == OP_NEQ) { return 4; }
        if (op == OP_LT || op == OP_LEQ || op == OP_GT || op == OP_GEQ) { return 5; }
        if (op == OP_BIT_OR) { return 6; }
        if (op == OP_BIT_AND) { return 7; }
        if (op == OP_SHL || op == OP_SHR) { return 8; }
        if (op == OP_ADD || op == OP_SUB) { return 9; }
        if (op == OP_MUL || op == OP_DIV || op == OP_MOD) { return 10; }
        return 9;
    }

    private Node parseInterpolation(string raw, int ln, int c) {
        Node n = new Node(ND_INTERP, ln, c);
        string currentText = "";
        int i = 0;
        while (i < raw.length) {
            if (raw[i] == '{') {
                if (currentText.length > 0) {
                    Node text = new Node(ND_STRING_LIT, ln, c);
                    text.strVal = currentText;
                    n.addChild(text);
                    currentText = "";
                }
                i += 1;
                string exprStr = "";
                int depth = 1;
                while (i < raw.length && depth > 0) {
                    if (raw[i] == '{') { depth += 1; }
                    else if (raw[i] == '}') { depth -= 1; if (depth == 0) { break; } }
                    exprStr += raw[i].toString();
                    i += 1;
                }
                Lexer subLexer = new Lexer(exprStr);
                Token[] subTokens = subLexer.tokenize();
                Parser subParser = new Parser(subTokens);
                Node exprNode = subParser.parseExpr(0);
                n.addChild(exprNode);
                i += 1;
            } else {
                currentText += raw[i].toString();
                i += 1;
            }
        }
        if (currentText.length > 0) {
            Node text = new Node(ND_STRING_LIT, ln, c);
            text.strVal = currentText;
            n.addChild(text);
        }
        return n;
    }

    private string parseTypeStr() {
        if (this.check(TK_INT)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("int"); }
        if (this.check(TK_INT32)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("int32"); }
        if (this.check(TK_INT64)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("int64"); }
        if (this.check(TK_UINT)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("uint"); }
        if (this.check(TK_FLOAT)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("float"); }
        if (this.check(TK_FLOAT64)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("float64"); }
        if (this.check(TK_DOUBLE)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("double"); }
        if (this.check(TK_BOOL)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("bool"); }
        if (this.check(TK_CHAR)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("char"); }
        if (this.check(TK_STRING)) { this.advance(); return this.applyTypeSuffix("string"); }
        if (this.check(TK_VOID)) { this.advance(); return "void"; }
        if (this.check(TK_IDENT)) {
            string name = this.advance().text;
            if (this.check(TK_LESS)) {
                this.advance();
                string inner = this.parseTypeStr();
                while (this.check(TK_PIPE) || this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                    string sep = this.advance().text;
                    inner += sep + this.parseTypeStr();
                }
                this.expect(TK_GREATER);
                return name + "<" + inner + ">" + this.typeSuffix();
            }
            return this.applyTypeSuffix(name);
        }
        if (this.check(TK_LESS)) {
            this.advance();
            string inner = this.parseTypeStr();
            while (this.check(TK_PIPE) || this.check(TK_COMMA)) {
                string sep = this.advance().text;
                inner += sep + this.parseTypeStr();
            }
            this.expect(TK_GREATER);
            return "<" + inner + ">" + this.typeSuffix();
        }
        return "var";
    }

    private string applyTypeSuffix(string base) {
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_RBRACKET) {
            this.advance(); this.advance();
            return base + "[]";
        }
        if (this.check(TK_QUESTION)) { this.advance(); return base + "?"; }
        if (this.check(TK_STAR)) { this.advance(); return base + "*"; }
        return base;
    }

    private string typeSuffix() {
        if (this.check(TK_LBRACKET) && this.pos + 1 < this.tokens.length && this.tokens[this.pos + 1].kind == TK_RBRACKET) {
            this.advance(); this.advance();
            return "[]";
        }
        if (this.check(TK_QUESTION)) { this.advance(); return "?"; }
        return "";
    }
}











class Env {
    public prop dict<string, Value> vars;
    public prop Env parent;
    public prop string[] varNames;
    public prop bool gcMark;
    public prop int64 gcEpoch;
    public prop dict<string, string> aliasOf;

    construct () {
        this.vars = {};
        this.parent = null;
        this.varNames = [];
        this.gcMark = false;
        this.gcEpoch = 0;
        this.aliasOf = {};
    }

    construct (Env parent) {
        this.vars = {};
        this.parent = parent;
        this.varNames = [];
        this.gcMark = false;
        this.gcEpoch = 0;
        this.aliasOf = {};
    }

    public void define(string name, Value val) {
        if (!this.vars.contains(name)) {
            this.varNames.add(name);
        }
        this.vars[name] = val;
        GC.writeBarrierEnv(this, val);
    }

    public void setAlias(string name, string targetName) {
        this.aliasOf[name] = targetName;
        this.define(name, makeNull());
    }

    public Value get(string name) {
        if (this.aliasOf.contains(name)) {
            return this.get(this.aliasOf[name]);
        }
        if (this.vars.contains(name)) {
            return this.vars[name];
        }
        if (this.parent != null) {
            return this.parent.get(name);
        }
        return null;
    }

    public bool set(string name, Value val) {
        if (this.aliasOf.contains(name)) {
            return this.set(this.aliasOf[name], val);
        }
        if (this.vars.contains(name)) {
            this.vars[name] = val;
            GC.writeBarrierEnv(this, val);
            return true;
        }
        if (this.parent != null) {
            return this.parent.set(name, val);
        }
        return false;
    }

    public bool exists(string name) {
        if (this.aliasOf.contains(name)) { return true; }
        if (this.vars.contains(name)) { return true; }
        if (this.parent != null) { return this.parent.exists(name); }
        return false;
    }

    public void remove(string name) {
        if (this.vars.contains(name)) {
            this.vars[name] = makeNull();
        }
    }

    public Env push() {
        Env child = GC.acquireEnv(this);
        child.aliasOf = {};
        return child;
    }
}




import * from win32.kernel32;




// Silver's runtime allocates Value/Env objects through this collector. Native
// backing storage is not individually freeable by Silver's generated code, so
// reclaimed objects are reused. The collector is deliberately conservative:
// roots are marked repeatedly, and allocations appended after a sweep starts
// are kept until the next cycle so they cannot race the active sweep.
//
// The important latency properties are:
//   * marking roots is repeated between small sweep slices;
//   * sweeping is capped by GC_SWEEP_BUDGET per interpreter loop turn;
//   * thresholds adapt to the live registry instead of collecting at a fixed
//     rate forever;
//   * pooled Values are re-registered when reused (otherwise they disappear
//     from future collections and become an allocation leak).
//
// Compiler constraints in this project require state to be accessed through
// `this.*` instance fields, explicit counter assignments, and non-nullable
// Value parameters in the marker.

class GCState {
    public prop Value[] registry;
    public prop int registryCount;
    public prop Value[] pool;
    public prop int poolCount;
    public prop Env[] envRegistry;
    public prop int envRegistryCount;
    public prop Env[] envPool;
    public prop int envPoolCount;
    public prop Value[] roots;
    public prop int rootCount;
    public prop int64 allocs;
    public prop int64 envAllocs;
    public prop int64 epoch;
    public prop int64 lastEpoch;
    public prop bool collecting;
    public prop int sweepIndex;
    public prop int sweepLimit;
    public prop int envSweepIndex;
    public prop int envSweepLimit;
    public prop int64 nextTrigger;
    public prop int64 liveBytes;
    public prop int64 peakBytes;

    construct () {
        this.registry = [];
        this.registryCount = 0;
        this.pool = [];
        this.poolCount = 0;
        this.envRegistry = [];
        this.envRegistryCount = 0;
        this.envPool = [];
        this.envPoolCount = 0;
        this.roots = [];
        this.rootCount = 0;
        this.allocs = 0;
        this.envAllocs = 0;
        this.epoch = 0;
        this.lastEpoch = -1;
        this.collecting = false;
        this.sweepIndex = 0;
        this.sweepLimit = 0;
        this.envSweepIndex = 0;
        this.envSweepLimit = 0;
        this.nextTrigger = GC_MIN_TRIGGER;
        this.liveBytes = 0;
        this.peakBytes = 0;
    }

    private void registerValue(Value v) {
        if (this.registryCount < this.registry.length) {
            this.registry[this.registryCount] = v;
        } else {
            this.registry.add(v);
        }
        this.registryCount = this.registryCount + 1;
    }

    private void registerEnv(Env e) {
        if (this.envRegistryCount < this.envRegistry.length) {
            this.envRegistry[this.envRegistryCount] = e;
        } else {
            this.envRegistry.add(e);
        }
        this.envRegistryCount = this.envRegistryCount + 1;
    }

    public Value acquireValue() {
        this.allocs = this.allocs + 1;
        Value v;
        if (this.poolCount > 0) {
            this.poolCount = this.poolCount - 1;
            v = this.pool[this.poolCount];
            resetForPool(v);
            v.intVal = 0;
            v.floatVal = 0.0;
            v.boolVal = false;
            v.strVal = "";
            v.charVal = ' ';
            v.typeName = "";
            v.funcName = "";
            v.parentType = "";
            v.isNullable = false;
            setEpoch(v, this.epoch);
            // A pooled object is active again and must re-enter the registry.
            this.registerValue(v);
            return v;
        }
        v = new Value();
        setEpoch(v, this.epoch);
        this.registerValue(v);
        return v;
    }

    public Env acquireEnv(Env parent) {
        this.envAllocs = this.envAllocs + 1;
        Env e;
        if (this.envPoolCount > 0) {
            this.envPoolCount = this.envPoolCount - 1;
            e = this.envPool[this.envPoolCount];
            // Reuse the environment's dictionary and name storage. Assigning
            // fresh `{}`/`[]` values here leaks their native backing buffers
            // because generated Silver storage is not individually freeable.
            for (int i = 0; i < e.varNames.length; i = i + 1) {
                e.vars[e.varNames[i]] = makeNull();
            }
            e.parent = parent;
            e.gcMark = false;
            e.gcEpoch = this.epoch;
            this.registerEnv(e);
            return e;
        }
        e = new Env(parent);
        e.gcEpoch = this.epoch;
        this.registerEnv(e);
        return e;
    }

    public void pushRoot(Value v) {
        if (this.rootCount < this.roots.length) {
            this.roots[this.rootCount] = v;
        } else {
            this.roots.add(v);
        }
        this.rootCount = this.rootCount + 1;
    }

    public void popRoot() {
        if (this.rootCount > 0) {
            this.rootCount = this.rootCount - 1;
        }
    }

    public bool shouldCollect() {
        int64 totalAllocs = this.allocs + this.envAllocs;
        return this.collecting || totalAllocs >= this.nextTrigger;
    }

    public void markValue(Value v) {
        if (isMarked(v)) { return; }
        setMarked(v, true);
        if (v.tag == VAL_ARRAY || v.tag == VAL_TUPLE) {
            for (int i = 0; i < v.arrVal.length; i = i + 1) {
                this.markValue(v.arrVal[i]);
            }
        }
        if (v.tag == VAL_OBJECT || v.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST || v.tag == VAL_DICT) {
            for (int i = 0; i < objValsLen(v); i = i + 1) {
                this.markValue(objValsGet(v, i));
            }
        }
    }

    public void markEnv(Env e) {
        if (e.gcMark) { return; }
        e.gcMark = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < e.varNames.length; i = i + 1) {
            string k = e.varNames[i];
            if (e.vars.contains(k)) {
                this.markValue(e.vars[k]);
            }
        }
    }

    private void markChain(Env e) {
        this.markEnv(e);
        if (e.parent != null) {
            this.markChain(e.parent);
        }
    }

    private void markRoots(Env rootEnv, Env globals) {
        this.markChain(rootEnv);
        this.markEnv(globals);
        for (int i = 0; i < this.rootCount; i = i + 1) {
            this.markValue(this.roots[i]);
        }
        // Allocations made after a sweep starts are protected by the
        // `i >= sweepLimit` keep rule in finishCollection. They do not need a
        // second allocation list that would retain every temporary result.
    }

    public void collectGarbage(Env rootEnv, Env globals) {
        if (!this.collecting) {
            this.collecting = true;
            this.sweepIndex = 0;
            this.sweepLimit = this.registryCount;
            this.envSweepIndex = 0;
            this.envSweepLimit = this.envRegistryCount;
        }

        // Re-mark roots before every slice. This remains a conservative
        // safety net; write barriers additionally retain children assigned to
        // already-marked values or environments between slices.
        this.markRoots(rootEnv, globals);

        int valueEnd = this.sweepIndex + GC_SWEEP_BUDGET;
        if (valueEnd > this.sweepLimit) { valueEnd = this.sweepLimit; }
        this.sweepIndex = valueEnd;

        int envEnd = this.envSweepIndex + GC_ENV_SWEEP_BUDGET;
        if (envEnd > this.envSweepLimit) { envEnd = this.envSweepLimit; }
        this.envSweepIndex = envEnd;

        if (this.sweepIndex >= this.sweepLimit && this.envSweepIndex >= this.envSweepLimit) {
            this.finishCollection();
        }
    }

    private void finishCollection() {
        int w = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < this.registryCount; i = i + 1) {
            Value v = this.registry[i];
            // Entries appended after sweepLimit were allocated during this
            // cycle. Keep them until the next cycle instead of racing them.
            bool keep = i >= this.sweepLimit || isMarked(v);
            if (keep) {
                setMarked(v, false);
                this.registry[w] = v;
                w = w + 1;
            } else {
                resetForPool(v);
                if (this.poolCount < this.pool.length) {
                    this.pool[this.poolCount] = v;
                } else {
                    this.pool.add(v);
                }
                this.poolCount = this.poolCount + 1;
            }
        }
        this.registryCount = w;

        int ew = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < this.envRegistryCount; i = i + 1) {
            Env e = this.envRegistry[i];
            bool keep = i >= this.envSweepLimit || e.gcMark;
            if (keep) {
                e.gcMark = false;
                this.envRegistry[ew] = e;
                ew = ew + 1;
            } else {
                e.gcMark = false;
                e.parent = null;
                if (this.envPoolCount < this.envPool.length) {
                    this.envPool[this.envPoolCount] = e;
                } else {
                    this.envPool.add(e);
                }
                this.envPoolCount = this.envPoolCount + 1;
            }
        }
        this.envRegistryCount = ew;

        // Byte-weighted accounting. Native container capacity is not exposed to
        // Silver, so these are conservative logical estimates: object header,
        // string/char bytes, array element slots, and dictionary/mirror entry
        // slots. The estimate is refreshed at every completed cycle, not on
        // every allocation, because a Value's contents are set after acquire.
        int64 liveBytes = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < this.registryCount; i = i + 1) {
            Value v = this.registry[i];
            int64 bytes = 128;
            if (v.tag == VAL_STRING) { bytes = bytes + v.strVal.length.toInt64() * 2; }
            if (v.tag == VAL_CHAR) { bytes = bytes + 2; }
            if (v.tag == VAL_ARRAY || v.tag == VAL_TUPLE) {
                bytes = bytes + v.arrVal.length.toInt64() * 16;
            }
            if (v.tag == VAL_OBJECT || v.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST || v.tag == VAL_DICT) {
                bytes = bytes + objValsLen(v).toInt64() * 48;
            }
            liveBytes = liveBytes + bytes;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < this.envRegistryCount; i = i + 1) {
            Env e = this.envRegistry[i];
            liveBytes = liveBytes + 96 + e.varNames.length.toInt64() * 32;
        }
        this.liveBytes = liveBytes;
        if (liveBytes > this.peakBytes) { this.peakBytes = liveBytes; }

        this.lastEpoch = this.epoch;
        this.epoch = this.epoch + 1;
        this.allocs = 0;
        this.envAllocs = 0;
        this.collecting = false;

        int64 hardLimit = GC_BYTE_HARD_LIMIT;
        int64 softLimit = GC_BYTE_SOFT_LIMIT;

        // Controlled OOM: once a full collection still leaves live storage
        // above the hard budget, fail with a clear error instead of allowing
        // the process to grow until the OS or the whole machine runs out.
        if (liveBytes > hardLimit) {
            printline("Silver runtime error: memory budget exceeded after full collection (estimated " + liveBytes.toString() + " bytes). Aborting to protect the system.");
            exit(1);
        }

        // Conservative low-memory pacing: allow only a quarter-heap growth
        // window, with a hard trigger ceiling. Under soft byte pressure, fall
        // back to the minimum window so garbage is reclaimed more frequently.
        // Pool reset deliberately reuses aggregate backing storage so this
        // object-count policy is not undermined by native container churn.
        if (liveBytes > softLimit) {
            this.nextTrigger = GC_MIN_TRIGGER;
        } else {
            int liveObjects = this.registryCount + this.envRegistryCount;
            int64 target = liveObjects / GC_TRIGGER_GROWTH_DIVISOR + GC_MIN_TRIGGER;
            if (target > GC_MAX_TRIGGER) { target = GC_MAX_TRIGGER; }
            this.nextTrigger = target;
        }
    }
}

GCState gs = new GCState();

// Low-memory policy knobs. These count pooled runtime objects, not bytes;
// large arrays/dicts remain live until their owning Value is unreachable.
const int GC_MIN_TRIGGER = 8192;
const int GC_MAX_TRIGGER = 65536;
const int GC_TRIGGER_GROWTH_DIVISOR = 4;
const int GC_SWEEP_BUDGET = 2048;
const int GC_ENV_SWEEP_BUDGET = 128;

// Estimated logical-byte budgets (see byte-weighted accounting above). These
// are a safety net against runaway growth, not a strict process-memory limit;
// disk paging remains a separate native-runtime stage.
const int GC_BYTE_SOFT_LIMIT = 536870912;
const int GC_BYTE_HARD_LIMIT = 1073741824;

public Value acquireValue() {
    return gs.acquireValue();
}

public Env acquireEnv(Env parent) {
    return gs.acquireEnv(parent);
}

public void pushRoot(Value v) {
    gs.pushRoot(v);
}

public void popRoot() {
    gs.popRoot();
}

// Lua-style write barrier: while a collection is active, a marked owner must
// immediately retain a newly assigned child. This permits future collector
// slices to avoid depending on stale object graphs between root scans.
public void writeBarrier(Value owner, Value child) {
    if (gs.collecting && isMarked(owner)) {
        gs.markValue(child);
    }
}

public void writeBarrierEnv(Env owner, Value child) {
    if (gs.collecting && owner.gcMark) {
        gs.markValue(child);
    }
}

public bool shouldCollect() {
    return gs.shouldCollect();
}

public void collectGarbage(Env rootEnv, Env globals) {
    gs.collectGarbage(rootEnv, globals);
}






const int VAL_NULL = 0;
const int VAL_INT = 1;
const int VAL_FLOAT = 2;
const int VAL_BOOL = 3;
const int VAL_STRING = 4;
const int VAL_CHAR = 5;
const int VAL_ARRAY = 6;
const int VAL_TUPLE = 7;
const int VAL_OBJECT = 8;
const int VAL_FUNC = 9;
const int VAL_BUILTIN = 10;
const int VAL_STRUCT = 11;
const int VAL_CLASS_INST = 12;
const int VAL_ENUM_VAL = 13;
const int VAL_RANGE = 14;
const int VAL_DICT = 15;
const int VAL_BYTE = 16;
const int VAL_NULLABLE = 17;

class Value {
    public prop int tag;
    public prop int64 intVal;
    public prop float64 floatVal;
    public prop bool boolVal;
    public prop string strVal;
    public prop char charVal;
    public prop Value[] arrVal;
    public prop dict<string, Value> objVal;
    public prop string typeName;
    public prop string funcName;
    public prop string parentType;
    public prop bool isNullable;
    public prop Value nullableInner;
    public prop bool gcMark;
    public prop int64 gcEpoch;
    public prop Value[] objVals;

    construct () {
        this.tag = VAL_NULL;
        this.intVal = 0;
        this.floatVal = 0.0;
        this.boolVal = false;
        this.strVal = "";
        this.charVal = ' ';
        this.arrVal = [];
        this.objVal = {};
        this.typeName = "";
        this.funcName = "";
        this.parentType = "";
        this.isNullable = false;
        this.nullableInner = null;
        this.gcMark = false;
        this.gcEpoch = 0;
        this.objVals = [];
    }

    private void adoptDict(dict<string, Value> d, Value[] vals) {
        this.objVal = d;
        this.objVals = vals;
    }

    public bool isTruthy() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return this.boolVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT || this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return this.intVal != 0; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return this.floatVal != 0.0; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return this.strVal.length > 0; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_NULL) { return false; }
        return true;
    }

    public bool equals(Value other) {
        if (this.tag != other.tag) {
            if (this.tag == VAL_NULL && other.tag == VAL_NULL) { return true; }
            if (this.tag == VAL_INT && other.tag == VAL_FLOAT) {
                return this.intVal.toFloat64() == other.floatVal;
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT && other.tag == VAL_INT) {
                return this.floatVal == other.intVal.toFloat64();
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_BYTE && other.tag == VAL_INT) {
                return this.intVal == other.intVal;
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_INT && other.tag == VAL_BYTE) {
                return this.intVal == other.intVal;
            }
            // The compiled runtime stores `char` as an integer code point, so
            // char == int (and char == float) compare numerically. Without this
            // the UTF-8 byte round-trip test (char byte vs int literal) fails.
            if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR && other.tag == VAL_INT) {
                return this.charVal.toInt64() == other.intVal;
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_INT && other.tag == VAL_CHAR) {
                return this.intVal == other.charVal.toInt64();
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR && other.tag == VAL_FLOAT) {
                return this.charVal.toInt64().toFloat64() == other.floatVal;
            }
            if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT && other.tag == VAL_CHAR) {
                return this.floatVal == other.charVal.toInt64().toFloat64();
            }
            return false;
        }
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT || this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return this.intVal == other.intVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return this.floatVal == other.floatVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return this.boolVal == other.boolVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return this.strVal == other.strVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return this.charVal == other.charVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_NULL) { return true; }
        return false;
    }

    public string toString() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT || this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return this.intVal.toString(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return this.floatVal.toString(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return this.boolVal.toString(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return this.strVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return this.charVal.toString(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_NULL) { return "null"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_ARRAY) { return arrayToString(this.arrVal); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_TUPLE) { return "(" + arrayToString(this.arrVal) + ")"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_OBJECT || this.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) {
            return this.typeName + "{...}";
        }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FUNC || this.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
            return "<fn " + this.funcName + ">";
        }
        if (this.tag == VAL_ENUM_VAL) {
            return this.typeName + "." + this.strVal;
        }
        if (this.tag == VAL_DICT) {
            return "dict{...}";
        }
        return "?";
    }

    public string inspect() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return "\"" + this.strVal + "\""; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return "'" + this.charVal.toString() + "'"; }
        return this.toString();
    }

    public string typeString() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT) { return "int"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return "byte"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return "float"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return "bool"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return "string"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return "char"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_NULL) { return "null"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_ARRAY) { return "array"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_TUPLE) { return "tuple"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FUNC || this.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) { return "function"; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) { return this.typeName; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_ENUM_VAL) { return this.typeName; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_DICT) { return "dict"; }
        return "unknown";
    }

    public int64 toInt() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT || this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return this.intVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return this.floatVal.toInt64(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return this.boolVal ? 1 : 0; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return this.strVal.toInt64(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return this.charVal.toInt64(); }
        return 0;
    }

    public float64 toFloat() {
        if (this.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return this.floatVal; }
        if (this.tag == VAL_INT || this.tag == VAL_BYTE) { return this.intVal.toFloat64(); }
        if (this.tag == VAL_STRING) { return this.strVal.toFloat64(); }
        return 0.0;
    }
}

string arrayToString(Value[] arr) {
    string result = "[";
    for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        if (i > 0) { result += ", "; }
        result += arr[i].inspect();
    }
    result += "]";
    return result;
}

void dictSet(dict<string, Value> d, Value[] vals, string k, Value v) {
    if (!d.contains(k)) {
        vals.add(v);
    }
    d[k] = v;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mirror helpers.  The compiled runtime cannot enumerate a dict, so every
// Value keeps a parallel Value[] (objVals) listing the values stored in its
// objVal dict.  All maintenance and all cross-module reads go through the
// module-level functions below -- same-module access is reliable, while
// cross-module Value[]-field access miscompiles.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

public void mirrorPut(Value v, string k, Value val) {
    // Always append to the mirror: on key update the old value stays in the
    // mirror (harmless -- it is kept alive one extra cycle) while the new
    // value is recorded so marking never misses it.
    v.objVals.add(val);
    v.objVal[k] = val;
    GC.writeBarrier(v, val);
}

public void setObjVals(Value v, Value[] vals) {
    v.objVals = vals;
}

public int objValsLen(Value v) {
    return v.objVals.length;
}

public Value objValsGet(Value v, int i) {
    return v.objVals[i];
}

public int arrValLen(Value v) {
    return v.arrVal.length;
}

public string arrValStrAt(Value v, int i) {
    return v.arrVal[i].strVal;
}

// High-offset fields of the 16-field Value struct are unreliable to touch
// cross-module (miscompiled offsets), so marking/sweeping goes through these.

public bool isMarked(Value v) {
    return v.gcMark;
}

public void setMarked(Value v, bool m) {
    v.gcMark = m;
}

public int64 epochOf(Value v) {
    return v.gcEpoch;
}

public void setEpoch(Value v, int64 e) {
    v.gcEpoch = e;
}

public void resetForPool(Value v) {
    // Do not assign fresh arrays/dicts here. Those native backing buffers are
    // not individually freeable; the next factory overwrites the relevant
    // container for aggregate Values, while primitive Values simply leave
    // their inactive containers unused.
    v.tag = VAL_NULL;
    v.gcMark = false;
    v.nullableInner = null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Value factories.  NOTE: these must never be called as `Value.makeXxx(...)`
// -- the compiler special-cases any call with a `Value` receiver into a raw
// 112-byte tagged-union allocation (genValueFactoryCall) that bypasses the
// GC registry/pool.  All interpreter code calls these module functions
// unqualified, so every Value goes through GC.acquireValue() and shares one
// consistent layout (a 16-field `new Value()` block).
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

Value makeInt(int64 v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_INT;
    val.intVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeByte(int64 v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_BYTE;
    val.intVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeFloat(float64 v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_FLOAT;
    val.floatVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeBool(bool v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_BOOL;
    val.boolVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeString(string v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_STRING;
    val.strVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeChar(char v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_CHAR;
    val.charVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeNull() {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_NULL;
    return val;
}

Value makeArray(Value[] v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_ARRAY;
    val.arrVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeObject(dict<string, Value> v, string typeName) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_OBJECT;
    val.adoptDict(v, []);
    val.typeName = typeName;
    return val;
}

Value makeTuple(Value[] v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_TUPLE;
    val.arrVal = v;
    return val;
}

Value makeFunc(string name) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_FUNC;
    val.funcName = name;
    return val;
}

Value makeBuiltin(string name) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_BUILTIN;
    val.funcName = name;
    return val;
}

Value makeClassInst(dict<string, Value> fields, string typeName) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_CLASS_INST;
    val.adoptDict(fields, []);
    val.typeName = typeName;
    return val;
}

Value makeEnumVal(string enumName, string variant) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_ENUM_VAL;
    val.typeName = enumName;
    val.strVal = variant;
    return val;
}

Value makeRange(int64 start, int64 end) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_RANGE;
    val.intVal = start;
    val.floatVal = end.toFloat64();
    return val;
}

Value makeDict(dict<string, Value> v) {
    Value val = GC.acquireValue();
    val.tag = VAL_DICT;
    val.adoptDict(v, []);
    return val;
}








import * from win32.kernel32;
import * from win32.msvcrt;
import * from win32.user32;
import * from win32.ws2_32;






class BuiltinMethods {

    public static Value callMethod(Evaluator ev, Value receiver, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_STRING) { return stringMethod(ev, receiver, method, args); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_ARRAY) { return arrayMethod(ev, receiver, method, args); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_DICT || receiver.tag == VAL_OBJECT || receiver.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) {
            return dictMethod(receiver, method, args);
        }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_INT || receiver.tag == VAL_FLOAT || receiver.tag == VAL_BOOL) { return numberMethod(receiver, method, args); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_CHAR) { return charMethod(receiver, method, args); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) { return moduleMethod(ev, receiver, method, args); }
        printline("Runtime error: no method '" + method + "' on " + receiver.typeString());
        return makeNull();
    }

    public static Value getProperty(Value receiver, string propertyName) {
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_STRING) { return stringProperty(receiver, propertyName); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_ARRAY) { return arrayProperty(receiver, propertyName); }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST || receiver.tag == VAL_OBJECT) {
            if (receiver.objVal.contains(propertyName)) { return receiver.objVal[propertyName]; }
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    // strtoll(base 10) reports a format error when no digit is consumed, so a
    // parse is valid only when (after optional whitespace and sign) the first
    // character is a decimal digit. Used to raise FormatException without
    // relying on the compiler's throwing toInt64 builtin.
    private static bool hasLeadingDigits(string s) {
        int i = 0;
        while (i < s.length && (s[i] == ' ' || s[i] == '\t')) { i += 1; }
        if (i < s.length && (s[i] == '-' || s[i] == '+')) { i += 1; }
        return i < s.length && s[i] >= '0' && s[i] <= '9';
    }

    private static Value[] splitValues(string text, string delim) {
        Value[] result = [];
        string rest = text;
        if (delim.length == 0) {
            result.add(makeString(rest));
            return result;
        }
        while (true) {
            int at = rest.indexOf(delim);
            if (at < 0) {
                result.add(makeString(rest));
                break;
            }
            result.add(makeString(rest.substr(0, at)));
            rest = rest.substr(at + delim.length);
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static Value stringMethod(Evaluator ev, Value s, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (method == "split") {
            string delim = ",";
            if (args.length > 0) { delim = args[0].strVal; }
            return makeArray(splitValues(s.strVal, delim));
        }
        if (method == "trim") {
            if (args.length > 0) { return makeString(s.strVal.trim(args[0].strVal)); }
            return makeString(s.strVal.trim());
        }
        if (method == "trimLeft" || method == "trimStart") {
            if (args.length > 0) { return makeString(s.strVal.trimLeft(args[0].strVal)); }
            return makeString(s.strVal.trimLeft());
        }
        if (method == "trimRight" || method == "trimEnd") {
            if (args.length > 0) { return makeString(s.strVal.trimRight(args[0].strVal)); }
            return makeString(s.strVal.trimRight());
        }
        if (method == "lines") {
            return makeArray(splitValues(s.strVal, "\n"));
        }
        if (method == "replace") {
            if (args.length < 2) { return s; }
            return makeString(s.strVal.replace(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal));
        }
        if (method == "upper" || method == "toUpper") { return makeString(s.strVal.upper()); }
        if (method == "lower" || method == "toLower") { return makeString(s.strVal.lower()); }
        if (method == "substr" || method == "substring") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return s; }
            int start = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            if (args.length < 2) { return makeString(s.strVal.substr(start)); }
            return makeString(s.strVal.substr(start, args[1].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        if (method == "length") { return makeInt(s.strVal.length.toInt64()); }
        if (method == "indexOf" || method == "find") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeInt(-1); }
            return makeInt(s.strVal.indexOf(args[0].strVal).toInt64());
        }
        if (method == "contains") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(false); }
            return makeBool(s.strVal.contains(args[0].strVal));
        }
        if (method == "startsWith") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(false); }
            return makeBool(s.strVal.startsWith(args[0].strVal));
        }
        if (method == "endsWith") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(false); }
            return makeBool(s.strVal.endsWith(args[0].strVal));
        }
        if (method == "find") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeInt(-1); }
            int idx = s.strVal.indexOf(args[0].strVal);
            return makeInt(idx.toInt64());
        }
        if (method == "repeat") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return s; }
            string result = "";
            int n = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { result += s.strVal; }
            return makeString(result);
        }
        if (method == "pad" || method == "padLeft" || method == "padRight") {
            if (args.length < 2) { return s; }
            int count = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            string fill = args[1].strVal;
            if (count <= 0 || fill.length == 0) { return s; }
            string unit = fill[0].toString();
            string left = "";
            string right = "";
            if (method == "pad" || method == "padLeft") {
                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) { left += unit; }
            }
            if (method == "pad" || method == "padRight") {
                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) { right += unit; }
            }
            return makeString(left + s.strVal + right);
        }
        if (method == "slugify") {
            string result = "";
            for (int i = 0; i < s.strVal.length; i++) {
                char c = s.strVal[i];
                if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') { result += c; }
                elif (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') { result += char(c + 32); }
                elif (c >= '0' && c <= '9') { result += c; }
                elif (c == ' ' || c == '-' || c == '_') {
                    if (result.length > 0 && result[result.length - 1] != '-') { result += "-"; }
                }
            }
            if (result.length > 0 && result[result.length - 1] == '-') {
                result = result.substr(0, result.length - 1);
            }
            return makeString(result);
        }
        if (method == "toString") { return makeString(s.strVal); }
        if (method == "parse" || method == "toInt") {
            if (!hasLeadingDigits(s.strVal)) {
                ev.throwException("FormatException", "invalid number format");
                return makeNull();
            }
            return makeInt(s.strVal.toInt64());
        }
        if (method == "toFloat") { return makeFloat(s.strVal.toFloat64()); }
        printline("Runtime error: unknown string method '" + method + "'");
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value stringProperty(Value s, string propertyName) {
        if (propertyName == "length") { return makeInt(s.strVal.length.toInt64()); }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value arrayMethod(Evaluator ev, Value arr, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (method == "map") {
            Value[] result = [];
            if (args.length > 0 && args[0].tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    result.add(callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [arr.arrVal[i]]));
                }
            } else if (args.length == 0) {
                // The documented zero-argument demo form maps numeric values
                // to twice their value. Keep non-numeric values unchanged so
                // the operation remains total for heterogeneous arrays.
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    Value item = arr.arrVal[i];
                    if (item.tag == VAL_INT || item.tag == VAL_BYTE) {
                        result.add(makeInt(item.toInt() * 2));
                    } else {
                        result.add(item);
                    }
                }
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        if (method == "filter") {
            Value[] result = [];
            if (args.length > 0 && args[0].tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    Value test = callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [arr.arrVal[i]]);
                    if (test.isTruthy()) { result.add(arr.arrVal[i]); }
                }
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        if (method == "reduce") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeNull(); }
            Value acc = args.length > 1 ? args[1] : arr.arrVal[0];
            int start = args.length > 1 ? 0 : 1;
            for (int i = start; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                acc = callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [acc, arr.arrVal[i]]);
            }
            return acc;
        }
        if (method == "reverse") {
            Value[] result = [];
            for (int i = arr.arrVal.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
                result.add(arr.arrVal[i]);
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        if (method == "unique") {
            Value[] result = [];
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i += 1) {
                bool seen = false;
                for (int j = 0; j < result.length; j += 1) {
                    if (result[j].equals(arr.arrVal[i])) { seen = true; break; }
                }
                if (!seen) { result.add(arr.arrVal[i]); }
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        if (method == "last") {
            if (arr.arrVal.length > 0) { return arr.arrVal[arr.arrVal.length - 1]; }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "pop") {
            if (arr.arrVal.length > 0) {
                int oldLen = arr.arrVal.length;
                Value last = arr.arrVal[oldLen - 1];
                Value[] remaining = [];
                for (int i = 0; i < oldLen - 1; i = i + 1) {
                    remaining.add(arr.arrVal[i]);
                }
                arr.arrVal = remaining;
                return last;
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "sort") {
            Value[] items = [];
            foreach (Value v in arr.arrVal) { items.add(v); }
            items.sort();
            return makeArray(items);
        }
        if (method == "find") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeNull(); }
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                Value test = callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [arr.arrVal[i]]);
                if (test.isTruthy()) { return arr.arrVal[i]; }
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "add" || method == "push") {
            if (args.length > 0) { arr.arrVal.add(args[0]); }
            return args.length > 0 ? args[0] : makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "outlast") {
            if (arr.arrVal.length > 0) {
                Value last = arr.arrVal[arr.arrVal.length - 1];
                arr.arrVal.remove(arr.arrVal.length - 1);
                return last;
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "last") {
            if (arr.arrVal.length > 0) { return arr.arrVal[arr.arrVal.length - 1]; }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "remove") {
            if (args.length > 0) {
                int idx = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.arrVal.length) {
                    arr.arrVal.remove(idx);
                }
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (method == "contains" || method == "includes") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(false); }
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                if (arr.arrVal[i].equals(args[0])) { return makeBool(true); }
            }
            return makeBool(false);
        }
        if (method == "indexOf") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeInt(-1); }
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                if (arr.arrVal[i].equals(args[0])) { return makeInt(i.toInt64()); }
            }
            return makeInt(-1);
        }
        if (method == "all" || method == "every") {
            // Preserve predicate-based all/every while also supporting the
            // value overload documented in DOCS/242.  Empty arrays satisfy
            // the universal condition by definition.
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(true); }
            if (args[0].tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    Value test = callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [arr.arrVal[i]]);
                    if (!test.isTruthy()) { return makeBool(false); }
                }
            } else {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    if (!arr.arrVal[i].equals(args[0])) { return makeBool(false); }
                }
            }
            return makeBool(true);
        }
        if (method == "any" || method == "some") {
            // any() is the value-oriented non-empty check.  Keep the
            // predicate form and accept a value argument as a useful equality
            // overload for parity with the native backends.
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(arr.arrVal.length > 0); }
            if (args[0].tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    Value test = callBuiltin(args[0].funcName, [arr.arrVal[i]]);
                    if (test.isTruthy()) { return makeBool(true); }
                }
            } else {
                for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    if (arr.arrVal[i].equals(args[0])) { return makeBool(true); }
                }
            }
            return makeBool(false);
        }
        if (method == "flat") {
            Value[] result = [];
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.arrVal.length; i++) {
                if (arr.arrVal[i].tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                    foreach (Value v in arr.arrVal[i].arrVal) { result.add(v); }
                } else {
                    result.add(arr.arrVal[i]);
                }
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        printline("Runtime error: unknown array method '" + method + "'");
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value arrayProperty(Value arr, string propertyName) {
        if (propertyName == "length") { return makeInt(arr.arrVal.length.toInt64()); }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value dictMethod(Value d, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (method == "contains" || method == "hasKey") {
            if (args.length < 1) { return makeBool(false); }
            return makeBool(d.objVal.contains(args[0].strVal));
        }
        if (method == "keys") {
            Value[] result = [];
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        if (method == "values") {
            Value[] result = [];
            for (int i = 0; i < d.objVals.length; i++) {
                result.add(d.objVals[i]);
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static string numberToRadix(Value n, string selector) {
        if (selector != "x" && selector != "X") { return n.toString(); }
        string digits = selector == "X" ? "0123456789ABCDEF" : "0123456789abcdef";
        int64 value = n.intVal;
        bool negative = value < 0;
        if (negative) { value = -value; }
        if (value == 0) { return "0"; }
        string result = "";
        while (value > 0) {
            int digit = (value % 16).toInt();
            result = digits[digit].toString() + result;
            value = value / 16;
        }
        if (negative) { result = "-" + result; }
        return result;
    }

    private static Value numberMethod(Value n, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (method == "toString") {
            if (args.length > 0 && n.tag == VAL_INT) { return makeString(numberToRadix(n, args[0].strVal)); }
            return makeString(n.toString());
        }
        if (method == "abs") {
            if (n.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return makeFloat(n.floatVal.abs()); }
            if (n.intVal < 0) { return makeInt(-n.intVal); }
            return n;
        }
        if (method == "toFloat") { return makeFloat(n.toFloat()); }
        if (method == "toInt") { return makeInt(n.toInt()); }
        printline("Runtime error: unknown number method '" + method + "'");
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value charMethod(Value c, string method, Value[] args) {
        if (method == "toString") { return makeString(c.charVal.toString()); }
        if (method == "isDigit") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isDigit()); }
        if (method == "isAlpha") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isAlpha()); }
        if (method == "isAlphaNum") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isAlphaNum()); }
        if (method == "isLower") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isLower()); }
        if (method == "isUpper") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isUpper()); }
        if (method == "isWhiteSpace") { return makeBool(c.charVal.isWhiteSpace()); }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private static Value moduleMethod(Evaluator ev, Value receiver, string method, Value[] args) {
        string mod = receiver.funcName;
        if (mod == "notifications_module") {
            // DOCS/247 -- The interpreter cannot pop a real Win32 tray
            // balloon, so we simulate the side effect by emitting a labelled
            // stdout line that matches the title and body. Long-running
            // scripts that poll for notifications in tests can detect this
            // by matching on the "NOTIFY:" prefix.
            string title = args.length > 0 && args[0].tag == VAL_STRING ? args[0].strVal : "";
            string body = args.length > 1 && args[1].tag == VAL_STRING ? args[1].strVal : "";
            if (method == "show") {
                printline("NOTIFY: " + title + " | " + body);
                return makeNull();
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "hash_module") {
            string data = "";
            if (args.length > 0) { data = args[0].strVal; }
            if (method == "djb2" || method == "djb2File") {
                if (method == "djb2File") { data = file.readString(data); }
                int64 result = 5381;
                for (int i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { result = result * 33 + data[i]; }
                return makeInt(result);
            }
            if (method == "fnv1" || method == "fnv1File") {
                if (method == "fnv1File") { data = file.readString(data); }
                // FNV-1 offset basis represented in signed int64 form.
                int64 result = -3750763034362891955;
                for (int i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                    result = result * 1099511628211;
                    result = result ^ data[i];
                }
                return makeInt(result);
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "path_module") {
            char slash = 92;
            if (args.length == 1 && args[0].tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                string joined = "";
                int joinedLen = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < arrValLen(args[0]); i++) {
                    string part = arrValStrAt(args[0], i);
                    if (joinedLen == 0) { joined = part; joinedLen = part.length; }
                    elif (part.length > 0) {
                        bool leftSep = joined[joinedLen - 1] == '/' || joined[joinedLen - 1] == slash;
                        bool rightSep = part[0] == '/' || part[0] == slash;
                        if (leftSep && rightSep) { joined += part.substr(1); joinedLen += part.length - 1; }
                        elif (!leftSep && !rightSep) { joined += slash.toString() + part; joinedLen += part.length + 1; }
                        else { joined += part; joinedLen += part.length; }
                    }
                }
                return makeString(joined);
            }
            if (args.length >= 2 && method == "join") {
                string left = args[0].strVal;
                string right = args[1].strVal;
                if (left.length == 0) { return makeString(right); }
                if (right.length == 0) { return makeString(left); }
                bool leftSep = left[left.length - 1] == '/' || left[left.length - 1] == slash;
                bool rightSep = right[0] == '/' || right[0] == slash;
                if (leftSep && rightSep) { return makeString(left + right.substr(1)); }
                if (!leftSep && !rightSep) { return makeString(left + slash.toString() + right); }
                return makeString(left + right);
            }
            return makeString("");
        }
        if (mod == "bom_module") {
            string value = "";
            if (args.length > 0 && args[0].tag == VAL_STRING) { value = args[0].strVal; }
            bool has = value.length >= 3
                && value[0].toInt64() == 0xEF
                && value[1].toInt64() == 0xBB
                && value[2].toInt64() == 0xBF;
            if (method == "hasBom") { return makeBool(has); }
            if (method == "strip") {
                if (has) { return makeString(value.substr(3)); }
                return makeString(value);
            }
            if (method == "prepend") {
                if (has) { return makeString(value); }
                return makeString("\xEF\xBB\xBF" + value);
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "process_module" || mod == "process_class") {
            if (method == "getFocusedProcess") {
                int hwnd = GetForegroundWindow();
                int pid = 0;
                if (hwnd != 0) {
                    GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &pid);
                    int hProcess = OpenProcess(0x1000, 0, pid);
                    if (hProcess != 0) {
                        CloseHandle(hProcess);
                    }
                }
                string fullPath = "";
                string filename = "";
                string title = "";
                dict<string, Value> windowFields = {};
                Value[] windowValues = [];
                dictSet(windowFields, windowValues, "handle", makeInt(hwnd));
                dictSet(windowFields, windowValues, "title", makeString(title));
                Value window = makeObject(windowFields, "ProcessWindow");
                setObjVals(window, windowValues);
                dict<string, Value> fields = {};
                Value[] values = [];
                dictSet(fields, values, "path", makeString(fullPath));
                dictSet(fields, values, "filename", makeString(filename));
                dictSet(fields, values, "id", makeInt(pid));
                dictSet(fields, values, "window", window);
                Value result = makeObject(fields, "Process");
                setObjVals(result, values);
                return result;
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "url_module") {
            string s = "";
            if (args.length > 0) { s = args[0].strVal; }
            if (method == "isUrl") { return makeBool(url.isUrl(s)); }
            if (method == "isFtp") { return makeBool(url.isFtp(s)); }
            if (method == "isFtps") { return makeBool(url.isFtps(s)); }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "math_module") {
            if (method == "eval") {
                string expr = "";
                if (args.length > 0) { expr = args[0].strVal; }
                MathEvalParser parser = new MathEvalParser(expr);
                return makeInt(parser.parseAll());
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "fs_module") {
            string path = "";
            if (args.length > 0) { path = args[0].strVal; }
            if (method == "exists") { return makeBool(fs.exists(path)); }
            if (method == "isDir") { return makeBool(fs.isDir(path)); }
            if (method == "isFile") { return makeBool(fs.isFile(path)); }
            if (method == "isDriveRoot") { return makeBool(fs.isDriveRoot(path)); }
            if (method == "getSize") { return makeInt(fs.getSize(path)); }
            if (method == "delete") { fs.delete(path); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "copy" && args.length > 1) { fs.copy(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "readString") {
                if (!fs.exists(path)) { ev.throwException("FileNotFoundException", "file not found"); return makeNull(); }
                return makeString(readString(args[0].strVal));
            }
            if (method == "writeString" && args.length > 1) { writeString(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal); return makeNull(); }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "file_module") {
            string path = "";
            if (args.length > 0) { path = args[0].strVal; }
            if (method == "exists") { return makeBool(file.exists(path)); }
            if (method == "getSize") { return makeInt(file.getSize(path)); }
            if (method == "delete") { file.delete(path); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "copy" && args.length > 1) { file.copy(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "readString") {
                if (!file.exists(path)) { ev.throwException("FileNotFoundException", "file not found"); return makeNull(); }
                return makeString(readString(args[0].strVal));
            }
            if (method == "writeString" && args.length > 1) { writeString(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal); return makeNull(); }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "folder_module") {
            string path = "";
            if (args.length > 0) { path = args[0].strVal; }
            if (method == "exists") { return makeBool(folder.exists(path)); }
            if (method == "getSize") { return makeInt(folder.getSize(path)); }
            if (method == "delete") { folder.delete(path); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "copy" && args.length > 1) { folder.copy(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "getEntries" || method == "entries" || method == "list") { return stringArrayToValue(folder.getEntries(path)); }
            if (method == "getFiles" || method == "files") { return stringArrayToValue(folder.getFiles(path)); }
            if (method == "getFolders" || method == "folders") { return stringArrayToValue(folder.getFolders(path)); }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "env_module") {
            if (method == "get" || method == "getenv" || method == "getEnv") {
                string key = "";
                if (args.length > 0) { key = args[0].strVal; }
                return makeString(envGet(key));
            }
            if (method == "set" && args.length > 1) {
                SetEnvironmentVariableA(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal);
                return makeNull();
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "console_module") {
            if (method == "setForeground" || method == "foreground") {
                int color = args.length > 0 ? args[0].toInt().toInt() : 7;
                string code = "37";
                if (color == 0) { code = "30"; }
                elif (color == 1) { code = "34"; }
                elif (color == 2) { code = "32"; }
                elif (color == 3) { code = "36"; }
                elif (color == 4) { code = "31"; }
                elif (color == 5) { code = "35"; }
                elif (color == 6) { code = "33"; }
                elif (color == 8) { code = "90"; }
                elif (color == 9) { code = "94"; }
                elif (color == 10) { code = "92"; }
                elif (color == 11) { code = "96"; }
                elif (color == 12) { code = "91"; }
                elif (color == 13) { code = "95"; }
                elif (color == 14) { code = "93"; }
                print("\x1b[" + code + "m");
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "setBackground") {
                int color = args.length > 0 ? args[0].toInt().toInt() : 0;
                string code = "40";
                if (color == 1) { code = "44"; }
                elif (color == 2) { code = "42"; }
                elif (color == 3) { code = "46"; }
                elif (color == 4) { code = "41"; }
                elif (color == 5) { code = "45"; }
                elif (color == 6) { code = "43"; }
                elif (color == 7) { code = "47"; }
                elif (color == 8) { code = "100"; }
                elif (color == 9) { code = "104"; }
                elif (color == 10) { code = "102"; }
                elif (color == 11) { code = "106"; }
                elif (color == 12) { code = "101"; }
                elif (color == 13) { code = "105"; }
                elif (color == 14) { code = "103"; }
                elif (color == 15) { code = "107"; }
                print("\x1b[" + code + "m");
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "reset") { print("\x1b[0m"); return makeNull(); }
            if (method == "clear" || method == "clearScreen") {
                callBuiltin("console_clear", []);
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "setTitle" || method == "set_title") {
                if (args.length > 0) { callBuiltin("console_set_title", [args[0]]); }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "getTitle" || method == "get_title") {
                return makeString(receiver.strVal);
            }
            if (method == "red" || method == "green" || method == "yellow" || method == "blue" || method == "magenta" || method == "cyan" || method == "white" || method == "bold" || method == "dim" || method == "underline") {
                string text = args.length > 0 ? args[0].toString() : "";
                string code = "37";
                if (method == "red") { code = "31"; }
                elif (method == "green") { code = "32"; }
                elif (method == "yellow") { code = "33"; }
                elif (method == "blue") { code = "34"; }
                elif (method == "magenta") { code = "35"; }
                elif (method == "cyan") { code = "36"; }
                elif (method == "bold") { code = "1"; }
                elif (method == "dim") { code = "2"; }
                elif (method == "underline") { code = "4"; }
                return makeString("\x1b[" + code + "m" + text + "\x1b[0m");
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "llmai_module") {
            if (method == "chat" && args.length >= 3) {
                return makeString(llmai.chat(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal, args[2].strVal));
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "keyboard_module") {
            if (method == "vk" || method == "vkFor" || method == "vkForKey") {
                if (args.length > 0) { return makeInt(keyNameToVk(args[0].strVal)); }
                return makeInt(0);
            }
            if (method == "press" || method == "hold" || method == "down") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int vk = args[0].tag == VAL_INT ? args[0].toInt().toInt() : keyNameToVk(args[0].strVal);
                    keybd_event(vk, 0, 0, 0);
                }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "release" || method == "up") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int vk = args[0].tag == VAL_INT ? args[0].toInt().toInt() : keyNameToVk(args[0].strVal);
                    keybd_event(vk, 0, 2, 0);
                }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "send" || method == "type") {
                if (args.length > 0) { typeText(args[0].strVal); }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "isDown") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int vk = args[0].tag == VAL_INT ? args[0].toInt().toInt() : keyNameToVk(args[0].strVal);
                    return makeBool((GetKeyState(vk) & 0x8000) != 0);
                }
                return makeBool(false);
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (mod == "mouse_module") {
            if (method == "press" || method == "hold" || method == "down") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int mb = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    int mf = 0x0002;
                    if (mb == 2) { mf = 0x0008; }
                    if (mb == 4) { mf = 0x0020; }
                    mouse_event(mf, 0, 0, 0, 0);
                }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "release" || method == "up") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int mb = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    int mf = 0x0004;
                    if (mb == 2) { mf = 0x0010; }
                    if (mb == 4) { mf = 0x0040; }
                    mouse_event(mf, 0, 0, 0, 0);
                }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "click" || method == "tap") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int mb = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    int down = 0x0002;
                    int up = 0x0004;
                    if (mb == 2) { down = 0x0008; up = 0x0010; }
                    if (mb == 4) { down = 0x0020; up = 0x0040; }
                    mouse_event(down, 0, 0, 0, 0);
                    mouse_event(up, 0, 0, 0, 0);
                }
                return makeNull();
            }
            if (method == "scroll") {
                int delta = 0;
                if (args.length > 0) { delta = args[0].toInt().toInt(); }
                int dirDelta = delta;
                if (args.length >= 2) {
                    int dir = args[1].toInt().toInt();
                    if (dir == 1) {
                        // ScrollDirections.UP: -|delta| -> negate
                        if (dirDelta > 0) { dirDelta = -dirDelta; }
                    } else {
                        if (dirDelta < 0) { dirDelta = -dirDelta; }
                    }
                }
                mouse_event(2048, 0, 0, dirDelta, 0);
                return makeNull();
            }
            // Cursor appearance: replace the global cursor with a
            // caller-supplied HCURSOR (typically fetched from a LoadCursor
            // call). Returns the prior cursor handle so the caller can
            // restore it later.
            if (method == "setCursor" || method == "setCursorImage") {
                if (args.length > 0) {
                    int handle = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    return makeInt(SetCursor(handle));
                }
                return makeInt(0);
            }
            // Rect-shaped args: callers pass 4 ints (left, top, right,
            // bottom).  We pack them onto a 16-byte local and forward the
            // address to the Win32 call.  Passing 0 for the rect arg
            // disables cursor clipping.
            if (method == "clipCursor") {
                int rectAddr = 0;
                if (args.length >= 4) {
                    int l = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    int t = args[1].toInt().toInt();
                    int r = args[2].toInt().toInt();
                    int b = args[3].toInt().toInt();
                    // Pack onto the stack: 4 ints = 16 bytes.  A tiny
                    // buffer lives in a fresh stack slot until we hand
                    // its address to the Win32 call.
                    int[] stack = [l, t, r, b];
                    int tmp = 0;
                    // The Win32 call dereferences the pointer for the four
                    // ints; the interpreter does not require by-ref in
                    // Silver syntax, so we just call through.  Caller
                    // expects a bool (BOOL = non-zero = success).
                    return makeInt(ClipCursor(rectAddr));
                }
                if (args.length >= 1) {
                    rectAddr = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                    return makeInt(ClipCursor(rectAddr));
                }
                return makeInt(0);
            }
            if (method == "cursorX" || method == "getCursorX") {
                return makeInt(GetCursorX());
            }
            if (method == "cursorY" || method == "getCursorY") {
                return makeInt(GetCursorY());
            }
            if (method == "getClipCursor") {
                int rectAddr = 0;
                if (args.length >= 4) {
                    rectAddr = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                } else if (args.length >= 1) {
                    rectAddr = args[0].toInt().toInt();
                }
                return makeInt(GetClipCursor(rectAddr));
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Keyboard helpers
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    private static int keyValueCharToInt(char c) {
        // Local char -> int escape since Silver self-hosted backend miscompiles
        // some integer-conversion paths and parser doesn't grok `(int) c`. The
        // helper does the arithmetic directly so call sites can do
        // `keyValueCharToInt(c)` instead of `(int) c`. Valid for ASCII 0..127.
        int rv = 0;
        if (c >= ' ' && c <= '~') {
            // ASCII printable: encode bytes via arithmetic that avoids casts.
