            // Each digit-set between ' ' (0x20) and '~' (0x7E) is well-defined.
            rv = 0;
            // Use a tiny switch ladder to avoid `(int) c`.
            if (c == ' ') { rv = 0x20; }
            if (c == '!') { rv = 0x21; }
            if (c == '"') { rv = 0x22; }
            if (c == '#') { rv = 0x23; }
            if (c == '$') { rv = 0x24; }
            if (c == '%') { rv = 0x25; }
            if (c == '&') { rv = 0x26; }
            if (c == '\'') { rv = 0x27; }
            if (c == '(') { rv = 0x28; }
            if (c == ')') { rv = 0x29; }
            if (c == '*') { rv = 0x2A; }
            if (c == '+') { rv = 0x2B; }
            if (c == ',') { rv = 0x2C; }
            if (c == '-') { rv = 0x2D; }
            if (c == '.') { rv = 0x2E; }
            if (c == '/') { rv = 0x2F; }
            if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') { rv = keyValueCharToIntDigit(c); }
            if (c == ':') { rv = 0x3A; }
            if (c == ';') { rv = 0x3B; }
            if (c == '<') { rv = 0x3C; }
            if (c == '=') { rv = 0x3D; }
            if (c == '>') { rv = 0x3E; }
            if (c == '?') { rv = 0x3F; }
            if (c == '@') { rv = 0x40; }
            if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') { rv = keyValueCharToIntUpper(c); }
            if (c == '[') { rv = 0x5B; }
            if (c == '\\') { rv = 0x5C; }
            if (c == ']') { rv = 0x5D; }
            if (c == '^') { rv = 0x5E; }
            if (c == '_') { rv = 0x5F; }
            if (c == '`') { rv = 0x60; }
            if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') { rv = keyValueCharToIntLower(c); }
            if (c == '{') { rv = 0x7B; }
            if (c == '|') { rv = 0x7C; }
            if (c == '}') { rv = 0x7D; }
            if (c == '~') { rv = 0x7E; }
            return rv;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int keyValueCharToIntDigit(char c) {
        // Returns the Win32 VK code for an ASCII digit (0x30..0x39).
        // silverc.exe's self-hosted codegen has a known bug with literal
        // returns inside if-ladders of char comparisons, so we use the
        // string's indexOf to map the digit to its position in the
        // lookup string, then add the VK_0 base (0x30) to that index.
        string digits = "0123456789";
        int idx = digits.indexOf(c.toString());
        if (idx < 0) { return 0; }
        return 0x30 + idx;
    }

    private static int keyValueCharToIntUpper(char c) {
        if (c == 'A') { return 0x41; }
        if (c == 'B') { return 0x42; }
        if (c == 'C') { return 0x43; }
        if (c == 'D') { return 0x44; }
        if (c == 'E') { return 0x45; }
        if (c == 'F') { return 0x46; }
        if (c == 'G') { return 0x47; }
        if (c == 'H') { return 0x48; }
        if (c == 'I') { return 0x49; }
        if (c == 'J') { return 0x4A; }
        if (c == 'K') { return 0x4B; }
        if (c == 'L') { return 0x4C; }
        if (c == 'M') { return 0x4D; }
        if (c == 'N') { return 0x4E; }
        if (c == 'O') { return 0x4F; }
        if (c == 'P') { return 0x50; }
        if (c == 'Q') { return 0x51; }
        if (c == 'R') { return 0x52; }
        if (c == 'S') { return 0x53; }
        if (c == 'T') { return 0x54; }
        if (c == 'U') { return 0x55; }
        if (c == 'V') { return 0x56; }
        if (c == 'W') { return 0x57; }
        if (c == 'X') { return 0x58; }
        if (c == 'Y') { return 0x59; }
        if (c == 'Z') { return 0x5A; }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int keyValueCharToIntLower(char c) {
        if (c == 'a') { return 0x61; }
        if (c == 'b') { return 0x62; }
        if (c == 'c') { return 0x63; }
        if (c == 'd') { return 0x64; }
        if (c == 'e') { return 0x65; }
        if (c == 'f') { return 0x66; }
        if (c == 'g') { return 0x67; }
        if (c == 'h') { return 0x68; }
        if (c == 'i') { return 0x69; }
        if (c == 'j') { return 0x6A; }
        if (c == 'k') { return 0x6B; }
        if (c == 'l') { return 0x6C; }
        if (c == 'm') { return 0x6D; }
        if (c == 'n') { return 0x6E; }
        if (c == 'o') { return 0x6F; }
        if (c == 'p') { return 0x70; }
        if (c == 'q') { return 0x71; }
        if (c == 'r') { return 0x72; }
        if (c == 's') { return 0x73; }
        if (c == 't') { return 0x74; }
        if (c == 'u') { return 0x75; }
        if (c == 'v') { return 0x76; }
        if (c == 'w') { return 0x77; }
        if (c == 'x') { return 0x78; }
        if (c == 'y') { return 0x79; }
        if (c == 'z') { return 0x7A; }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int keyNameToVk(string name) {
        if (name.length == 1) {
            char c = name[0];
            if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') { return keyValueCharToInt(c); }
            if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') { return keyValueCharToInt('A' + (c - 'a')); }
            if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') { return keyValueCharToInt(c); }
            if (c == ' ')  { return 0x20; }
            if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') { return 0x0D; }
            if (c == '\t') { return 0x09; }
        }
        if (name == "TAB")       { return 0x09; }
        if (name == "SPACE")     { return 0x20; }
        if (name == "SPACE")     { return 0x20; }
        if (name == "ENTER" || name == "RETURN") { return 0x0D; }
        if (name == "ESC" || name == "ESCAPE") { return 0x1B; }
        if (name == "BACKSPACE") { return 0x08; }
        if (name == "DELETE" || name == "DEL") { return 0x2E; }
        if (name == "INSERT")    { return 0x2D; }
        if (name == "HOME")      { return 0x24; }
        if (name == "END")       { return 0x23; }
        if (name == "PAGEUP"  || name == "PRIOR")   { return 0x21; }
        if (name == "PAGEDOWN" || name == "NEXT")   { return 0x22; }
        if (name == "LEFT")      { return 0x25; }
        if (name == "UP")        { return 0x26; }
        if (name == "RIGHT")     { return 0x27; }
        if (name == "DOWN")      { return 0x28; }
        if (name == "SHIFT")     { return 0x10; }
        if (name == "CTRL" || name == "CONTROL") { return 0x11; }
        if (name == "ALT" || name == "MENU") { return 0x12; }
        if (name == "WIN_KEY" || name == "WIN") { return 0x5B; }
        if (name == "CAPS_LOCK" || name == "CAPS") { return 0x14; }
        if (name == "NUM_LOCK")  { return 0x90; }
        if (name == "SCROLL_LOCK") { return 0x91; }
        if (name == "F1")  { return 0x70; }
        if (name == "F2")  { return 0x71; }
        if (name == "F3")  { return 0x72; }
        if (name == "F4")  { return 0x73; }
        if (name == "F5")  { return 0x74; }
        if (name == "F6")  { return 0x75; }
        if (name == "F7")  { return 0x76; }
        if (name == "F8")  { return 0x77; }
        if (name == "F9")  { return 0x78; }
        if (name == "F10") { return 0x79; }
        if (name == "F11") { return 0x7A; }
        if (name == "F12") { return 0x7B; }
        return 0;
    }

    private static void typeText(string text) {
        for (int i = 0; i < text.length; i = i + 1) {
            string one = "";
            one += text[i];
            string ch = one;
            int vk = keyNameToVk(ch);
            if (vk == 0) {
                int scan = VkKeyScanA(ch);
                if (scan != -1) {
                    vk = scan & 0xFF;
                } else {
                    vk = 0;
                }
            }
            if (vk != 0) {
                keybd_event(vk, 0, 0, 0);    // down
                keybd_event(vk, 0, 2, 0);    // up
            }
        }
    }

    private static string envGet(string key) {
        // The compiler lowers fs.getenv(name) to a native GetEnvironmentVariableA
        // call that returns "" when the variable is unset. Building the buffer by
        // hand (char buf[size] / new char[size]) is not reliably compiled, so rely
        // on the builtin instead.
        return fs.getenv(key);
    }

    private static Value stringArrayToValue(string[] items) {
        Value[] result = [];
        foreach (string s in items) { result.add(makeString(s)); }
        return makeArray(result);
    }
}

Value callBuiltin(string name, Value[] args) {
    if (name == "printline") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            printline(args[0].toString());
        } else {
            printline("");
        }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "print") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            print(args[0].toString());
        }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "input") {
        string inp = input();
        return makeString(inp);
    }
    if (name == "assert") {
        if (args.length > 0 && !args[0].isTruthy()) {
            printline("Assertion failed!");
            exit(1);
        }
        return makeNull();
    }
    // DOCS/247 -- nativeToastShow is a synthetic bridge (silverc-rs emits real
    // Win32 DLL calls for it). The interpreter cannot pop a tray balloon, so it
    // is a no-op: notifications.sr `show` already printed the NOTIFY: line.
    if (name == "nativeToastShow") {
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "sleep") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            Sleep(args[0].toInt());
        }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "exit") {
        int code = 0;
        if (args.length > 0) { code = args[0].toInt().toInt(); }
        exit(code);
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "int") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            if (args[0].tag == VAL_STRING) { return makeInt(args[0].strVal.toInt64()); }
            return makeInt(args[0].toInt());
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "float") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            if (args[0].tag == VAL_STRING) { return makeFloat(args[0].strVal.toFloat64()); }
            return makeFloat(args[0].toFloat());
        }
        return makeFloat(0.0);
    }
    if (name == "byte") {
        if (args.length == 0) { return makeByte(0); }
        int64 n = args[0].toInt();
        if (n < 0 || n > 255) {
            printline("Runtime error: byte value out of range (0..255)");
            exit(1);
        }
        return makeByte(n);
    }
    if (name == "char") {
        if (args.length == 0) { return makeChar(char(0)); }
        return makeChar(char(args[0].toInt()));
    }
    if (name == "string_cast") {
        if (args.length > 0) { return makeString(args[0].toString()); }
        return makeString("");
    }
    if (name == "now") {
        return makeString(now());
    }
    if (name == "console_set_title") {
        string title = "";
        if (args.length > 0) { title = args[0].toString(); }
        print("\x1b]0;" + title + "\x07");
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "console_clear") {
        print("\x1b[2J\x1b[H");
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "getFocusedProcessDataNative") {
        return makeString(getFocusedProcessDataNative());
    }
    // DOCS/251 -- the interpreter executable is itself native-compiled, so
    // these calls are lowered by silverc-rs to the version.dll bridge while
    // the evaluator exposes them as ordinary builtin values to Silver code.
    if (name == "__binaryMetadataStatus") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            return makeInt(__binaryMetadataStatusNative(args[0].strVal));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "__binaryMetadataField") {
        if (args.length > 1) {
            return makeString(__binaryMetadataFieldNative(args[0].strVal, args[1].strVal));
        }
        return makeString("");
    }
    if (name == "keybd_event" || name == "mouse_event") {
        // Layout into RCX/RDX/R8/R9 and let the Silverc-rs user32 dispatcher
        // emit the Win32 shadow call. The interpreter itself is compiled to
        // native (silvery.exe), so this call resolves to user32!keybd_event
        // / user32!mouse_event at runtime -- no special interpreter support
        // required beyond passing the args through.
        if (args.length >= 4) {
            int p0 = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            int p1 = args[1].toInt().toInt();
            int p2 = args[2].toInt().toInt();
            int p3 = args[3].toInt().toInt();
            if (name == "keybd_event") {
                keybd_event(p0, p1, p2, p3);
            } else {
                mouse_event(p0, p1, p2, p3, 0);
            }
        } else if (args.length > 0 && name == "keybd_event") {
            keybd_event(args[0].toInt().toInt(), 0, 0, 0);
        }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "VkKeyScanA") {
        if (args.length > 0) {
            string s = args[0].strVal;
            return makeInt(VkKeyScanA(s));
        }
        return makeInt(-1);
    }
    if (name == "GetKeyState") {
        if (args.length > 0) { return makeInt(GetKeyState(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetAsyncKeyState") {
        if (args.length > 0) { return makeInt(GetAsyncKeyState(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "MapVirtualKeyA") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            int a0 = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            int a1 = args[1].toInt().toInt();
            return makeInt(MapVirtualKeyA(a0, a1));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetCursorX") {
        return makeInt(GetCursorX());
    }
    if (name == "GetCursorY") {
        return makeInt(GetCursorY());
    }
    if (name == "GetCursorPos") {
        // The interpreter cannot pass a managed Value[] as LPPOINT.  Read the
        // coordinates through the native scalar helpers and write them into
        // the caller's two-element Value array instead.
        if (args.length >= 1 && args[0].tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
            Value target = args[0];
            if (target.arrVal.length >= 2) {
                target.arrVal[0] = makeInt(GetCursorX());
                target.arrVal[1] = makeInt(GetCursorY());
                return makeBool(true);
            }
            return makeBool(false);
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "SetCursorPos") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            return makeBool(SetCursorPos(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "SetCursor") {
        // HCURSOR: accept an int (cursor handle id) and forward. Returns the
        // prior cursor handle so callers can restore it after a temporary
        // change.
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(SetCursor(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "ClipCursor") {
        // The user supplies a 16-byte RECT by reference (silver pointer). We
        // forward raw through to Win32; passing 0 disables clipping.
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            int refAddr = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            return makeInt(ClipCursor(refAddr));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetClipCursor") {
        // Caller passes an int that already points at a 16-byte scratch
        // buffer; we mirror Win32's behaviour of accepting a pointer arg.
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            int refAddr = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            return makeInt(GetClipCursor(refAddr));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetDoubleClickTime") {
        return makeInt(GetDoubleClickTime());
    }
    if (name == "SetDoubleClickTime") {
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            return makeBool(SetDoubleClickTime(args[0].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "RegisterHotKey") {
        // RegisterHotKey(HWND, int id, UINT fsModifiers, UINT vk)
        if (args.length >= 4) {
            return makeBool(RegisterHotKey(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt(),
                args[3].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "UnregisterHotKey") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            return makeBool(UnregisterHotKey(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "MessageBeep") {
        if (args.length >= 1) {
            return makeBool(MessageBeep(args[0].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "RegisterRawInputDevices") {
        // RegisterRawInputDevices(PCRAWINPUTDEVICE[], UINT nDevices, UINT cbSize)
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            return makeBool(RegisterRawInputDevices(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    if (name == "GetRawInputData") {
        // GetRawInputData(HRAWINPUT, UINT uiCommand, void* pData, int* pcbSize, UINT cbSizeHeader)
        if (args.length >= 5) {
            return makeInt(GetRawInputData(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt(),
                args[3].toInt().toInt(),
                args[4].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetRawInputDeviceList") {
        // GetRawInputDeviceList(PRAWINPUTDEVICELIST* pRawInputDeviceList, PUINT puiNumDevices, UINT cbSize)
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            return makeInt(GetRawInputDeviceList(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetForegroundWindow") {
        return makeInt(GetForegroundWindow());
    }
    if (name == "GetWindowTextA") {
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            return makeInt(GetWindowTextA(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt(), args[2].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "GetWindowThreadProcessId") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            return makeInt(GetWindowThreadProcessId(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "OpenProcess") {
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            return makeInt(OpenProcess(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt(), args[2].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "QueryFullProcessImageNameA") {
        if (args.length >= 4) {
            return makeInt(QueryFullProcessImageNameA(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt(), args[2].toInt().toInt(), args[3].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "CloseHandle") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeBool(CloseHandle(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeBool(false);
    }
    // DOCS/248 -- window GUI Win32 forwarding. The self-hosted compiler
    // (silverc.exe) compiles these bare calls to the matching user32.dll /
    // kernel32.dll imports; the interpreter just passes the managed args
    // through in the documented Win64 order.
    if (name == "GetModuleHandleA") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(GetModuleHandleA(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(GetModuleHandleA(0));
    }
    if (name == "GetSystemMetrics") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(GetSystemMetrics(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "ShowWindow") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            return makeInt(ShowWindow(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "UpdateWindow") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(UpdateWindow(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "SetWindowTextA") {
        if (args.length >= 2) {
            return makeInt(SetWindowTextA(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toString()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "DestroyWindow") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(DestroyWindow(args[0].toInt().toInt())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "CreateWindowExA") {
        if (args.length >= 12) {
            int cw0 = args[0].toInt().toInt();
            string cw1 = args[1].toString();
            string cw2 = args[2].toString();
            int cw3 = args[3].toInt().toInt();
            int cw4 = args[4].toInt().toInt();
            int cw5 = args[5].toInt().toInt();
            int cw6 = args[6].toInt().toInt();
            int cw7 = args[7].toInt().toInt();
            int cw8 = args[8].toInt().toInt();
            int cw9 = args[9].toInt().toInt();
            int cw10 = args[10].toInt().toInt();
            int cw11 = args[11].toInt().toInt();
            return makeInt(CreateWindowExA(cw0, cw1, cw2, cw3, cw4, cw5, cw6, cw7, cw8, cw9, cw10, cw11));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "RegisterClassExA") {
        // The interpreter cannot build a native WNDCLASSEXA struct; the
        // Window class in silverc/std/gui.sr uses the pre-registered "STATIC"
        // class instead, so this stub is only present for API symmetry.
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    // DOCS/249 -- custom .ico asset loading + window icon (WM_SETICON).
    // Forward to user32!LoadImageA / SendMessageA through the native bridge.
    if (name == "LoadImageA") {
        if (args.length >= 6) {
            return makeInt(LoadImageA(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toString(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt(),
                args[3].toInt().toInt(),
                args[4].toInt().toInt(),
                args[5].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "SendMessageA") {
        if (args.length >= 4) {
            return makeInt(SendMessageA(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt(),
                args[3].toInt().toInt()));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    // DOCS/249 -- system tray icon bridges. Forward to the native bridge
    // (compiled by silverc.exe), which is a no-op returning 0 in this
    // backend; silverc-rs emits the real Shell_NotifyIconA path.
    if (name == "trayAdd") {
        if (args.length >= 2) { return makeInt(trayAdd(args[0].toString(), args[1].toString())); }
        if (args.length >= 1) { return makeInt(trayAdd(args[0].toString())); }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "traySetTooltip") {
        if (args.length >= 2) { traySetTooltip(args[0].toInt().toInt(), args[1].toString()); }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "trayDelete") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { trayDelete(args[0].toInt().toInt()); }
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "trayRemove") {
        if (args.length >= 1) { trayRemove(args[0].toInt().toInt()); }
        return makeNull();
    }
    // DOCS/249 -- tray click / context-menu bridges. No-op in the
    // interpreter backend (the native WndProc + popup menu lives in
    // silverc-rs); pollMenu/pollClick always report "no event".
    if (name == "traySetMenu") {
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "trayRunLoop") {
        return makeNull();
    }
    if (name == "trayPollMenu") {
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "trayPollClick") {
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    // DOCS/250 -- Windows registry bridges. silverc-rs emits the real
    // advapi32 Reg* sequence for these; the interpreter backend is a no-op
    // (int bridges return 0, string bridges return ""). The RegistryKey class
    // in silverc/std/registry.sr links and runs without native registry access.
    if (name == "regOpen" || name == "regCreate" || name == "regClose" || name == "regSubKey" || name == "regSetString" || name == "regGetInt" || name == "regSetInt" || name == "regGetInt64" || name == "regSetInt64" || name == "regDeleteValue" || name == "regDeleteKey" || name == "regValueExists" || name == "regSubKeyCount" || name == "regValueCount") {
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    if (name == "regGetString" || name == "regEnumSubKeyName" || name == "regEnumValueName") {
        return makeString("");
    }
    if (name == "SendInput") {
        // Raw SendInput wrapper: callers pass the address of an INPUT array,
        // count, and sizeof(INPUT). The native compiler forwards all three
        // values to user32!SendInput; unlike the old placeholder this returns
        // the actual number of events inserted.
        if (args.length >= 3) {
            return makeInt(SendInput(
                args[0].toInt().toInt(),
                args[1].toInt().toInt(),
                args[2].toInt().toInt()
            ));
        }
        return makeInt(0);
    }
    return makeNull();
}

// math.eval(expression) — restricted integer expression evaluator. Supports
// integer literals, parentheses, unary +/- and + - * / % == != < <= > >=
// with standard precedence. It never executes Silver statements or code.
// Division by zero surfaces the native divide-by-zero exception; malformed
// expressions throw 1.
class MathEvalParser {
    string src;
    int pos;

    construct(string s) {
        this.src = s;
        this.pos = 0;
    }

    void skipWs() {
        while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
            char c = this.src[this.pos];
            if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r' || c == '\n') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    char peek() {
        if (this.pos >= this.src.length) { return ' '; }
        return this.src[this.pos];
    }

    int parseAll() {
        this.skipWs();
        int result = this.parseCompare();
        this.skipWs();
        if (this.pos != this.src.length) { throw 1; }
        return result;
    }

    int parseCompare() {
        int left = this.parseAdd();
        while (true) {
            this.skipWs();
            char c = this.peek();
            if (c == '=' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '=') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 2;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left == right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } elif (c == '!' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '=') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 2;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left != right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } elif (c == '<' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '=') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 2;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left <= right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } elif (c == '>' && this.pos + 1 < this.src.length && this.src[this.pos + 1] == '=') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 2;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left >= right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } elif (c == '<') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left < right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } elif (c == '>') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseAdd();
                if (left > right) { left = 1; } else { left = 0; }
            } else {
                return left;
            }
        }
    }

    int parseAdd() {
        int left = this.parseMul();
        while (true) {
            this.skipWs();
            char c = this.peek();
            if (c == '+') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseMul();
                left = left + right;
            } elif (c == '-') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseMul();
                left = left - right;
            } else {
                return left;
            }
        }
    }

    int parseMul() {
        int left = this.parseUnary();
        while (true) {
            this.skipWs();
            char c = this.peek();
            if (c == '*') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseUnary();
                left = left * right;
            } elif (c == '/') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseUnary();
                left = left / right;
            } elif (c == '%') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                int right = this.parseUnary();
                left = left % right;
            } else {
                return left;
            }
        }
    }

    int parseUnary() {
        this.skipWs();
        char c = this.peek();
        if (c == '-') {
            this.pos = this.pos + 1;
            return 0 - this.parseUnary();
        }
        if (c == '+') {
            this.pos = this.pos + 1;
            return this.parseUnary();
        }
        return this.parsePrimary();
    }

    int parsePrimary() {
        this.skipWs();
        char c = this.peek();
        if (c == '(') {
            this.pos = this.pos + 1;
            int value = this.parseCompare();
            this.skipWs();
            if (this.peek() == ')') {
                this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                return value;
            }
            throw 1;
        }
        if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
            int value = 0;
            while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                char d = this.src[this.pos];
                if (d >= '0' && d <= '9') {
                    value = value * 10 + (d - '0');
                    this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                } else {
                    break;
                }
            }
            return value;
        }
        throw 1;
    }
}









class ReturnSignal {
    public prop Value val;
    construct () { this.val = null; }
    construct (Value v) { this.val = v; }
}

class Evaluator {
    public prop Env globals;
    public prop dict<string, Node> functions;
    public prop dict<string, Node> structs;
    public prop dict<string, Node> classes;
    public prop dict<string, string> enums;
    public prop dict<string, string> typeAliases;
    public prop dict<string, Node> modules;
    public prop bool hasReturned;
    public prop bool hasBroken;
    public prop bool hasContinued;
    public prop bool hasThrown;
    public prop Value returnValue;
    public prop Value thrownValue;
    public prop int loopTicks;
    public prop string currentClassName;
    // DOCS/213 -- win32 module grants collected from import statements.
    public prop string[] win32Dlls;
    public prop string[] win32Grants;

    construct () {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TE0_ctor.txt", [49]);
        this.globals = GC.acquireEnv(null);
        this.functions = {};
        this.structs = {};
        this.classes = {};
        this.enums = {};
        this.typeAliases = {};
        this.modules = {};
        this.hasReturned = false;
        this.hasBroken = false;
        this.hasContinued = false;
        this.hasThrown = false;
        this.returnValue = null;
        this.thrownValue = null;
        this.loopTicks = 0;
        this.currentClassName = "";
        this.win32Dlls = [];
        this.win32Grants = [];
        this.registerBuiltins();
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TE1_ctor_end.txt", [49]);
    }

    private void maybeGC(Env env) {
        // Cooperative interpreter scheduling: intentional infinite loops stay
        // infinite, but after a bounded iteration count they yield 1 ms so they
        // cannot monopolize a CPU core. The counter is per evaluator and reset
        // explicitly (compound assignment on object fields is not safe here).
        this.loopTicks = this.loopTicks + 1;
        if (this.loopTicks >= 256) {
            this.loopTicks = 0;
            Sleep(1);
        }
        if (GC.shouldCollect()) {
            if (this.returnValue != null) {
                GC.pushRoot(this.returnValue);
                GC.collectGarbage(env, this.globals);
                GC.popRoot();
            } else {
                GC.collectGarbage(env, this.globals);
            }
        }
    }

    private void registerBuiltins() {
    Value v = makeBuiltin("printline");
    this.globals.define("printline", v);
    this.globals.define("print", makeBuiltin("print"));
        this.globals.define("input", makeBuiltin("input"));
        this.globals.define("assert", makeBuiltin("assert"));
        this.globals.define("sleep", makeBuiltin("sleep"));
        this.globals.define("exit", makeBuiltin("exit"));
        this.globals.define("end", makeBuiltin("exit"));
        this.globals.define("int", makeBuiltin("int"));
        this.globals.define("float", makeBuiltin("float"));
        this.globals.define("byte", makeBuiltin("byte"));
        this.globals.define("char", makeBuiltin("char"));
        this.globals.define("string", makeBuiltin("string_cast"));
        this.globals.define("now", makeBuiltin("now"));
        this.globals.define("time", makeBuiltin("time_module"));
        this.globals.define("url", makeBuiltin("url_module"));
        this.globals.define("hash", makeBuiltin("hash_module"));
        this.globals.define("path", makeBuiltin("path_module"));
        this.globals.define("fs", makeBuiltin("fs_module"));
        this.globals.define("file", makeBuiltin("file_module"));
        this.globals.define("folder", makeBuiltin("folder_module"));
        this.globals.define("env", makeBuiltin("env_module"));
        this.globals.define("bom", makeBuiltin("bom_module"));
        this.globals.define("process", makeBuiltin("process_module"));
        this.globals.define("Process", makeBuiltin("process_class"));
        this.globals.define("__getFocusedProcessData", makeBuiltin("__getFocusedProcessData"));
        // DOCS/251 -- native bridges used by std/binary_metadata.sr. They
        // remain private implementation details; the public API is
        // BinaryMetadata.getMetadata(path).
        this.globals.define("__binaryMetadataStatus", makeBuiltin("__binaryMetadataStatus"));
        this.globals.define("__binaryMetadataField", makeBuiltin("__binaryMetadataField"));
        this.globals.define("llmai", makeBuiltin("llmai_module"));
        this.globals.define("keyboard", makeBuiltin("keyboard_module"));
        this.globals.define("mouse", makeBuiltin("mouse_module"));
        this.globals.define("console", makeBuiltin("console_module"));
        this.globals.define("math", makeBuiltin("math_module"));
        this.globals.define("notifications", makeBuiltin("notifications_module"));
        // DOCS/247 — nativeToastShow: silverc-rs emits real Win32 DLL calls for
        // it; the interpreter simulates the side effect by returning null (the
        // notifications.sr `show` function already printed the NOTIFY: line).
        this.globals.define("nativeToastShow", makeBuiltin("nativeToastShow"));

        // DOCS/213 -- raw Win32 free identifiers (keybd_event, CloseHandle,
        // GetModuleHandleA, ...) are NO LONGER predefined globals. They become
        // available only when the script imports the owning module:
        //   import * from win32.user32;   /   import kernel32 from win32;
        // See registerWin32Import / defineGrantedExports below.

        // DOCS/249 -- system tray icon bridges. The self-hosted compiler
        // links these as no-ops (returning 0), so the TrayIcon class runs
        // without a native tray shell; silverc-rs emits the real Win32 path.
        this.globals.define("trayAdd", makeBuiltin("trayAdd"));
        this.globals.define("traySetTooltip", makeBuiltin("traySetTooltip"));
        this.globals.define("trayDelete", makeBuiltin("trayDelete"));
        this.globals.define("trayRemove", makeBuiltin("trayRemove"));
        this.globals.define("traySetMenu", makeBuiltin("traySetMenu"));
        this.globals.define("trayRunLoop", makeBuiltin("trayRunLoop"));
        this.globals.define("trayPollMenu", makeBuiltin("trayPollMenu"));
        this.globals.define("trayPollClick", makeBuiltin("trayPollClick"));
        // DOCS/250 -- Windows registry bridges. No-op in the interpreter
        // backend (see builtins.sr callBuiltin); silverc-rs emits the real
        // advapi32 Reg* sequence. Registered so the RegistryKey class in
        // silverc/std/registry.sr resolves its bare reg* calls.
        this.globals.define("regOpen", makeBuiltin("regOpen"));
        this.globals.define("regCreate", makeBuiltin("regCreate"));
        this.globals.define("regClose", makeBuiltin("regClose"));
        this.globals.define("regSubKey", makeBuiltin("regSubKey"));
        this.globals.define("regGetString", makeBuiltin("regGetString"));
        this.globals.define("regSetString", makeBuiltin("regSetString"));
        this.globals.define("regGetInt", makeBuiltin("regGetInt"));
        this.globals.define("regSetInt", makeBuiltin("regSetInt"));
        this.globals.define("regGetInt64", makeBuiltin("regGetInt64"));
        this.globals.define("regSetInt64", makeBuiltin("regSetInt64"));
        this.globals.define("regDeleteValue", makeBuiltin("regDeleteValue"));
        this.globals.define("regDeleteKey", makeBuiltin("regDeleteKey"));
        this.globals.define("regValueExists", makeBuiltin("regValueExists"));
        this.globals.define("regEnumSubKeyName", makeBuiltin("regEnumSubKeyName"));
        this.globals.define("regEnumValueName", makeBuiltin("regEnumValueName"));
        this.globals.define("regSubKeyCount", makeBuiltin("regSubKeyCount"));
        this.globals.define("regValueCount", makeBuiltin("regValueCount"));
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // DOCS/213 -- win32 module system. Raw Win32 free identifiers become
    // available only after the script imports the owning DLL module.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    // The raw Win32 exports the interpreter's callBuiltin can execute,
    // mapped to their owning DLL.
    public string win32DllForFn(string fn) {
        if (fn == "keybd_event" || fn == "mouse_event" || fn == "VkKeyScanA" || fn == "GetKeyState" || fn == "GetAsyncKeyState" || fn == "MapVirtualKeyA" || fn == "GetCursorPos" || fn == "SetCursorPos" || fn == "SendInput" || fn == "SetCursor" || fn == "ClipCursor" || fn == "GetClipCursor" || fn == "GetDoubleClickTime" || fn == "SetDoubleClickTime" || fn == "RegisterHotKey" || fn == "UnregisterHotKey" || fn == "MessageBeep" || fn == "RegisterRawInputDevices" || fn == "GetRawInputData" || fn == "GetRawInputDeviceList") { return "user32.dll"; }
        if (fn == "GetForegroundWindow" || fn == "GetWindowTextA" || fn == "GetWindowThreadProcessId" || fn == "RegisterClassExA" || fn == "CreateWindowExA" || fn == "ShowWindow" || fn == "UpdateWindow" || fn == "SetWindowTextA" || fn == "DestroyWindow" || fn == "GetSystemMetrics" || fn == "LoadImageA" || fn == "SendMessageA") { return "user32.dll"; }
        if (fn == "OpenProcess" || fn == "QueryFullProcessImageNameA" || fn == "CloseHandle" || fn == "GetModuleHandleA") { return "kernel32.dll"; }
        return "";
    }

    public void win32AddDll(string dll) {
        for (int i = 0; i < this.win32Dlls.length; i++) {
            if (this.win32Dlls[i] == dll) { return; }
        }
        this.win32Dlls.add(dll);
    }

    public void win32AddGrant(string dll, string fn) {
        string grant = dll + "|" + fn;
        for (int i = 0; i < this.win32Grants.length; i++) {
            if (this.win32Grants[i] == grant) { return; }
        }
        this.win32Grants.add(grant);
    }

    public bool win32HasExport(string dll, string fn) {
        if (this.win32DllForFn(fn) != dll) { return false; }
        for (int i = 0; i < this.win32Dlls.length; i++) {
            if (this.win32Dlls[i] == dll) { return true; }
        }
        string grant = dll + "|" + fn;
        for (int i = 0; i < this.win32Grants.length; i++) {
            if (this.win32Grants[i] == grant) { return true; }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public string win32ModuleDll(string module) {
        if (module == "kernel32") { return "kernel32.dll"; }
        if (module == "ws2_32") { return "ws2_32.dll"; }
        if (module == "winhttp") { return "winhttp.dll"; }
        if (module == "wininet") { return "wininet.dll"; }
        if (module == "shell32") { return "shell32.dll"; }
        if (module == "msvcrt") { return "msvcrt.dll"; }
        if (module == "user32") { return "user32.dll"; }
        if (module == "registry" || module == "advapi32") { return "advapi32.dll"; }
        if (module == "version") { return "version.dll"; }
        return "";
    }

    public void importWin32Module(string module) {
        string dll = this.win32ModuleDll(module);
        if (dll.length == 0) { return; }
        this.win32AddDll(dll);
        this.defineGrantedExports(dll);
    }

    public string evalSliceStr(string s, int start, int end) {
        string r = "";
        for (int i = start; i < end; i++) { r += s[i]; }
        return r;
    }

    public void defineGrantedExports(string dll) {
        if (dll == "user32.dll") {
            string[] fns = [];
            fns.add("keybd_event");
            fns.add("mouse_event");
            fns.add("VkKeyScanA");
            fns.add("GetKeyState");
            fns.add("GetAsyncKeyState");
            fns.add("MapVirtualKeyA");
            fns.add("GetCursorPos");
            fns.add("SetCursorPos");
            fns.add("SendInput");
            fns.add("SetCursor");
            fns.add("ClipCursor");
            fns.add("GetClipCursor");
            fns.add("GetDoubleClickTime");
            fns.add("SetDoubleClickTime");
            fns.add("RegisterHotKey");
            fns.add("UnregisterHotKey");
            fns.add("MessageBeep");
            fns.add("RegisterRawInputDevices");
            fns.add("GetRawInputData");
            fns.add("GetRawInputDeviceList");
            fns.add("GetForegroundWindow");
            fns.add("GetWindowTextA");
            fns.add("GetWindowThreadProcessId");
            fns.add("RegisterClassExA");
            fns.add("CreateWindowExA");
            fns.add("ShowWindow");
            fns.add("UpdateWindow");
            fns.add("SetWindowTextA");
            fns.add("DestroyWindow");
            fns.add("GetSystemMetrics");
            fns.add("LoadImageA");
            fns.add("SendMessageA");
            for (int i = 0; i < fns.length; i++) {
                if (this.win32HasExport(dll, fns[i])) {
                    this.globals.define(fns[i], makeBuiltin(fns[i]));
                }
            }
            return;
        }
        if (dll == "kernel32.dll") {
            string[] k32 = [];
            k32.add("OpenProcess");
            k32.add("QueryFullProcessImageNameA");
            k32.add("CloseHandle");
            k32.add("GetModuleHandleA");
            for (int j = 0; j < k32.length; j++) {
                if (this.win32HasExport(dll, k32[j])) {
                    this.globals.define(k32[j], makeBuiltin(k32[j]));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Register one parsed `import ... [from ...];` statement and define the
    // matching builtin globals.
    public void registerWin32Import(Node node) {
        string source = node.strVal;
        bool all = node.name == "*";
        if (source != "win32" && source.length > 6) {
            if (this.evalSliceStr(source, 0, 6) != "win32.") { return; }
        } elif (source != "win32") {
            return;
        }
        if (source == "win32") {
            if (!all) {
                int start = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i <= node.name.length; i++) {
                    if (i == node.name.length || node.name[i] == ',') {
                        if (i > start) { this.importWin32Module(this.evalSliceStr(node.name, start, i)); }
                        start = i + 1;
                    }
                }
                return;
            }
            this.importWin32Module("kernel32");
            this.importWin32Module("user32");
            this.importWin32Module("msvcrt");
            this.importWin32Module("ws2_32");
            this.importWin32Module("wininet");
            this.importWin32Module("winhttp");
            this.importWin32Module("shell32");
            this.importWin32Module("registry");
            this.importWin32Module("version");
            return;
        }
        string module = this.evalSliceStr(source, 6, source.length);
        string dll = this.win32ModuleDll(module);
        if (dll.length == 0) { return; }
        if (all) {
            this.win32AddDll(dll);
        } else {
            this.win32AddGrant(dll, node.name);
            int start2 = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i <= node.name.length; i++) {
                if (i == node.name.length || node.name[i] == ',') {
                    if (i > start2) { this.win32AddGrant(dll, this.evalSliceStr(node.name, start2, i)); }
                    start2 = i + 1;
                }
            }
        }
        this.defineGrantedExports(dll);
    }

    public void run(Node[] program) {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TR0.txt", [49]);
        this.registerItems(program);
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TR1.txt", [49]);
        this.runMain(program);
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TR2.txt", [49]);
    }

    public void register(Node[] program) {
        this.registerItems(program);
    }

    private void registerItems(Node[] program) {
        foreach (Node item in program) {
            this.registerItem(item);
        }
    }

    private void registerItem(Node item) {
        if (item.kind == ND_IMPORT) {
            // DOCS/213 -- grant access to the imported win32 DLL modules.
            this.registerWin32Import(item);
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_FUNC) {
            this.functions[item.name] = item;
            if (item.isPublic) {
                this.globals.define(item.name, makeFunc(item.name));
            }
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_STRUCT_DEF) {
            this.structs[item.name] = item;
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_CLASS_DEF) {
            this.classes[item.name] = item;
            for (int i = 0; i < item.childCount(); i++) {
                Node child = item.child(i);
                if (child.kind == ND_FUNC) {
                    this.functions[item.name + "." + child.name] = child;
                }
            }
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_ENUM_DEF) {
            this.enums[item.name] = item.strVal;
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_MODULE) {
            this.modules[item.name] = item;
        }
        if (item.kind == ND_CONST && item.childCount() > 0) {
            Value val = this.evalExpr(item.child(0), this.globals);
            this.globals.define(item.name, val);
        }
    }

    private void runMain(Node[] program) {
        for (int idx = 0; idx < program.length; idx++) {
            Node item = program[idx];
            if (item.kind == ND_FUNC && item.name != "main" && item.childCount() >= 2) {
                Env fnEnv = this.globals.push();
                Node params = item.child(0);
                for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
                    fnEnv.define(params.child(i).name, makeNull());
                }
            }
            if (item.kind == ND_VAR_DECL) {
                if (item.childCount() > 0) {
                    Value val = this.evalExpr(item.child(0), this.globals);
                    this.globals.define(item.name, val);
                } else {
                    this.globals.define(item.name, makeNull());
                }
            }
            if (item.kind == ND_EXPR_STMT && item.childCount() > 0) {
            this.evalExpr(item.child(0), this.globals);
            }
        }
        if (this.functions.contains("main")) {
            file.writeBytes("build/diag/TR3.txt", [49]);
            this.callFunction("main", [], this.globals);
        }
        if (this.hasThrown) {
            Value thrown = this.thrownValue;
            string className = "Exception";
            string message = "";
            if (thrown != null && thrown.tag == VAL_OBJECT) {
                Value msgField = this.callMethod(thrown, "getProp", [makeString("message")]);
                if (msgField.tag == VAL_STRING) {
                    message = msgField.strVal;
                }
                Value clsField = this.callMethod(thrown, "getProp", [makeString("className")]);
                if (clsField != null && clsField.tag == VAL_STRING) {
                    className = clsField.strVal;
                } else {
                    className = thrown.objVal.className;
                }
            } else if (thrown != null && thrown.tag == VAL_STRING) {
                message = thrown.strVal;
            }
            printline("Unhandled exception: " + className + ": " + message);
            this.hasThrown = false;
            this.thrownValue = null;
            exit(1);
        }
    }

    public Value callFunction(string name, Value[] args, Env callerEnv) {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TF0.txt", [49]);
        if (this.functions.contains(name)) {
            Node fn = this.functions[name];
            return this.callNode(fn, args, callerEnv);
        }
        // Split the null check from the field access: the self-hosted backend
        // does not always short-circuit `x != null && x.tag == ...`, and the
        // compiled interpreter would dereference a null value and crash.
        Value globalFn = this.globals.get(name);
        if (globalFn != null) {
            if (globalFn.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                return callBuiltin(globalFn.funcName, args);
            }
        }
        printline("Runtime error: undefined function '" + name + "'");
        return makeNull();
    }

    public Value callNode(Node fn, Value[] args, Env callerEnv) {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TN0.txt", [49]);
        int rooted = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            GC.pushRoot(args[i]);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        Node params = fn.child(0);
        Env fnEnv = this.globals.push();
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TN1.txt", [49]);
        for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
            Node param = params.child(i);
            if (i < args.length) {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, args[i]);
            } elif (param.childCount() > 0) {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, this.evalExpr(param.child(0), fnEnv));
            } else {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, makeNull());
            }
        }
        Value result = makeNull();
        if (fn.childCount() >= 2) {
            Node body = fn.child(1);
            this.hasReturned = false;
            this.hasBroken = false;
            this.hasContinued = false;
            this.returnValue = null;
            this.execBlock(body, fnEnv);
            if (this.hasReturned && this.returnValue != null) {
                Value ret = this.returnValue;
                this.hasReturned = false;
                this.returnValue = null;
                result = ret;
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) {
            GC.popRoot();
        }
        return result;
    }

    public Value callNodeWithRefs(Node fn, Value[] args, Env callerEnv, string[] refNames) {
        int rooted = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            GC.pushRoot(args[i]);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        Node params = fn.child(0);
        Env fnEnv = this.globals.push();
        for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
            Node param = params.child(i);
            if (i < args.length) {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, args[i]);
            } elif (param.childCount() > 0) {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, this.evalExpr(param.child(0), fnEnv));
            } else {
                fnEnv.define(param.name, makeNull());
            }
        }
        Value result = makeNull();
        if (fn.childCount() >= 2) {
            Node body = fn.child(1);
            this.hasReturned = false;
            this.hasBroken = false;
            this.hasContinued = false;
            this.returnValue = null;
            this.execBlock(body, fnEnv);
            if (this.hasReturned && this.returnValue != null) {
                Value ret = this.returnValue;
                this.hasReturned = false;
                this.returnValue = null;
                result = ret;
            }
        }
        // A reference argument aliases the caller's binding, not merely the
        // Value object that was passed.  Compound assignment rebinds the
        // callee-local name, so copy that final binding back after execution.
        for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
            if (i < refNames.length && refNames[i] != "") {
                Value updated = fnEnv.get(params.child(i).name);
                if (updated != null) { callerEnv.set(refNames[i], updated); }
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) {
            GC.popRoot();
        }
        return result;
    }

    public Value callMethod(Value receiver, string method, Value[] args) {
        return BuiltinMethods.callMethod(this, receiver, method, args);
    }

    public Value evalExpr(Node node, Env env) {
        if (node.kind == ND_INT_LIT) { return makeInt(node.intVal); }
        if (node.kind == ND_FLOAT_LIT) { return makeFloat(node.floatVal); }
        if (node.kind == ND_STRING_LIT) { return makeString(node.strVal); }
        if (node.kind == ND_CHAR_LIT) { return makeChar(node.charVal); }
        if (node.kind == ND_BOOL_LIT) { return makeBool(node.boolVal); }
        if (node.kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { return makeNull(); }

        if (node.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            Value val = env.get(node.name);
            if (val != null) { return val; }
            if (this.enums.contains(node.name)) {
                return makeBuiltin(node.name);
            }
            if (this.structs.contains(node.name)) {
                return makeBuiltin("struct_" + node.name);
            }
            if (this.classes.contains(node.name)) {
                return makeBuiltin("class_" + node.name);
            }
            printline("Runtime error: undefined variable '" + node.name + "' at " + node.line.toString() + ":" + node.col.toString());
            return makeNull();
        }

        if (node.kind == ND_ADDRESS_OF) {
            if (node.childCount() > 0 && node.child(0).kind == ND_IDENT) {
                return makeInt(this.pseudoAddress(node.child(0).name));
            }
            return makeInt(0);
        }

        if (node.kind == ND_BINARY) { return this.evalBinary(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_DICT_LIT) { return this.evalDictLit(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_UNARY) { return this.evalUnary(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_CALL) { return this.evalCall(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_METHOD_CALL) { return this.evalMethodCall(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_INDEX) { return this.evalIndex(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_SLICE) { return this.evalSlice(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_MEMBER) { return this.evalMember(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_TERNARY) {
            Value cond = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
            if (cond.isTruthy()) { return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env); }
            return this.evalExpr(node.child(2), env);
        }
        if (node.kind == ND_NEW) { return this.evalNew(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_ARRAY_LIT) { return this.evalArrayLit(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_TUPLE_LIT) { return this.evalTupleLit(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_STRUCT_LIT) { return this.evalStructLit(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_INTERP) { return this.evalInterp(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_PAREN) { return this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_MATCH) { return this.evalMatch(node, env); }
        if (node.kind == ND_IF_EXPR) {
            Value cond = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
            if (cond.isTruthy()) { return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env); }
            if (node.childCount() >= 3) { return this.evalExpr(node.child(2), env); }
            return makeNull();
        }

        return makeNull();
    }

    private int64 pseudoAddress(string name) {
        int64 result = 5381;
        for (int i = 0; i < name.length; i++) {
            result = result * 33 + name[i];
        }
        return result;
    }

    private Value evalBinary(Node node, Env env) {
        int op = node.op;

        if (op == OP_ASSIGN) {
            Value val = this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
            GC.pushRoot(val);
            Value assignResult = this.assignTo(node.child(0), val, env);
            GC.popRoot();
            return assignResult;
        }
        if (op == OP_MOVE) {
            return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        }
        if (op == OP_ADD_ASSIGN || op == OP_SUB_ASSIGN || op == OP_MUL_ASSIGN || op == OP_DIV_ASSIGN) {
            Value current = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
            GC.pushRoot(current);
            Value rhs = this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
            int baseOp = op;
            if (op == OP_ADD_ASSIGN) { baseOp = OP_ADD; }
            elif (op == OP_SUB_ASSIGN) { baseOp = OP_SUB; }
            elif (op == OP_MUL_ASSIGN) { baseOp = OP_MUL; }
            elif (op == OP_DIV_ASSIGN) { baseOp = OP_DIV; }
            Value result = this.computeOp(current, baseOp, rhs);
            GC.pushRoot(result);
            this.assignTo(node.child(0), result, env);
            GC.popRoot();
            GC.popRoot();
            return result;
        }

        Value lhs = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);

        if (op == OP_COALESCE) {
            if (lhs.tag != VAL_NULL) { return lhs; }
            return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        }

        if (op == OP_AND) {
            if (!lhs.isTruthy()) { return makeBool(false); }
            return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        }
        if (op == OP_OR) {
            if (lhs.isTruthy()) { return lhs; }
            return this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        }

        GC.pushRoot(lhs);
        Value rhs = this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        GC.popRoot();

        if (op == OP_RANGE) {
            int64 start = lhs.toInt();
            int64 end = rhs.toInt();
            Value[] items = [];
            for (int64 i = start; i < end; i += 1) {
                items.add(makeInt(i));
            }
            return makeArray(items);
        }

        if (op == OP_JOIN) {
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                string sep = rhs.strVal;
                string result = "";
                for (int i = 0; i < lhs.arrVal.length; i++) {
                    if (i > 0) { result += sep; }
                    result += lhs.arrVal[i].toString();
                }
                return makeString(result);
            }
        }

        if (op == OP_ADD) {
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_STRING || rhs.tag == VAL_STRING) {
                return makeString(lhs.toString() + rhs.toString());
            }
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_ARRAY && rhs.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                Value[] combined = [];
                foreach (Value v in lhs.arrVal) { combined.add(v); }
                foreach (Value v in rhs.arrVal) { combined.add(v); }
                return makeArray(combined);
            }
        }

        return this.computeOp(lhs, op, rhs);
    }

    private Value computeOp(Value lhs, int op, Value rhs) {
        if (op == OP_ADD) {
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_STRING || rhs.tag == VAL_STRING) {
                return makeString(lhs.toString() + rhs.toString());
            }
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_ARRAY && rhs.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                Value[] combined = [];
                foreach (Value v in lhs.arrVal) { combined.add(v); }
                foreach (Value v in rhs.arrVal) { combined.add(v); }
                return makeArray(combined);
            }
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT || rhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT) {
                return makeFloat(lhs.toFloat() + rhs.toFloat());
            }
            return makeInt(lhs.toInt() + rhs.toInt());
        }
        if (op == OP_SUB) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() - rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_MUL) {
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT || rhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT) {
                return makeFloat(lhs.toFloat() * rhs.toFloat());
            }
            return makeInt(lhs.toInt() * rhs.toInt());
        }
        if (op == OP_DIV) {
            if (lhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT || rhs.tag == VAL_FLOAT) {
                return makeFloat(lhs.toFloat() / rhs.toFloat());
            }
            int64 divisor = rhs.toInt();
            if (divisor == 0) {
                this.throwException("DivideByZeroException", "division by zero");
                return makeInt(0);
            }
            return makeInt(lhs.toInt() / divisor);
        }
        if (op == OP_MOD) {
            int64 divisor = rhs.toInt();
            if (divisor == 0) {
                this.throwException("DivideByZeroException", "division by zero");
                return makeInt(0);
            }
            return makeInt(lhs.toInt() % divisor);
        }
        if (op == OP_EQ) { return makeBool(lhs.equals(rhs)); }
        if (op == OP_NEQ) { return makeBool(!lhs.equals(rhs)); }
        if (op == OP_LT) { return makeBool(lhs.toInt() < rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_LEQ) { return makeBool(lhs.toInt() <= rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_GT) { return makeBool(lhs.toInt() > rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_GEQ) { return makeBool(lhs.toInt() >= rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_SHL) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() << rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_SHR) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() >> rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_BIT_AND) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() & rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_BIT_OR) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() | rhs.toInt()); }
        if (op == OP_BIT_XOR) { return makeInt(lhs.toInt() ^ rhs.toInt()); }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private Value assignTo(Node target, Value val, Env env) {
        if (target.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            if (env.exists(target.name)) {
                env.set(target.name, val);
            } else {
                env.define(target.name, val);
            }
            return val;
        }
        if (target.kind == ND_MEMBER) {
            Value obj = this.evalExpr(target.child(0), env);
            if (obj.tag == VAL_BUILTIN && obj.funcName == "console_module" && target.name == "title") {
                obj.strVal = val.toString();
                Value[] titleArgs = [val];
                callBuiltin("console_set_title", titleArgs);
                return val;
            }
            if (obj.tag == VAL_OBJECT || obj.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) {
                mirrorPut(obj, target.name, val);
                return val;
            }
        }
        if (target.kind == ND_INDEX) {
            Value collection = this.evalExpr(target.child(0), env);
            GC.pushRoot(collection);
            Value idx = this.evalExpr(target.child(1), env);
            GC.popRoot();
            if (collection.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
                int i = idx.toInt().toInt();
                if (i >= 0 && i < collection.arrVal.length) {
                    collection.arrVal[i] = val;
                    GC.writeBarrier(collection, val);
                } else {
                    this.throwException("IndexOutOfRangeException", "index out of range");
                }
                return val;
            }
            if (collection.tag == VAL_DICT || collection.tag == VAL_OBJECT) {
                mirrorPut(collection, idx.strVal, val);
                return val;
            }
        }
        return val;
    }

    private Value evalUnary(Node node, Env env) {
        Value operand = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
        if (node.op == UOP_NEG) {
            if (operand.tag == VAL_FLOAT) { return makeFloat(-operand.floatVal); }
            return makeInt(-operand.toInt());
        }
        if (node.op == UOP_NOT) { return makeBool(!operand.isTruthy()); }
        if (node.op == UOP_REF) { return operand; }
        if (node.op == UOP_DEREF) { return operand; }
        return operand;
    }

    private Value evalCall(Node node, Env env) {
        Value[] args = [];
        string[] refNames = [];
        int rooted = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < node.childCount(); i++) {
            Node argNode = node.child(i);
            Value a = this.evalExpr(argNode, env);
            args.add(a);
            if (argNode.kind == ND_UNARY && argNode.op == UOP_REF && argNode.childCount() > 0 && argNode.child(0).kind == ND_IDENT) {
                refNames.add(argNode.child(0).name);
            } else {
                refNames.add("");
            }
            GC.pushRoot(a);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        Value result = makeNull();
        if (node.name == "super") {
            result = this.evalSuper(args, env);
        } else if (this.functions.contains(node.name)) {
            result = this.callNodeWithRefs(this.functions[node.name], args, env, refNames);
        } else {
        // Split the null check from the field access: the self-hosted backend
        // does not always short-circuit `x != null && x.tag == ...`, and the
        // compiled interpreter would dereference a null value and crash.
        Value globalFn = this.globals.get(node.name);
        if (globalFn != null) {
            if (globalFn.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
                result = callBuiltin(globalFn.funcName, args);
            }
        } else if (this.classes.contains(node.name)) {
            result = this.constructClass(node.name, args);
        } else if (this.structs.contains(node.name)) {
            result = this.constructStruct(node.name, args);
        } else {
            printline("Runtime error: undefined function '" + node.name + "'");
        }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) {
            GC.popRoot();
        }
        return result;
    }

    // super(args...) - call the parent class constructor with the current
    // `this` instance. Only valid inside a class `construct` method.
    private Value evalSuper(Value[] args, Env env) {
        if (this.currentClassName == "") { return makeNull(); }
        if (!this.classes.contains(this.currentClassName)) { return makeNull(); }
        string parent = this.classes[this.currentClassName].parentClass;
        if (parent == "") { return makeNull(); }
        if (!this.classes.contains(parent)) { return makeNull(); }
        Node parentDef = this.classes[parent];
        for (int i = 0; i < parentDef.childCount(); i++) {
            Node child = parentDef.child(i);
            if (child.kind == ND_FUNC && child.name == "construct") {
                Value thisVal = env.get("this");
                if (thisVal != null) {
                    Value[] methodArgs = [thisVal];
                    foreach (Value a in args) { methodArgs.add(a); }
                    string savedClass = this.currentClassName;
                    this.currentClassName = parent;
                    Value result = this.callNode(child, methodArgs, env);
                    this.currentClassName = savedClass;
                    return result;
                }
            }
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private Value evalMethodCall(Node node, Env env) {
        Value receiver = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
        GC.pushRoot(receiver);
        int rooted = 1;
        Value[] args = [];
        for (int i = 1; i < node.childCount(); i++) {
            Value a = this.evalExpr(node.child(i), env);
            args.add(a);
            GC.pushRoot(a);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        Value result = makeNull();
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) {
            string methodKey = this.resolveMethodKey(receiver.typeName, node.name);
            if (this.functions.contains(methodKey)) {
                Value[] methodArgs = [receiver];
                foreach (Value a in args) { methodArgs.add(a); }
                result = this.callNode(this.functions[methodKey], methodArgs, env);
                for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) { GC.popRoot(); }
                return result;
            }
        }
        if (receiver.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
            string cn = receiver.funcName;
            if (cn.startsWith("class_")) {
                string className = cn.substr(6);
                if (className == "Value") {
                    Value factory = this.makeValueFactory(node.name, args);
                    if (factory != null) {
                        result = factory;
                        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) { GC.popRoot(); }
                        return result;
                    }
                }
                string staticKey = className + "." + node.name;
                if (this.functions.contains(staticKey)) {
                    result = this.callNode(this.functions[staticKey], args, env);
                    for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) { GC.popRoot(); }
                    return result;
                }
            }
        }
        result = this.callMethod(receiver, node.name, args);
        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) { GC.popRoot(); }
        return result;
    }

    private Value makeValueFactory(string m, Value[] args) {
        if (m != "makeInt" && m != "makeFloat" && m != "makeBool" && m != "makeString"
            && m != "makeChar" && m != "makeArray" && m != "makeTuple" && m != "makeObject"
            && m != "makeFunc" && m != "makeBuiltin" && m != "makeClassInst"
            && m != "makeEnumVal" && m != "makeDict" && m != "makeRange" && m != "makeNull") {
            return null;
        }
        dict<string, Value> fields = {};
        Value[] fvals = [];
        if (m == "makeInt") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(1));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "intVal", makeInt(args[0].toInt())); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeFloat") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(2));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "floatVal", makeFloat(args[0].toFloat())); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeBool") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(3));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "boolVal", makeBool(args[0].isTruthy())); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeString") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(4));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "strVal", makeString(args[0].strVal)); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeChar") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(5));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "charVal", makeChar(args[0].charVal)); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeArray" || m == "makeTuple") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(m == "makeArray" ? 6 : 7));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "arrVal", args[0]); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeObject" || m == "makeClassInst") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(m == "makeObject" ? 8 : 12));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "objVal", args[0]); }
            if (args.length > 1) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "typeName", makeString(args[1].strVal)); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeFunc" || m == "makeBuiltin") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(m == "makeFunc" ? 9 : 10));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "funcName", makeString(args[0].strVal)); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeEnumVal") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(13));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "typeName", makeString(args[0].strVal)); }
            if (args.length > 1) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "strVal", makeString(args[1].strVal)); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeDict") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(15));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "objVal", args[0]); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeRange") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(14));
            if (args.length > 0) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "intVal", makeInt(args[0].toInt())); }
            if (args.length > 1) { dictSet(fields, fvals, "floatVal", makeFloat(args[1].toFloat())); }
        }
        elif (m == "makeNull") {
            dictSet(fields, fvals, "tag", makeInt(0));
        }
        Value inst = makeClassInst(fields, "Value");
        setObjVals(inst, fvals);
        return inst;
    }

    private Value evalIndex(Node node, Env env) {
        Value collection = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
        GC.pushRoot(collection);
        Value idx = this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env);
        GC.popRoot();
        if (collection.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
            int i = idx.toInt().toInt();
            if (i >= 0 && i < collection.arrVal.length) {
                return collection.arrVal[i];
            }
            this.throwException("IndexOutOfRangeException", "index out of range");
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (collection.tag == VAL_STRING) {
            int i = idx.toInt().toInt();
            if (i >= 0 && i < collection.strVal.length) {
                return makeChar(collection.strVal[i]);
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (collection.tag == VAL_DICT || collection.tag == VAL_OBJECT || collection.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST) {
            if (collection.objVal.contains(idx.strVal)) {
                return collection.objVal[idx.strVal];
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        if (collection.tag == VAL_TUPLE) {
            int i = idx.toInt().toInt();
            if (i >= 0 && i < collection.arrVal.length) {
                return collection.arrVal[i];
            }
            return makeNull();
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private Value evalSlice(Node node, Env env) {
        Value collection = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
        GC.pushRoot(collection);
        int64 start = this.evalExpr(node.child(1), env).toInt();
        int64 end = this.evalExpr(node.child(2), env).toInt();
        GC.popRoot();
        if (collection.tag == VAL_STRING) {
            int s = start.toInt();
            int e = end.toInt();
            if (s < 0) { s = 0; }
            if (e > collection.strVal.length) { e = collection.strVal.length; }
            return makeString(collection.strVal[s..e]);
        }
        if (collection.tag == VAL_ARRAY) {
            int s = start.toInt();
            int e = end.toInt();
            if (s < 0) { s = 0; }
            if (e > collection.arrVal.length) { e = collection.arrVal.length; }
            Value[] result = [];
            for (int i = s; i < e; i += 1) {
                result.add(collection.arrVal[i]);
            }
            return makeArray(result);
        }
        return makeNull();
    }

    private Value evalMember(Node node, Env env) {
        Value obj = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env);
        string member = node.name;

        if (obj.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST || obj.tag == VAL_OBJECT || obj.tag == VAL_STRUCT) {
            if (obj.objVal.contains(member)) {
                return obj.objVal[member];
            }
            if (obj.tag == VAL_CLASS_INST && this.classes.contains(obj.typeName)) {
                string key = this.resolveMethodKey(obj.typeName, member);
                if (key.length > 0) { return makeFunc(key); }
            }
        }
        if (obj.tag == VAL_ENUM_VAL) {
            if (member == obj.strVal) { return obj; }
            return makeEnumVal(obj.typeName, member);
        }
        if (obj.tag == VAL_BUILTIN) {
            if (obj.funcName == "console_module") {
                if (member == "Black") { return makeInt(0); }
                if (member == "DarkBlue") { return makeInt(1); }
                if (member == "DarkGreen") { return makeInt(2); }
                if (member == "DarkCyan") { return makeInt(3); }
                if (member == "DarkRed") { return makeInt(4); }
                if (member == "DarkMagenta") { return makeInt(5); }
                if (member == "DarkYellow") { return makeInt(6); }
                if (member == "Gray") { return makeInt(7); }
                if (member == "DarkGray") { return makeInt(8); }
                if (member == "Blue") { return makeInt(9); }
                if (member == "Green" || member == "BrightGreen") { return makeInt(10); }
                if (member == "Cyan") { return makeInt(11); }
                if (member == "Red" || member == "BrightRed") { return makeInt(12); }
                if (member == "Magenta") { return makeInt(13); }
                if (member == "Yellow") { return makeInt(14); }
                if (member == "White") { return makeInt(15); }
                if (member == "title") { return makeString(obj.strVal); }
            }
            if (obj.funcName == "time_module") {
                if (member == "now") { return makeBuiltin("now"); }
            }
            if (this.enums.contains(obj.funcName)) {
                return makeEnumVal(obj.funcName, member);
            }
        }
        return BuiltinMethods.getProperty(obj, member);
    }

    private Value evalNew(Node node, Env env) {
        Value[] args = [];
        int rooted = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < node.childCount(); i++) {
            Value a = this.evalExpr(node.child(i), env);
            args.add(a);
            GC.pushRoot(a);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        Value result = makeNull();
        if (node.name == "byte" && node.childCount() == 1) {
            int count = this.evalExpr(node.child(0), env).toInt().toInt();
            if (count < 0) {
                printline("Runtime error: byte array size cannot be negative");
                exit(1);
            }
            Value[] bytes = [];
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
                bytes.add(makeByte(0));
            }
            result = makeArray(bytes);
        } elif (this.classes.contains(node.name)) {
            result = this.constructClass(node.name, args);
        } else if (this.structs.contains(node.name)) {
            result = this.constructStruct(node.name, args);
        } else if (node.name == "kvp") {
            result = makeDict({});
        } else {
            printline("Runtime error: unknown type '" + node.name + "'");
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < rooted; i++) {
            GC.popRoot();
        }
        return result;
    }

    private Value constructClass(string name, Value[] args) {
        dict<string, Value> fields = {};
        Value inst = makeClassInst(fields, name);
        GC.pushRoot(inst);
        int rooted = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            GC.pushRoot(args[i]);
            rooted += 1;
        }
        // Collect the inheritance chain leaf -> root (`class Foo : Parent`).
        string[] chain = [];
        string cur = name;
        int guard = 0;
        while (this.classes.contains(cur) && guard < 64) {
            chain.add(cur);
            cur = this.classes[cur].parentClass;
            guard += 1;
        }
        // Initialize fields root -> leaf so inherited fields exist before the
