                return value;
            }
            throw 1;
        }
        if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
            int value = 0;
            while (this.pos < this.src.length) {
                char d = this.src[this.pos];
                if (d >= '0' && d <= '9') {
                    value = value * 10 + (d - '0');
                    this.pos = this.pos + 1;
                } else {
                    break;
                }
            }
            return value;
        }
        throw 1;
    }
}




// Whole-word string equality for command-line arguments. The self-hosted
// stage-2 backend represents strings as pointers, so `a == b` compares
// pointers rather than contents. This helper walks the characters and is
// correct on both backends (mirrors string.cliArgEq; kept here so the cli
// module is self-contained when callers only need arg parsing).
public bool eq(string a, string b) {
    if (a.length != b.length) { return false; }
    for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (a[i] != b[i]) { return false; }
    }
    return true;
}

// Returns true if `arg` matches any of the listed option spellings.
// Useful for accepting short and long forms together, e.g.
// cli.isFlag(arg, ["-h", "--help", "/?"]).
public bool isFlag(string arg, string[] names) {
    for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (eq(arg, names[i])) { return true; }
    }
    return false;
}

// Returns true if `arg` looks like an option (begins with '-' or '/').
// Used to distinguish positional arguments from options while scanning.
public bool isOption(string arg) {
    if (arg.length == 0) { return false; }
    char c = arg[0];
    return c == '-' || c == '/';
}

// Returns true if `arg` begins with `prefix`. Useful for combined short
// options like `-vh`.
public bool hasPrefix(string arg, string prefix) {
    if (arg.length < prefix.length) { return false; }
    for (int i = 0; i < prefix.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (arg[i] != prefix[i]) { return false; }
    }
    return true;
}

// Returns the value following `args[idx]` for a value-taking option, or
// the empty string when no value is present or the next item is itself
// an option. Pair this with the caller's index increment to consume the
// value when it is non-empty.
public string valueAfter(string[] args, int idx) {
    int next = idx + 1;
    if (next >= args.length) { return ""; }
    string nxt = args[next];
    if (isOption(nxt)) { return ""; }
    return nxt;
}

// Returns the array of positional (non-option) arguments from `args`.
// Useful for collecting input/output file operands after a flag scan.
public string[] positional(string[] args) {
    string[] result = [];
    for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (!isOption(args[i])) { result.add(args[i]); }
    }
    return result;
}

public string[] args() << cargs;

public string programName() {
    if (cargs.length > 0) { return cargs[0]; }
    return "";
}




// The llmai client is a source-level standard library in the compiler. The
// interpreter has no source-loader, so we embed the same enum + classes here
// and parse them at startup (see main.sr). The only native piece is
// llmai.chat(url, headers, body), dispatched through the builtin module.
public string source() {
    return "def Compatibility = enum { OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, DeepSeek, Qwen, Zhipu, Moonshot, Baichuan, Doubao, MiniMax, Custom };

class LlmHeaders {
    public prop string contentType;
    public prop string authorization;
    public prop string extraName;
    public prop string extraValue;

    construct(string key, Compatibility compatibility) {
        this.contentType = \"application/json\";
        this.authorization = \"Bearer \" + key;
        this.extraName = \"\";
        this.extraValue = \"\";
    }

    private bool same(string a, string b) {
        if (a.length != b.length) { return false; }
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i = i + 1) {
            char ca = a[i];
            char cb = b[i];
            if (ca >= 'a' && ca <= 'z') { ca = ca - 32; }
            if (cb >= 'a' && cb <= 'z') { cb = cb - 32; }
            if (ca != cb) { return false; }
        }
        return true;
    }

    public bool has(string name) {
        return this.same(name, \"Content-Type\") || this.same(name, \"Authorization\")
            || (this.extraName.length > 0 && this.same(name, this.extraName));
    }

    public string get(string name) {
        if (this.same(name, \"Content-Type\")) { return this.contentType; }
        if (this.same(name, \"Authorization\")) { return this.authorization; }
        if (this.extraName.length > 0 && this.same(name, this.extraName)) { return this.extraValue; }
        return \"\";
    }

    public void set(string name, string value) {
        if (this.same(name, \"Content-Type\")) { this.contentType = value; }
        elif (this.same(name, \"Authorization\")) { this.authorization = value; }
        else { this.extraName = name; this.extraValue = value; }
    }

    public string serialize() {
        string nl = \"\\r\\n\";
        string result = \"Content-Type: \" + this.contentType + nl;
        result = result + \"Authorization: \" + this.authorization + nl;
        if (this.extraName.length > 0) {
            result = result + this.extraName + \": \" + this.extraValue + nl;
        }
        return result;
    }
}

class ChatResponseMessage {
    public prop string content;
    construct() { this.content = \"\"; }
}

class ChatChoice {
    public prop ChatResponseMessage message;
    construct() { this.message = new ChatResponseMessage(); }
}

class ChatCompletionResponse {
    public prop ChatChoice[] choices;
    construct() {
        this.choices = [];
        this.choices.push(new ChatChoice());
    }
}

class ChatCompletionRequest {
    public prop string model;
    public prop string messageRole;
    public prop string messageContent;
    public prop ChatCompletionResponse response;

    construct(string modelName) {
        this.model = modelName;
        this.messageRole = \"\";
        this.messageContent = \"\";
        this.response = new ChatCompletionResponse();
    }

    public void addMessage(string role, string content) {
        this.messageRole = role;
        this.messageContent = content;
    }

    public string serialize() {
        string q = '\"'.toString();
        return \"{\" + q + \"model\" + q + \":\" + q + this.model + q + \",\" + q + \"messages\" + q + \":[{\" + q + \"role\" + q + \":\" + q + this.messageRole + q + \",\" + q + \"content\" + q + \":\" + q + this.messageContent + q + \"}]}\";
    }

    public void updateResponse(string raw) {
        this.response.choices[0].message.content = raw;
    }
}

class ChatCompletions {
    public prop string url;
    public prop LlmHeaders headers;

    construct(string endpoint, LlmHeaders requestHeaders) {
        this.url = endpoint;
        this.headers = requestHeaders;
    }

    public string create(ChatCompletionRequest request) {
        string raw = llmai.chat(this.url, this.headers.serialize(), request.serialize());
        request.updateResponse(raw);
        return raw;
    }
}

class ChatRoot {
    public prop ChatCompletions completions;

    construct(string endpoint, LlmHeaders requestHeaders) {
        this.completions = new ChatCompletions(endpoint, requestHeaders);
    }
}

class LLMClient {
    public prop string url;
    public prop string apiKey;
    public prop Compatibility compatibility;
    public prop LlmHeaders headers;
    public prop ChatRoot chat;

    construct(string endpoint, string key, Compatibility mode) {
        this.url = endpoint;
        this.apiKey = key;
        this.compatibility = mode;
        this.headers = new LlmHeaders(key, mode);
        this.chat = new ChatRoot(endpoint, this.headers);
    }
}
";
}


import * from win32.msvcrt;



// The interpreter has no source-loader, so the exception hierarchy is
// embedded here and parsed at startup (see main.sr), mirroring llmai_std.
// Keep this in sync with silverc-rs/std/exception.sr and
// silverc/std/exception.sr.
//
// NOTE: the accessor must NOT be named `source()`: the self-hosted
// compiler flattens module-level functions to the global namespace by
// bare name, so it would collide with llmai_std.source().
public string exceptionSource() {
    return "
// Silver exception hierarchy.
//
// `throw` raises an instance of one of these classes (or any user class), and
// `try` / `catch (Exception e) { ... }` catches it. Every exception class
// derives from `Exception` in spirit; the native code generator currently
// lowers each class self-containedly, so the shared fields are repeated here
// until class inheritance is fully wired into codegen.
//
// Every exception carries the standard props required by the spec:
// `message`, `line`, `column`, and `reason`, so a handler can report the exact
// line and column of the offending value together with a reason.

class Exception {
    string message;
    int line;
    int column;
    string reason;
    construct(string msg) {
        this.message = msg;
        this.line = 0;
        this.column = 0;
        this.reason = msg;
    }
}

class TypeException {
    string message;
    int line;
    int column;
    string reason;
    construct(string msg) {
        this.message = msg;
        this.line = 0;
        this.column = 0;
        this.reason = msg;
    }
}

class ValueException {
    string message;
    int line;
    int column;
    string reason;
    construct(string msg) {
        this.message = msg;
        this.line = 0;
        this.column = 0;
        this.reason = msg;
    }
}

import * from win32.msvcrt;





bool projEndsWith(string text, string suffix) {
    if (text.length < suffix.length) { return false; }
    int start = text.length - suffix.length;
    for (int i = 0; i < suffix.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (text[start + i] != suffix[i]) { return false; }
    }
    return true;
}

int projFind(string text, string needle, int start) {
    for (int i = start; i + needle.length <= text.length; i = i + 1) {
        bool hit = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < needle.length; j = j + 1) {
            if (text[i + j] != needle[j]) { hit = false; }
        }
        if (hit) { return i; }
    }
    return -1;
}

string projSlice(string text, int start, int end) {
    string result = "";
    for (int i = start; i < end; i = i + 1) {
        result = result + text[i];
    }
    return result;
}

string projDirname(string path) {
    int last = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < path.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (path[i] == '/' || path[i] == '\\') { last = i; }
    }
    if (last < 0) { return "."; }
    return projSlice(path, 0, last);
}

string projJoin(string root, string path) {
    if (root == ".") { return path; }
    if (path.length > 0 && (path[0] == '/' || path[0] == '\\')) {
        return root + path;
    }
    return root + "\\" + path;
}

string projManifestValue(string manifest, string key) {
    int keyPos = projFind(manifest, key, 0);
    if (keyPos < 0) { return ""; }
    int colon = keyPos + key.length;
    while (colon < manifest.length && manifest[colon] != ':') {
        colon = colon + 1;
    }
    if (colon >= manifest.length) { return ""; }
    int quote = colon + 1;
    while (quote < manifest.length && manifest[quote] != '"') {
        quote = quote + 1;
    }
    if (quote >= manifest.length) { return ""; }
    int end = quote + 1;
    while (end < manifest.length && manifest[end] != '"') {
        end = end + 1;
    }
    return projSlice(manifest, quote + 1, end);
}

string[] projManifestList(string manifest, string key) {
    string[] result = [];
    int keyPos = projFind(manifest, key, 0);
    if (keyPos < 0) { return result; }
    int cursor = keyPos;
    while (cursor < manifest.length && manifest[cursor] != '[') {
        cursor = cursor + 1;
    }
    if (cursor >= manifest.length) { return result; }
    cursor = cursor + 1;
    while (cursor < manifest.length && manifest[cursor] != ']') {
        while (cursor < manifest.length && manifest[cursor] != '"' && manifest[cursor] != ']') {
            cursor = cursor + 1;
        }
        if (cursor >= manifest.length || manifest[cursor] == ']') { break; }
        int start = cursor + 1;
        cursor = start;
        while (cursor < manifest.length && manifest[cursor] != '"') {
            cursor = cursor + 1;
        }
        if (cursor <= manifest.length) {
            result.add(projSlice(manifest, start, cursor));
        }
        cursor = cursor + 1;
    }
    return result;
}

bool projIsExcluded(string rel, string[] excludes) {
    for (int i = 0; i < excludes.length; i = i + 1) {
        string pat = excludes[i];
        if (rel.length < pat.length) { continue; }
        bool hit = true;
        for (int j = 0; j < pat.length; j = j + 1) {
            if (pat[j] != rel[j]) {
                if (pat[j] == '/') {
                    if (rel[j] != '\\') { hit = false; }
                } elif (pat[j] == '\\') {
                    if (rel[j] != '/') { hit = false; }
                } else {
                    hit = false;
                }
            }
        }
        if (hit) {
            if (rel.length == pat.length) { return true; }
            char sep = rel[pat.length];
            if (sep == '\\') { return true; }
            if (sep == '/') { return true; }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool projVisited(string[] visited, string path) {
    for (int i = 0; i < visited.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (visited[i] == path) { return true; }
    }
    return false;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// QuickSilver (.qs) library bundles. A `.qs` file is a binary container (see
// DOCS/226): magic "QSLIB2", then fixed 4-byte base-255 big-endian u32
// lengths (each byte holds a digit in 1..=255, digit = byte - 1), then
// payloads. The NUL-free encoding is required because the compiled runtime
// reads files through strlen-based string primitives. Layout: magic | flags |
// meta_len | metadata (5 '\n'-joined lines) | module_count | then per module:
// name_len, filename, src_len, minified source.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

bool qsIsBundle(string content) {
    if (content.length < 6) { return false; }
    return content[0] == 'Q' && content[1] == 'S' && content[2] == 'L'
        && content[3] == 'I' && content[4] == 'B' && content[5] == '2';
}

// Read a fixed 4-byte base-255 big-endian u32 at pos (digit = byte - 1).
int qsU32(string content, int pos) {
    int v = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) {
        v = v * 255 + (content[pos + i] - 1);
    }
    return v;
}

// Extract the minified module sources of a `.qs` bundle, in order. Returns an
// empty array when the content is not a bundle.
string[] qsModuleSources(string content) {
    string[] result = [];
    if (!qsIsBundle(content)) { return result; }
    int pos = 6;
    pos = pos + 4;              // flags (reserved)
    int metaLen = qsU32(content, pos);
    pos = pos + 4 + metaLen;    // metadata payload
    int count = qsU32(content, pos);
    pos = pos + 4;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i = i + 1) {
        int nameLen = qsU32(content, pos);
        pos = pos + 4 + nameLen;
        int srcLen = qsU32(content, pos);
        pos = pos + 4;
        result.add(projSlice(content, pos, pos + srcLen));
        pos = pos + srcLen;
    }
    return result;
}

// The interpreter is compiled by silverc, whose self-hosted stage-2 backend
// has a code-generation edge case when the same mutable array is passed into
// helpers and grown there: the caller's length slot is not refreshed, silently
// truncating growth at 8 elements. These helpers build and return fresh
// arrays (every `.add` targets a local), and the caller dedups into its own
// locals.
string[] projWalk(string root, string relDir, string[] excludes) {
    string[] result = [];
    string dir = root;
    if (relDir.length > 0) { dir = projJoin(root, relDir); }
    string[] names = folder.getFiles(dir);
    for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i = i + 1) {
        string name = names[i];
        string rel = name;
        if (relDir.length > 0) { rel = relDir + "\\" + name; }
        if (!projEndsWith(rel, ".sr")) { continue; }
        if (projIsExcluded(rel, excludes)) { continue; }
        result.add(projJoin(root, rel));
    }
    string[] subdirs = folder.getFolders(dir);
    for (int k = 0; k < subdirs.length; k = k + 1) {
        string subname = subdirs[k];
        string subrel = subname;
        if (relDir.length > 0) { subrel = relDir + "\\" + subname; }
        if (projIsExcluded(subrel, excludes)) { continue; }
        string[] sub = projWalk(root, subrel, excludes);
        for (int m = 0; m < sub.length; m = m + 1) {
            result.add(sub[m]);
        }
    }
    return result;
}

string[] projAddEntry(string root, string entry, string[] excludes) {
    string[] result = [];
    if (projEndsWith(entry, ".sr")) {
        if (projIsExcluded(entry, excludes)) { return result; }
        result.add(projJoin(root, entry));
        return result;
    }
    return projWalk(root, entry, excludes);
}

string projTrim(string text) {
    int start = 0;
    int end = text.length;
    while (start < end && (text[start] == ' ' || text[start] == '\t' || text[start] == '\r' || text[start] == '\n')) { start = start + 1; }
    while (end > start && (text[end - 1] == ' ' || text[end - 1] == '\t' || text[end - 1] == '\r' || text[end - 1] == '\n')) { end = end - 1; }
    return projSlice(text, start, end);
}

string projCollectionEscape(string value) {
    string result = "";
    for (int i = 0; i < value.length; i = i + 1) {
        char c = value[i];
        if (c == '\\') { result = result + "\\\\"; }
        elif (c == '"') { result = result + "\\\""; }
        elif (c == '\t') { result = result + "\\t"; }
        else { result = result + c; }
    }
    return result;
}

string projCollectionLiteral(string path) {
    string content = fs.readFile(path);
    string result = "[";
    bool first = true;
    int start = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i <= content.length; i = i + 1) {
        bool atEnd = i == content.length;
        bool atLineEnd = false;
        if (!atEnd && content[i] == '\n') { atLineEnd = true; }
        if (atEnd || atLineEnd) {
            if (!atEnd || start < i) {
                int end = i;
                if (end > start && content[end - 1] == '\r') { end = end - 1; }
                string value = projSlice(content, start, end);
                if (!first) { result = result + ", "; }
                first = false;
                result = result + "\"" + projCollectionEscape(value) + "\"";
            }
            start = i + 1;
        }
    }
    return result + "]";
}

void projCollectionFail(string path, int line) {
    printline("UnknownCollectionException: unknown collection was tried to be collected \"" + path + "\" at line " + line.toString());
    exit(1);
}

string projExpandCollections(string source, string root, string collections) {
    string result = "";
    int cursor = 0;
    int line = 1;
    while (cursor < source.length) {
        int end = cursor;
        while (end < source.length && source[end] != '\n') { end = end + 1; }
        string text = projSlice(source, cursor, end);
        int marker = projFind(text, "collects", 0);
        int comment = projFind(text, "//", 0);
        if (marker >= 0 && (comment < 0 || comment > marker)) {
            string prefix = projTrim(projSlice(text, 0, marker));
            int split = prefix.length - 1;
            while (split >= 0 && (prefix[split] != ' ' && prefix[split] != '\t')) { split = split - 1; }
            if (split < 0 || projTrim(projSlice(prefix, 0, split)) != "string[]") {
                projCollectionFail("invalid declaration", line);
            }
            string path = projTrim(projSlice(text, marker + 8, text.length));
            int semi = projFind(path, ";", 0);
            if (semi < 0) { projCollectionFail("missing .col path", line); }
            path = projTrim(projSlice(path, 0, semi));
            if (collections.length == 0 || path.length == 0 || !projEndsWith(path, ".col") || projFind(path, "..", 0) >= 0) {
                projCollectionFail(path, line);
            }
            if (path[0] == '/' || path[0] == '\\') {
                projCollectionFail(path, line);
            }
            string fullPath = projJoin(root, collections + "\\" + path);
            if (!fs.exists(fullPath)) { projCollectionFail(path, line); }
            result = result + prefix + " = " + projCollectionLiteral(fullPath) + projSlice(path, semi, path.length);
        } else {
            result = result + text;
        }
        if (end < source.length) { result = result + '\n'; }
        cursor = end + 1;
        line = line + 1;
    }
    return result;
}

bool isProjectPath(string path) {
    return projEndsWith(path, ".srp");
}

string loadProjectSource(string projectPath) {
    string manifest = fs.readFile(projectPath);
    string root = projDirname(projectPath);
    string entry = projManifestValue(manifest, "entry");
    string[] sources = projManifestList(manifest, "sources");
    string[] excludes = projManifestList(manifest, "exclude");

    string[] files = [];
    string[] visited = [];
    // Normalize the entry to backslash form so dedup matches walk output:
    // folder entries come back with backslashes while manifests may use
    // forward slashes, and a mismatched separator would include the entry
    // twice when it also lives under a walked source directory.
    string entryPath = "";
    for (int ei = 0; ei < entry.length; ei = ei + 1) {
        if (entry[ei] == '/') { entryPath = entryPath + '\\'; }
        else { entryPath = entryPath + entry[ei]; }
    }
    entryPath = projJoin(root, entryPath);
    if (!projVisited(visited, entryPath)) {
        visited.add(entryPath);
        files.add(entryPath);
    }

    if (sources.length > 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < sources.length; i = i + 1) {
            string[] got = projAddEntry(root, sources[i], excludes);
            for (int k = 0; k < got.length; k = k + 1) {
                string p = got[k];
                if (!projVisited(visited, p)) {
                    visited.add(p);
                    files.add(p);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        string[] got = projWalk(root, "", excludes);
        for (int k = 0; k < got.length; k = k + 1) {
            string p = got[k];
            if (!projVisited(visited, p)) {
                visited.add(p);
                files.add(p);
            }
        }
    }

    string body = "";
    for (int k = 0; k < files.length; k = k + 1) {
        body = body + fs.readFile(files[k]) + "\n";
    }

    // `.qs` libraries listed under `imports` are not auto-imported: declaring
    // one only makes it available. Its module sources are parsed out of the
    // bundle and compiled alongside the project sources, so `import * from X;`
    // statements in the code resolve to the library's symbols.
    string[] imports = projManifestList(manifest, "imports");
    for (int ki = 0; ki < imports.length; ki = ki + 1) {
        string qsPath = projJoin(root, imports[ki]);
        string qsContent = fs.readFile(qsPath);
        if (qsContent.length > 0) {
            string[] libSources = qsModuleSources(qsContent);
            for (int si = 0; si < libSources.length; si = si + 1) {
                body = body + libSources[si] + "\n";
            }
        }
    }

    string collections = projManifestValue(manifest, "collections");
    body = projExpandCollections(body, root, collections);

    // Mirror silverc's projectBuildSource metadata injection so interpreted
    // projects expose the same APP_* constants as compiled ones. DOCS/253:
    // a missing `type` (or the legacy `exe` spelling) means `console`.
    string metadata = "";
    string name = projManifestValue(manifest, "name");
    string version = projManifestValue(manifest, "version");
    string license = projManifestValue(manifest, "license");
    if (name != "") { metadata = metadata + "const string APP_NAME = \"" + name + "\";\n"; }
    if (version != "") { metadata = metadata + "const string APP_VERSION = \"" + version + "\";\n"; }
    if (license != "") { metadata = metadata + "const string APP_LICENSE = \"" + license + "\";\n"; }
    metadata = metadata + "const string LANG_VERSION = \"0.4.0\";\n";
    metadata = metadata + "const string APP_COPYRIGHT = \"Edan M. © 2025\";\n";
    string ptype = projManifestValue(manifest, "type");
    if (ptype == "" || ptype == "exe") { ptype = "console"; }
    metadata = metadata + "const string APP_TYPE = \"" + ptype + "\";\n";
    return metadata + body;
}





// Whole-word string equality for command-line arguments. The self-hosted
// stage-2 backend represents strings as pointers, so `a == b` compares
// pointers rather than contents. This helper walks the characters and is
// correct on both backends (mirrors string.cliArgEq; kept here so the cli
// module is self-contained when callers only need arg parsing).
public bool eq(string a, string b) {
    if (a.length != b.length) { return false; }
    for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (a[i] != b[i]) { return false; }
    }
    return true;
}

// Returns true if `arg` matches any of the listed option spellings.
// Useful for accepting short and long forms together, e.g.
// cli.isFlag(arg, ["-h", "--help", "/?"]).
public bool isFlag(string arg, string[] names) {
    for (int i = 0; i < names.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (eq(arg, names[i])) { return true; }
    }
    return false;
}

// Returns true if `arg` looks like an option (begins with '-' or '/').
// Used to distinguish positional arguments from options while scanning.
public bool isOption(string arg) {
    if (arg.length == 0) { return false; }
    char c = arg[0];
    return c == '-' || c == '/';
}

// Returns true if `arg` begins with `prefix`. Useful for combined short
// options like `-vh`.
public bool hasPrefix(string arg, string prefix) {
    if (arg.length < prefix.length) { return false; }
    for (int i = 0; i < prefix.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (arg[i] != prefix[i]) { return false; }
    }
    return true;
}

// Returns the value following `args[idx]` for a value-taking option, or
// the empty string when no value is present or the next item is itself
// an option. Pair this with the caller's index increment to consume the
// value when it is non-empty.
public string valueAfter(string[] args, int idx) {
    int next = idx + 1;
    if (next >= args.length) { return ""; }
    string nxt = args[next];
    if (isOption(nxt)) { return ""; }
    return nxt;
}

// Returns the array of positional (non-option) arguments from `args`.
// Useful for collecting input/output file operands after a flag scan.
public string[] positional(string[] args) {
    string[] result = [];
    for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (!isOption(args[i])) { result.add(args[i]); }
    }
    return result;
}

public string[] args() << cargs;

public string programName() {
    if (cargs.length > 0) { return cargs[0]; }
    return "";
}







class GuardVar {
    public prop string name;
    public prop string typeHint;
    public prop bool isInit;
    public prop bool isAlive;
    public prop bool isOnce;
    public prop bool isNullable;
    public prop bool isNonNull;
    public prop bool isFixed;
    public prop bool isAlias;
    public prop bool holdsAddress;
    public prop string aliasOf;
    public prop int line;
    public prop int col;

    construct () {
        this.name = "";
        this.typeHint = "";
        this.isInit = false;
        this.isAlive = false;
        this.isOnce = false;
        this.isNullable = false;
        this.isNonNull = false;
        this.isFixed = false;
        this.isAlias = false;
        this.holdsAddress = false;
        this.aliasOf = "";
        this.line = 0;
        this.col = 0;
    }
}

class MemoryGuard {
    private prop GuardVar[] vars;
    private prop int[] scopeEnds;
    private prop string[] constNames;
    private prop int nullSafeDepth;

    construct () {
        this.vars = [];
        this.scopeEnds = [];
        this.constNames = [];
        this.nullSafeDepth = 0;
    }

    private bool eq(string a, string b) {
        if (a.length != b.length) { return false; }
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
            if (a[i] != b[i]) { return false; }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private bool endsWithQ(string s) {
        if (s.length == 0) { return false; }
        return s[s.length - 1] == '?';
    }

    private void fail(string exc, int line, int col, string varName, string msg) {
        printline("GuardError: " + exc + " at line " + line.toString() + ", column " + col.toString() + ": " + varName + ": " + msg);
        exit(1);
    }

    private int findVar(string name) {
        for (int i = this.vars.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (this.eq(this.vars[i].name, name)) { return i; }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    private void pushScope() {
        this.scopeEnds.add(this.vars.length);
    }

    private void popScope() {
        if (this.scopeEnds.length == 0) { return; }
        int start = this.scopeEnds[this.scopeEnds.length - 1];
        int[] newSE = [];
        for (int i = 0; i < this.scopeEnds.length - 1; i++) { newSE.add(this.scopeEnds[i]); }
        this.scopeEnds = newSE;
        for (int d = this.vars.length - 1; d >= start; d--) {
            GuardVar dying = this.vars[d];
            for (int j = 0; j < start; j++) {
                GuardVar outer = this.vars[j];
                if (outer.isAlive && outer.aliasOf.length > 0 && this.eq(outer.aliasOf, dying.name)) {
                    this.fail("UnsetAliasCallException", outer.line, outer.col, outer.name, "alias outlives its target " + dying.name);
                }
            }
        }
        GuardVar[] kept = [];
        for (int i = 0; i < start; i++) { kept.add(this.vars[i]); }
        this.vars = kept;
    }

    private GuardVar cloneVar(GuardVar v) {
        GuardVar c = new GuardVar();
        c.name = v.name;
        c.typeHint = v.typeHint;
        c.isInit = v.isInit;
        c.isAlive = v.isAlive;
        c.isOnce = v.isOnce;
        c.isNullable = v.isNullable;
        c.isNonNull = v.isNonNull;
        c.isFixed = v.isFixed;
        c.isAlias = v.isAlias;
        c.holdsAddress = v.holdsAddress;
        c.aliasOf = v.aliasOf;
        c.line = v.line;
        c.col = v.col;
        return c;
    }

    private GuardVar[] snapshot() {
        GuardVar[] out = [];
        for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) { out.add(this.cloneVar(this.vars[i])); }
        return out;
    }

    private void declareVar(string name, string typeHint, bool init, bool alive, bool onceFlag, bool fixedFlag, bool nullable, bool aliasFlag, int line, int col) {
        GuardVar v = new GuardVar();
        v.name = name;
        v.typeHint = typeHint;
        v.isInit = init;
        v.isAlive = alive;
        v.isOnce = onceFlag;
        v.isFixed = fixedFlag;
        v.isNullable = nullable;
        v.isAlias = aliasFlag;
        v.line = line;
        v.col = col;
        this.vars.add(v);
    }

    private bool targetAlive(string target) {
        int idx = this.findVar(target);
        if (idx < 0) { return false; }
        return this.vars[idx].isAlive;
    }

    private void killAliasesOf(string name) {
        for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) {
            GuardVar a = this.vars[i];
            if (a.aliasOf.length > 0 && this.eq(a.aliasOf, name)) {
                a.isAlive = false;
                a.isInit = false;
            }
        }
    }

    private void markNonNull(string name) {
        int idx = this.findVar(name);
        if (idx >= 0) {
            GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
            v.isNonNull = true;
        }
    }

    private void checkRead(Node n) {
        string name = n.name;
        if (this.eq(name, "this")) { return; }
        int idx = this.findVar(name);
        if (idx < 0) { return; }
        GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
        if (!v.isAlive) {
            this.fail("NonexistenceException", n.line, n.col, name, "variable is dead (moved, unset, or already consumed)");
        }
        if (!v.isInit) {
            this.fail("UnassignedVariableCallException", n.line, n.col, name, "read before it is assigned on all paths");
        }
        if (v.aliasOf.length > 0) {
            if (!this.targetAlive(v.aliasOf)) {
                this.fail("UnsetAliasCallException", n.line, n.col, name, "alias target is dead or out of scope");
            }
        }
        if (v.isOnce) {
            v.isAlive = false;
            v.isInit = false;
            this.killAliasesOf(name);
        }
    }

    private void checkPeek(Node n) {
        string name = n.name;
        if (this.eq(name, "this")) { return; }
        int idx = this.findVar(name);
        if (idx < 0) { return; }
        GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
        if (!v.isAlive) {
            this.fail("NonexistenceException", n.line, n.col, name, "variable is dead (moved, unset, or already consumed)");
        }
        if (!v.isInit) {
            this.fail("UnassignedVariableCallException", n.line, n.col, name, "read before it is assigned on all paths");
        }
    }

    private int checkAddr(Node n) {
        Node op = n.child(0);
        if (op.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            string name = op.name;
            if (!this.eq(name, "this")) {
                int idx = this.findVar(name);
                if (idx >= 0) {
        GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
        if (!v.isAlive) {
                        this.fail("NonexistenceException", op.line, op.col, name, "variable is dead (moved, unset, or already consumed)");
                    }
                    if (!v.isInit) {
                        this.fail("UnassignedVariableCallException", op.line, op.col, name, "read before it is assigned on all paths");
                    }
                    if (!v.isFixed) {
                        this.fail("AddressTypeMistmatchException", op.line, op.col, name, "@ requires a fixed variable");
                    }
                    if (v.isOnce) {
                        v.isAlive = false;
                        v.isInit = false;
                        this.killAliasesOf(name);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            this.checkExpr(op);
        }
        return 1;
    }

    private void killMoveSource(Node src) {
        if (src.kind == ND_IDENT && !this.eq(src.name, "this")) {
            int si = this.findVar(src.name);
            if (si >= 0) {
                GuardVar sv = this.vars[si];
                if (!sv.isAlive) {
                    this.fail("NonexistenceException", src.line, src.col, src.name, "moved-from variable is dead");
                }
                if (!sv.isInit) {
                    this.fail("UnassignedVariableCallException", src.line, src.col, src.name, "cannot move an uninitialized variable");
                }
                sv.isAlive = false;
                sv.isInit = false;
                this.killAliasesOf(src.name);
            }
        } else {
            this.checkExpr(src);
        }
    }

    private void checkMove(Node n) {
        Node dest = n.child(0);
        if (dest.kind == ND_IDENT && !this.eq(dest.name, "this")) {
            int di = this.findVar(dest.name);
            if (di >= 0) {
                this.vars[di].isInit = true;
                this.vars[di].isAlive = true;
            }
        } else if (dest.kind != ND_IDENT) {
            this.checkExpr(dest);
        }
        this.killMoveSource(n.child(1));
    }

    private void checkPunning(GuardVar v, Node init, Node lhs) {
        if (init.kind == ND_ADDRESS_OF) {
            Node op = init.child(0);
            if (op.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                int oi = this.findVar(op.name);
                if (oi >= 0) {
                    GuardVar ov = this.vars[oi];
                    if (v.typeHint.length > 0 && ov.typeHint.length > 0 && !this.eq(v.typeHint, ov.typeHint)) {
                        this.fail("AddressTypeMistmatchException", lhs.line, lhs.col, v.name, "type punning of an address (@" + op.name + " is " + ov.typeHint + ", target is " + v.typeHint + ")");
                    }
                    v.holdsAddress = true;
                }
            }
        } else if (init.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            int oi = this.findVar(init.name);
            if (oi >= 0 && this.vars[oi].holdsAddress) {
                GuardVar ov = this.vars[oi];
                if (v.typeHint.length > 0 && ov.typeHint.length > 0 && !this.eq(v.typeHint, ov.typeHint)) {
                    this.fail("AddressTypeMistmatchException", lhs.line, lhs.col, v.name, "type punning of an address (" + init.name + " is " + ov.typeHint + ", target is " + v.typeHint + ")");
                }
                v.holdsAddress = true;
            }
        }
    }

    private void checkNullableAssign(GuardVar v, Node init, Node lhs) {
        if (init.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            int ri = this.findVar(init.name);
            if (ri >= 0 && this.vars[ri].isNullable) {
                if (v.typeHint.length > 0 && !this.endsWithQ(v.typeHint)) {
                    this.fail("NullValueException", lhs.line, lhs.col, v.name, "nullable value " + init.name + " used without a null check (use ??)");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private int checkAssign(Node n) {
        Node lhs = n.child(0);
        Node rhs = n.child(1);
        bool lhsNullable = false;
        if (lhs.kind == ND_IDENT && !this.eq(lhs.name, "this")) {
            if (this.isConstName(lhs.name)) {
                this.fail("InvalidAssignment", lhs.line, lhs.col, lhs.name, "cannot write to a const");
            }
            int idx = this.findVar(lhs.name);
            if (idx >= 0) {
                GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
                lhsNullable = v.isNullable;
                if (v.isAlias && rhs.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                    v.aliasOf = rhs.name;
                    if (!this.targetAlive(rhs.name)) {
                        this.fail("UnsetAliasCallException", lhs.line, lhs.col, v.name, "alias target is dead or out of scope");
                    }
                }
                v.isInit = true;
                v.isAlive = true;
                this.checkNullableAssign(v, rhs, lhs);
                this.checkPunning(v, rhs, lhs);
            }
        } else if (lhs.kind == ND_MEMBER) {
            this.checkExpr(lhs.child(0));
        } else if (lhs.kind == ND_DICT_LIT) {
            this.checkExpr(lhs.child(0));
            this.checkExpr(lhs.child(1));
        } else {
            this.checkExpr(lhs);
        }
        if (lhsNullable) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth + 1; }
        int result = this.checkExpr(rhs);
        if (lhsNullable) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth - 1; }
        return result;
    }

    private bool isConstName(string name) {
        for (int i = 0; i < this.constNames.length; i++) {
            if (this.eq(this.constNames[i], name)) { return true; }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void checkInitCompat(Node node, Node init) {
        string tname = node.name;
        if (init.kind == ND_IDENT) {
            int ri = this.findVar(init.name);
            if (ri >= 0 && this.vars[ri].isNullable) {
                if (node.typeHint.length > 0 && !this.endsWithQ(node.typeHint)) {
                    this.fail("NullValueException", init.line, init.col, tname, "nullable value " + init.name + " used without a null check (use ??)");
                }
            }
        }
        if (init.kind == ND_ADDRESS_OF) {
            Node op = init.child(0);
            if (op.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                int oi = this.findVar(op.name);
                if (oi >= 0) {
                    GuardVar ov = this.vars[oi];
                    if (node.typeHint.length > 0 && ov.typeHint.length > 0 && !this.eq(node.typeHint, ov.typeHint)) {
                        this.fail("AddressTypeMistmatchException", init.line, init.col, tname, "type punning of an address (@" + op.name + " is " + ov.typeHint + ", target is " + node.typeHint + ")");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private bool isPrimitive(string t) {
        if (this.eq(t, "int") || this.eq(t, "string") || this.eq(t, "bool") || this.eq(t, "char")) { return true; }
        if (this.eq(t, "float") || this.eq(t, "float64") || this.eq(t, "int32") || this.eq(t, "int64")) { return true; }
        if (this.eq(t, "uint") || this.eq(t, "double") || this.eq(t, "void") || this.eq(t, "var")) { return true; }
        return false;
    }

    private void checkVarDecl(Node n) {
        string name = n.name;
        bool nullable = this.endsWithQ(n.typeHint);
        bool onceFlag = n.isOnce;
        bool fixedFlag = n.isFixed;
        if (n.childCount() == 0) {
            bool autoInit = !this.isPrimitive(n.typeHint);
            this.declareVar(name, n.typeHint, autoInit, true, onceFlag, fixedFlag, nullable, false, n.line, n.col);
            return;
        }
        Node init = n.child(0);
        if (init.kind == ND_BINARY && init.op == OP_MOVE) {
            this.killMoveSource(init.child(1));
            this.declareVar(name, n.typeHint, true, true, onceFlag, fixedFlag, nullable, false, n.line, n.col);
            return;
        }
        this.checkInitCompat(n, init);
        if (nullable) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth + 1; }
        this.checkExpr(init);
        if (nullable) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth - 1; }
        this.declareVar(name, n.typeHint, true, true, onceFlag, fixedFlag, nullable, false, n.line, n.col);
    }

    private void checkAlias(Node n) {
        string aname = n.name;
        bool nullable = this.endsWithQ(n.typeHint);
        if (n.childCount() >= 1) {
            Node tgt = n.child(0);
            if (tgt.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                this.checkPeek(tgt);
                GuardVar v = new GuardVar();
                v.name = aname;
                v.typeHint = n.typeHint;
                v.isInit = true;
                v.isAlive = true;
                v.isNullable = nullable;
                v.isAlias = true;
                v.aliasOf = tgt.name;
                v.line = n.line;
                v.col = n.col;
                this.vars.add(v);
            } else {
                this.checkExpr(tgt);
                this.declareVar(aname, n.typeHint, true, true, false, false, nullable, true, n.line, n.col);
            }
        } else {
            this.declareVar(aname, n.typeHint, false, true, false, false, nullable, true, n.line, n.col);
        }
    }

    private void checkUnset(Node n) {
        int idx = this.findVar(n.name);
        if (idx >= 0) {
            GuardVar v = this.vars[idx];
            v.isAlive = false;
            v.isInit = false;
            this.vars[idx] = v;
            this.killAliasesOf(n.name);
        }
    }

    private void checkBlock(Node n) {
        this.pushScope();
        for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) {
            this.checkStmt(n.child(i));
        }
        this.popScope();
    }

    private void checkIf(Node n) {
        this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
        string thenNonNull = "";
        string elseNonNull = "";
        Node cond = n.child(0);
        if (cond.kind == ND_BINARY) {
            if (cond.op == OP_NEQ) {
                if (cond.child(0).kind == ND_IDENT && cond.child(1).kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { thenNonNull = cond.child(0).name; }
                if (cond.child(1).kind == ND_IDENT && cond.child(0).kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { thenNonNull = cond.child(1).name; }
            } elif (cond.op == OP_EQ) {
                if (cond.child(0).kind == ND_IDENT && cond.child(1).kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { elseNonNull = cond.child(0).name; }
                if (cond.child(1).kind == ND_IDENT && cond.child(0).kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { elseNonNull = cond.child(1).name; }
            }
        }
        GuardVar[] before = this.snapshot();
        this.pushScope();
        if (thenNonNull.length > 0) { this.markNonNull(thenNonNull); }
        this.checkStmt(n.child(1));
        this.popScope();
        GuardVar[] afterThen = this.snapshot();
        this.vars = before;
        GuardVar[] afterElse = [];
        if (n.childCount() >= 3) {
            this.pushScope();
            if (elseNonNull.length > 0) { this.markNonNull(elseNonNull); }
            this.checkStmt(n.child(2));
            this.popScope();
            afterElse = this.snapshot();
        } else {
            afterElse = this.snapshot();
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) {
            GuardVar v = this.vars[i];
            v.isInit = afterThen[i].isInit && afterElse[i].isInit;
            v.isAlive = afterThen[i].isAlive || afterElse[i].isAlive;
            v.holdsAddress = afterThen[i].holdsAddress || afterElse[i].holdsAddress;
        }
    }

    private void checkWhile(Node n) {
        this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
        GuardVar[] before = this.snapshot();
        this.pushScope();
        this.checkStmt(n.child(1));
        this.popScope();
        this.vars = before;
    }

    private void checkFor(Node n) {
        this.pushScope();
        this.checkStmt(n.child(0));
        GuardVar[] before = this.snapshot();
        this.checkExpr(n.child(1));
        this.pushScope();
        this.checkStmt(n.child(3));
        this.popScope();
        this.vars = before;
        this.checkExpr(n.child(2));
        this.popScope();
    }

    private void checkForeach(Node n) {
        this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
        this.pushScope();
        string vn = n.name;
        this.declareVar(vn, n.typeHint, true, true, false, false, this.endsWithQ(n.typeHint), false, n.line, n.col);
        this.checkStmt(n.child(1));
        this.popScope();
    }

    private void checkTryCatch(Node n) {
        this.pushScope();
        this.checkStmt(n.child(0));
        this.popScope();
        GuardVar[] afterTry = this.snapshot();
        this.vars = afterTry;
        this.pushScope();
        string cv = n.name;
        if (cv.length > 0) {
            this.declareVar(cv, n.typeHint, true, true, false, false, this.endsWithQ(n.typeHint), false, n.line, n.col);
        }
        this.checkStmt(n.child(1));
        this.popScope();
        GuardVar[] afterCatch = this.snapshot();
        for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) {
            GuardVar v = this.vars[i];
            v.isInit = afterTry[i].isInit && afterCatch[i].isInit;
            v.isAlive = afterTry[i].isAlive || afterCatch[i].isAlive;
            v.holdsAddress = afterTry[i].holdsAddress || afterCatch[i].holdsAddress;
        }
        if (n.hasFinally && n.childCount() >= 3) {
            this.pushScope();
            this.checkStmt(n.child(2));
            this.popScope();
        }
    }

    private void checkMatchBody(Node body) {
        if (body.kind == ND_BLOCK) {
            this.checkStmt(body);
        } else {
            this.checkExpr(body);
        }
    }

    private void checkMatchStmt(Node n) {
        this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
        GuardVar[] before = this.snapshot();
        GuardVar[] merged = this.snapshot();
        bool hasDefault = false;
        for (int i = 1; i < n.childCount(); i++) {
            Node arm = n.child(i);
            if (arm.childCount() < 2) { continue; }
            Node pat = arm.child(0);
            if (pat.kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { hasDefault = true; }
            this.pushScope();
            this.checkMatchBody(arm.child(1));
            this.popScope();
            GuardVar[] after = this.snapshot();
            for (int j = 0; j < this.vars.length; j++) {
                merged[j].isInit = merged[j].isInit && after[j].isInit;
                merged[j].isAlive = merged[j].isAlive || after[j].isAlive;
                merged[j].holdsAddress = merged[j].holdsAddress || after[j].holdsAddress;
            }
            this.vars = merged;
        }
        if (!hasDefault) {
            for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) {
                merged[i].isInit = merged[i].isInit && before[i].isInit;
                merged[i].isAlive = merged[i].isAlive || before[i].isAlive;
                merged[i].holdsAddress = merged[i].holdsAddress || before[i].holdsAddress;
            }
        }
        this.vars = merged;
    }

    private void checkSwitch(Node n) {
        this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
        GuardVar[] before = this.snapshot();
        GuardVar[] merged = this.snapshot();
        bool hasDefault = false;
        for (int i = 1; i < n.childCount(); i++) {
            Node arm = n.child(i);
            if (arm.childCount() < 2) { continue; }
            if (arm.child(0).kind == ND_NULL_LIT) { hasDefault = true; }
            this.pushScope();
            this.checkMatchBody(arm.child(1));
            this.popScope();
            GuardVar[] after = this.snapshot();
            for (int j = 0; j < this.vars.length; j++) {
                merged[j].isInit = merged[j].isInit && after[j].isInit;
                merged[j].isAlive = merged[j].isAlive || after[j].isAlive;
                merged[j].holdsAddress = merged[j].holdsAddress || after[j].holdsAddress;
            }
            this.vars = merged;
        }
        if (!hasDefault) {
            for (int i = 0; i < this.vars.length; i++) {
                merged[i].isInit = merged[i].isInit && before[i].isInit;
                merged[i].isAlive = merged[i].isAlive || before[i].isAlive;
                merged[i].holdsAddress = merged[i].holdsAddress || before[i].holdsAddress;
            }
        }
        this.vars = merged;
    }

    private bool isArithOp(int op) {
        if (op == OP_ADD || op == OP_SUB || op == OP_MUL || op == OP_DIV || op == OP_MOD) { return true; }
        if (op == OP_ADD_ASSIGN || op == OP_SUB_ASSIGN || op == OP_MUL_ASSIGN || op == OP_DIV_ASSIGN) { return true; }
        if (op == OP_SHL || op == OP_SHR || op == OP_BIT_AND || op == OP_BIT_OR || op == OP_BIT_XOR) { return true; }
        return false;
    }

    private int checkExpr(Node n) {
        int k = n.kind;
        if (k == ND_INT_LIT || k == ND_FLOAT_LIT || k == ND_STRING_LIT || k == ND_CHAR_LIT || k == ND_BOOL_LIT || k == ND_NULL_LIT) { return 0; }
        if (k == ND_IDENT) {
            this.checkRead(n);
            int idx = this.findVar(n.name);
            if (idx >= 0 && this.vars[idx].holdsAddress) { return 1; }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_BINARY) {
            int op = n.op;
            if (op == OP_ASSIGN) { return this.checkAssign(n); }
            if (op == OP_MOVE) { this.checkMove(n); return 0; }
            if (op == OP_COALESCE) {
                this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth + 1;
                this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
                this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth - 1;
                this.checkExpr(n.child(1));
                return 0;
            }
            if (op == OP_EQ || op == OP_NEQ) {
                Node nl = n.child(0);
                Node nr = n.child(1);
                bool hasNull = (nl.kind == ND_NULL_LIT && nr.kind != ND_NULL_LIT) || (nr.kind == ND_NULL_LIT && nl.kind != ND_NULL_LIT);
                if (hasNull) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth + 1; }
                this.checkExpr(nl);
                this.checkExpr(nr);
                if (hasNull) { this.nullSafeDepth = this.nullSafeDepth - 1; }
                return 0;
            }
            int la = this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
            int ra = this.checkExpr(n.child(1));
            if (this.isArithOp(op)) {
                if (la == 1 || ra == 1) {
                    Node bad;
                    if (la == 1) { bad = n.child(0); } else { bad = n.child(1); }
                    string bname = "";
                    int bline = n.line;
                    int bcol = n.col;
                    if (bad.kind == ND_ADDRESS_OF) {
                        Node u = bad.child(0);
                        if (u.kind == ND_IDENT) { bname = u.name; bline = u.line; bcol = u.col; }
                    } else if (bad.kind == ND_IDENT) {
                        bname = bad.name;
                        bline = bad.line;
                        bcol = bad.col;
                    }
                    this.fail("AddressTypeMistmatchException", bline, bcol, bname, "address arithmetic is not allowed");
                }
            }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_UNARY) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); return 0; }
        if (k == ND_CALL) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_METHOD_CALL) {
            this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
            for (int i = 1; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_MEMBER) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); return 0; }
        if (k == ND_INDEX || k == ND_SLICE) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_PAREN) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); return 0; }
        if (k == ND_STRUCT_LIT) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) {
                Node f = n.child(i);
                if (f.childCount() > 0) { this.checkExpr(f.child(0)); }
            }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_ARRAY_LIT || k == ND_TUPLE_LIT || k == ND_DICT_LIT || k == ND_NEW) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_INTERP) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_THROW) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_ADDRESS_OF) { return this.checkAddr(n); }
        if (k == ND_MATCH) {
            this.checkExpr(n.child(0));
            for (int i = 1; i < n.childCount(); i++) {
                Node arm = n.child(i);
                if (arm.childCount() >= 2) { this.checkMatchBody(arm.child(1)); }
            }
            return 0;
        }
        if (k == ND_TERNARY || k == ND_IF_EXPR) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return 0;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private void checkStmt(Node n) {
        int k = n.kind;
        if (k == ND_BLOCK) { this.checkBlock(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_VAR_DECL) { this.checkVarDecl(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_ALIAS_DECL) { this.checkAlias(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_LIVE_DECL) { this.checkVarDecl(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_ONCE_DECL) { this.checkVarDecl(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_RETURN || k == ND_RETURN_SHORT) {
            if (n.childCount() > 0) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); }
            return;
        }
        if (k == ND_IF_STMT) { this.checkIf(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_WHILE) { this.checkWhile(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_FOR_C) { this.checkFor(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_FOREACH) { this.checkForeach(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_MATCH_STMT) { this.checkMatchStmt(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_TRY_CATCH) { this.checkTryCatch(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_UNSET) { this.checkUnset(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_EXPR_STMT) {
            if (n.childCount() > 0) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); }
            return;
        }
        if (k == ND_THROW) {
            for (int i = 0; i < n.childCount(); i++) { this.checkExpr(n.child(i)); }
            return;
        }
        if (k == ND_SWITCH) { this.checkSwitch(n); return; }
        if (k == ND_BREAK || k == ND_CONTINUE) { return; }
    }

    private void checkFunction(Node n) {
        this.vars = [];
        this.scopeEnds = [];
        Node params = n.child(0);
        for (int i = 0; i < params.childCount(); i++) {
            Node p = params.child(i);
            this.declareVar(p.name, p.typeHint, true, true, false, false, this.endsWithQ(p.typeHint), false, p.line, p.col);
        }
        if (n.childCount() >= 2) {
            Node body = n.child(1);
            if (body.kind == ND_BLOCK) {
                this.checkBlock(body);
            } else {
                this.checkExpr(body);
            }
        }
    }

    private void checkConst(Node n) {
        if (n.childCount() == 0) {
            this.fail("UnassignedConstDeclarationException", n.line, n.col, n.name, "const must be declared with a value");
        }
    }

    private void checkTop(Node n) {
        if (n.kind == ND_FUNC) {
            this.checkFunction(n);
        } elif (n.kind == ND_CONST) {
            this.checkConst(n);
        } elif (n.kind == ND_CLASS_DEF) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n.childCount(); j++) {
                Node m = n.child(j);
                if (m.kind == ND_FUNC) { this.checkFunction(m); }
                elif (m.kind == ND_CONST) { this.checkConst(m); }
            }
        } elif (n.kind == ND_MODULE) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n.childCount(); j++) {
                this.checkTop(n.child(j));
            }
        } elif (n.kind == ND_VAR_DECL) {
            this.checkVarDecl(n);
        } elif (n.kind == ND_ALIAS_DECL) {
            this.checkAlias(n);
        } elif (n.kind == ND_EXPR_STMT) {
            if (n.childCount() > 0) { this.checkExpr(n.child(0)); }
        } elif (n.kind == ND_RETURN || n.kind == ND_RETURN_SHORT || n.kind == ND_IF_STMT || n.kind == ND_WHILE
            || n.kind == ND_FOR_C || n.kind == ND_FOREACH || n.kind == ND_MATCH_STMT || n.kind == ND_TRY_CATCH
            || n.kind == ND_UNSET || n.kind == ND_SWITCH || n.kind == ND_THROW) {
            this.checkStmt(n);
        }
    }

    public void checkProgram(Node[] program) {
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TG0_guard_start.txt", [49]);
        for (int i = 0; i < program.length; i++) {
            Node n = program[i];
            if (n.kind == ND_CONST) {
                this.constNames.add(n.name);
            } elif (n.kind == ND_CLASS_DEF) {
                for (int j = 0; j < n.childCount(); j++) {
                    Node m = n.child(j);
                    if (m.kind == ND_CONST) { this.constNames.add(m.name); }
                }
            } elif (n.kind == ND_MODULE) {
                for (int j = 0; j < n.childCount(); j++) {
                    Node m = n.child(j);
                    if (m.kind == ND_CONST) { this.constNames.add(m.name); }
                }
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < program.length; i++) {
            Node n = program[i];
            this.checkTop(n);
        }
        file.writeBytes("build/diag/TG1_guard_end.txt", [49]);
    }
}



















// Native modules the interpreter provides without needing a source file.
bool nativeModule(string name) {
    string n = name.lower();
    return cli.eq(n, "fs") || cli.eq(n, "json") || cli.eq(n, "yaml")
        || cli.eq(n, "env") || cli.eq(n, "url") || cli.eq(n, "hash")
        || cli.eq(n, "path") || cli.eq(n, "bom") || cli.eq(n, "console")
        || cli.eq(n, "math") || cli.eq(n, "llmai") || cli.eq(n, "os");
}

// Case-insensitive substring search.
int findCI(string text, string needle, int start) {
    string t = text.lower();
    string n = needle.lower();
    return t.indexOf(n, start);
}

// Read std/<module>.sr from the standard checkout locations, returning empty
// string when the file is not found at any candidate path.
string readStdModule(string name, string inputFile) {
    string lower = name.lower();
    string[] paths = [];
    // Resolve user modules beside the script before probing the standard
    // library.  `silveri tests/main.sr` must be able to load tests/helper.sr,
    // not just std/helper.sr.
    int lastSep = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < inputFile.length; i = i + 1) {
        if (inputFile[i] == '\\' || inputFile[i] == '/') { lastSep = i; }
    }
    if (lastSep >= 0) {
        paths.add(inputFile.substr(0, lastSep + 1) + lower + ".sr");
    }
    paths.add(lower + ".sr");
    paths.add("std\\" + lower + ".sr");
    paths.add("silverc\\std\\" + lower + ".sr");
    paths.add("..\\std\\" + lower + ".sr");
    paths.add("..\\silverc\\std\\" + lower + ".sr");
    for (int i = 0; i < paths.length; i = i + 1) {
        string src = "";
        try {
            src = fs.readFile(paths[i]);
        } catch (int e) {
            src = "";
        }
        if (src.length > 0) { return src; }
    }
    return "";
}

// Resolve `import * from <module>` statements in `source` by prepending
// std/<module>.sr content for non-native modules. Native modules are
// handled directly by the interpreter and must not be loaded from disk.
string resolveImports(string source, string inputFile) {
    string prepend = "";
    int cursor = 0;
    while (cursor < source.length) {
        int importPos = findCI(source, "import * from ", cursor);
        if (importPos < 0) { break; }
        int semi = source.indexOf(";", importPos);
        if (semi < 0) { break; }
        int nameStart = importPos + 13;
        while (nameStart < semi && source[nameStart] == ' ') { nameStart = nameStart + 1; }
        int nameEnd = nameStart;
        while (nameEnd < semi && ((source[nameEnd] >= 'a' && source[nameEnd] <= 'z') || (source[nameEnd] >= 'A' && source[nameEnd] <= 'Z') || (source[nameEnd] >= '0' && source[nameEnd] <= '9') || source[nameEnd] == '_')) {
            nameEnd = nameEnd + 1;
        }
        string module = source.substr(nameStart, nameEnd - nameStart);
        if (module.length > 0 && !nativeModule(module)) {
            string modSrc = readStdModule(module, inputFile);
            if (modSrc.length > 0) {
                prepend = prepend + modSrc + "\n";
            }
        }
        cursor = semi + 1;
    }
    if (prepend.length > 0) {
        return prepend + source;
    }
    return source;
}

void printHelp() {
    printline("silveri " + APP_VERSION);
    printline("Copyright: © 2025 Edan M.");
    printline("License: " + APP_LICENSE);
    printline("Silver language version: " + LANG_VERSION);
    printline("");
    printline("Usage: silveri [options] <filename>");
    printline("");
    printline("Options:");
    printline("  -i <file>      Input Silver source file");
    printline("  -o <file>      Output file (ignored in interpreter)");
    printline("  --help, -h     Show this help message");
    printline("  --version      Show version information");
    printline("");
    printline("Examples:");
    printline("  silveri script.sr              Run a Silver script");
    printline("  silveri -i script.sr           Run a Silver script");
}

void main() {
    // Diagnostic crumbs go into a subfolder so they never pollute the
    // working directory (e.g. when running tests/ scripts).
    // Option parsing goes through the `cli` module (DOCS/252) so the same
    // spelling rules apply as silverc and the self-hosted compiler.
    string[] helpNames = ["-h", "--help"];
    string[] versionNames = ["--version"];

    string[] args = cargs;

    if (args.length < 2) {
        printHelp();
        exit(1);
    }

    string filename = "";

    for (int i = 1; i < args.length; i += 1) {
        string arg = args[i];
        if (cli.isFlag(arg, helpNames)) {
            printHelp();
            return;
        }
        if (cli.isFlag(arg, versionNames)) {
            printline("silveri " + APP_VERSION);
            printline("Silver " + LANG_VERSION);
            printline("Copyright: © 2025 Edan M.");
            printline("License: " + APP_LICENSE);
            return;
        }
        if (cli.eq(arg, "-i")) {
            string v = cli.valueAfter(args, i);
            if (v != "") {
                filename = v;
                i += 1;
            } else {
                printline("Error: -i requires a filename");
                exit(1);
            }
        } elif (cli.eq(arg, "-o")) {
            // -o is accepted for command compatibility but ignored because
            // silveri executes source directly instead of producing a
            // compiled output file.
            string v = cli.valueAfter(args, i);
            if (v != "") {
                i += 1;
            } else {
                printline("Error: -o requires a filename");
                exit(1);
            }
        } elif (cli.isOption(arg)) {
            printline("Error: Unknown option '" + arg + "'");
            printHelp();
            exit(1);
        } elif (filename == "") {
            filename = arg;
        } else {
            printline("Error: Unknown option '" + arg + "'");
            printHelp();
            exit(1);
        }
    }

    if (filename == "") {
        printline("Error: No input file specified");
        printline("Usage: silveri [options] <filename>");
        exit(1);
    }


    string inputSource = "";
    if (isProjectPath(filename)) {
        inputSource = loadProjectSource(filename);
    } else {
        // `.col` declarations require a project manifest. Reuse the project
        // loader's validation so standalone scripts report
        // UnknownCollectionException instead of a generic parser error.
        inputSource = projExpandCollections(fs.readFile(filename), ".", "");
    }

    // Resolve `import * from <module>` statements by prepending the module
    // source (like the compiler driver does) so its classes, constants and
    // functions are registered before the user source runs.
    inputSource = resolveImports(inputSource, filename);

    Lexer lexer = new Lexer(inputSource);
    Token[] tokens = lexer.tokenize();

    Parser p = new Parser(tokens);
    Node[] program = p.parseProgram();

    Evaluator evaluator = new Evaluator();
    string preludeSrc = source();
    Lexer preludeLexer = new Lexer(preludeSrc);
    Token[] preludeTokens = preludeLexer.tokenize();
    Parser preludeParser = new Parser(preludeTokens);
    Node[] llmaiPrelude = preludeParser.parseProgram();
    evaluator.register(llmaiPrelude);

    string excSrc = exceptionSource();
    Lexer excLexer = new Lexer(excSrc);
    Token[] excTokens = excLexer.tokenize();
    Parser excParser = new Parser(excTokens);
    Node[] excPrelude = excParser.parseProgram();
    evaluator.register(excPrelude);

    MemoryGuard guard = new MemoryGuard();
    guard.checkProgram(program);

    evaluator.run(program);
}
